# Daniel

דניאל

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel)

יועץ יהודי למלכי בבל הפותר חלומות ונמלט מסכנות בדרך נס.

A Jewish advisor to Babylonian kings interprets dreams and miraculously escapes danger.

Sections: Chapter-Verse

Location: Tanakh/Writings

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Miqra according to the Masorah (Segments: 357, License: CC-BY-SA)

Versions (En):
  - The Koren Jerusalem Bible (Segments: 357, License: CC-BY-NC)
  - The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein (Segments: 357, License: CC-BY)


## Chapter 1

###### Daniel 1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:1)


בִּשְׁנַ֣ת שָׁל֔וֹשׁ לְמַלְכ֖וּת יְהוֹיָקִ֣ים מֶֽלֶךְ־יְהוּדָ֑ה בָּ֣א נְבוּכַדְנֶאצַּ֧ר מֶֽלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֛ל יְרוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם וַיָּ֥צַר עָלֶֽיהָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In the third year of the reign of Yehoyaqim king of Yehuda, Nevukhadneżżar king of Bavel came to Yerushalayim, and besieged it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the third year of [the rebellion of] the kingdom of Judah [(against Nevuchadnezzar), viz. II Kings 24:1], Nevuchadnezzar king of Bavel came to Jerusalem, and he besieged it.


###### Daniel 1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:2)


וַיִּתֵּן֩ אֲדֹנָ֨י בְּיָד֜וֹ אֶת־יְהוֹיָקִ֣ים מֶֽלֶךְ־יְהוּדָ֗ה וּמִקְצָת֙ כְּלֵ֣י בֵית־הָֽאֱלֹהִ֔ים וַיְבִיאֵ֥ם אֶֽרֶץ־שִׁנְעָ֖ר בֵּ֣ית אֱלֹהָ֑יו וְאֶת־הַכֵּלִ֣ים הֵבִ֔יא בֵּ֖ית אוֹצַ֥ר אֱלֹהָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord gave Yehoyaqim king of Yehuda into his hand, with part of the vessels of the house of God: which he carried into the land of Shin῾ar to the house of his god; and he brought the vessels into the treasure house of his god.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd delivered into his hand Yehoyakim king of Judah and some of the vessels of the house of G-d, and he brought them [(the captives and the vessels)] to the land of Shinar to the house of his god [(in celebration of his victory)], and the vessels he [afterwards] brought to the treasure house of his god.


###### Daniel 1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:3)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ לְאַשְׁפְּנַ֖ז רַ֣ב סָרִיסָ֑יו לְהָבִ֞יא מִבְּנֵ֧י יִשְׂרָאֵ֛ל וּמִזֶּ֥רַע הַמְּלוּכָ֖ה וּמִן־הַֽפַּרְתְּמִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the king spoke to Ashpenaz the master of his eunuchs, that he should bring some of the children of Yisra᾽el and of the king’s seed, and of the nobles;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king told Ashpenaz, the chief of his eunuchs, to bring forth from the children of Israel and from the seed of the kingdom and from the nobles,


###### Daniel 1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:4)


יְלָדִ֣ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר אֵֽין־בָּהֶ֣ם כׇּל־מאֿוּם֩ וְטוֹבֵ֨י מַרְאֶ֜ה וּמַשְׂכִּלִ֣ים בְּכׇל־חׇכְמָ֗ה וְיֹ֤דְעֵי דַ֙עַת֙ וּמְבִינֵ֣י מַדָּ֔ע וַאֲשֶׁר֙ כֹּ֣חַ בָּהֶ֔ם לַעֲמֹ֖ד בְּהֵיכַ֣ל הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וּֽלְלַמְּדָ֥ם סֵ֖פֶר וּלְשׁ֥וֹן כַּשְׂדִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
youths in whom was no blemish, but well favoured, and skilful in all wisdom, and discerning in knowledge, and perceptive in understanding, and such as had ability in them to stand in the king’s palace, and whom they might teach the learning and the tongue of the Kasdim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
boys without any blemish, and of goodly appearance, and acquainted with all wisdom, and possessing understanding and insight, and having the strength in them to attend [deferentially] in the palace of the king and to be taught the script and the speech of the Kasdim.


###### Daniel 1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:5)


וַיְמַן֩ לָהֶ֨ם הַמֶּ֜לֶךְ דְּבַר־י֣וֹם בְּיוֹמ֗וֹ מִפַּת־בַּ֤ג הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ וּמִיֵּ֣ין מִשְׁתָּ֔יו וּֽלְגַדְּלָ֖ם שָׁנִ֣ים שָׁל֑וֹשׁ וּמִ֨קְצָתָ֔ם יַֽעַמְד֖וּ לִפְנֵ֥י הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the king appointed them a daily provision of the king’s food and of the wine which he drank; that they should be thus reared for three years, and at the end of that time, they might stand before the king.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king provided for them daily from the pathbag [(the food)] of the king, and from the wine that he drank, to be reared [on this regimen] for three years, [after which] some would [be selected to] pay attendance upon the king.


###### Daniel 1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:6)


וַיְהִ֥י בָהֶ֖ם מִבְּנֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֑ה דָּנִיֵּ֣אל חֲנַנְיָ֔ה מִֽישָׁאֵ֖ל וַעֲזַרְיָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now among these were of the children of Yehuda: Daniyyel, Ḥananya, Misha᾽el, and ῾Azarya:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And there were of them of the sons of Judah — Daniel, Chananiah, Mishael, and Azaryah.


###### Daniel 1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:7)


וַיָּ֧שֶׂם לָהֶ֛ם שַׂ֥ר הַסָּרִיסִ֖ים שֵׁמ֑וֹת וַיָּ֨שֶׂם לְדָֽנִיֵּ֜אל בֵּ֣לְטְשַׁאצַּ֗ר וְלַֽחֲנַנְיָה֙ שַׁדְרַ֔ךְ וּלְמִֽישָׁאֵ֣ל מֵישַׁ֔ךְ וְלַעֲזַרְיָ֖ה עֲבֵ֥ד נְגֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
to whom the chief of the eunuchs gave names: for he gave to Daniyyel the name of Belteshażżar; and to Ḥananya, of Shadrakh; and to Misha᾽el, of Meshakh; and to ῾Azarya, of ῾Aved-nego.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the chief of the eunuchs gave them names. He called Daniel "Belshazzar"; Chananiah, "Shadrach"; Mishael, "Meshach," and Azaryah, "Aved-nego."


###### Daniel 1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:8)


וַיָּ֤שֶׂם דָּנִיֵּאל֙ עַל־לִבּ֔וֹ אֲשֶׁ֧ר לֹֽא־יִתְגָּאַ֛ל בְּפַת־בַּ֥ג הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ וּבְיֵ֣ין מִשְׁתָּ֑יו וַיְבַקֵּשׁ֙ מִשַּׂ֣ר הַסָּרִיסִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֖ר לֹ֥א יִתְגָּאָֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But Daniyyel purposed in his heart that he would not defile himself with the portion of the king’s food, nor with the wine which he drank: therefore he requested of the chief of the eunuchs that he might not defile himself.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Daniel resolved that he would not sully himself with the pathbag of the king and the wine that he drank. And he besought the chief of the eunuchs that he not [be compelled to] sully himself.


###### Daniel 1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:9)


וַיִּתֵּ֤ן הָֽאֱלֹהִים֙ אֶת־דָּ֣נִיֵּ֔אל לְחֶ֖סֶד וּֽלְרַחֲמִ֑ים לִפְנֵ֖י שַׂ֥ר הַסָּרִיסִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now God had brought Daniyyel into grace and compassion with the chief of the eunuchs.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And G-d rendered Daniel the object of lovingkindness and mercy before the chief of the eunuchs.


###### Daniel 1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:10)


וַיֹּ֜אמֶר שַׂ֤ר הַסָּרִיסִים֙ לְדָ֣נִיֵּ֔אל יָרֵ֤א אֲנִי֙ אֶת־אֲדֹנִ֣י הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ אֲשֶׁ֣ר מִנָּ֔ה אֶת־מַאֲכַלְכֶ֖ם וְאֶת־מִשְׁתֵּיכֶ֑ם אֲשֶׁ֡ר לָ֩מָּה֩ יִרְאֶ֨ה אֶת־פְּנֵיכֶ֜ם זֹֽעֲפִ֗ים מִן־הַיְלָדִים֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר כְּגִֽילְכֶ֔ם וְחִיַּבְתֶּ֥ם אֶת־רֹאשִׁ֖י לַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the chief of the eunuchs said to Daniyyel, I fear my lord the king, who has appointed your food and your drink: for why should he see you looking more woebegone than other youths of your age? for then you should endanger my head to the king.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the chief of the eunuchs said to Daniel: I fear my master, the king, who has provided your food and your drink. Why should he see your faces more haggard than (those of) the (other) boys of your age, whereby you would render my head [i.e., my life] liable to the king.


###### Daniel 1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:11)


וַיֹּ֥אמֶר דָּנִיֵּ֖אל אֶל־הַמֶּלְצַ֑ר אֲשֶׁ֤ר מִנָּה֙ שַׂ֣ר הַסָּֽרִיסִ֔ים עַל־דָּנִיֵּ֣אל חֲנַנְיָ֔ה מִֽישָׁאֵ֖ל וַעֲזַרְיָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Daniyyel said to the steward whom the chief of the eunuchs has set over Daniyyel, Ḥananya, Misha᾽el, and ῾Azarya,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Daniel said to the steward whom the chief of the eunuchs had appointed over Daniel, Chananiah, Mishael, and Azaryah:


###### Daniel 1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:12)


נַס־נָ֥א אֶת־עֲבָדֶ֖יךָ יָמִ֣ים עֲשָׂרָ֑ה וְיִתְּנוּ־לָ֜נוּ מִן־הַזֵּרֹעִ֛ים וְנֹאכְלָ֖ה וּמַ֥יִם וְנִשְׁתֶּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Put thy servants to the proof I beseech thee, ten days; and let them give us vegetables to eat, and water to drink.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Try, I pray you, your servants, for ten days, and let them give us legumes to eat and water to drink.


###### Daniel 1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:13)


וְיֵרָא֤וּ לְפָנֶ֙יךָ֙ מַרְאֵ֔ינוּ וּמַרְאֵה֙ הַיְלָדִ֔ים הָאֹ֣כְלִ֔ים אֵ֖ת פַּת־בַּ֣ג הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וְכַאֲשֶׁ֣ר תִּרְאֵ֔ה עֲשֵׂ֖ה עִם־עֲבָדֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then let our appearances, and the appearances of the youths that eat of the king’s food be observed by you; and as thou seest, so deal with thy servants.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And let there be seen [and compared] before you our appearance and the appearance of the children that eat of the pathbag of the king, and, according to what you see, do with your servants.


###### Daniel 1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:14)


וַיִּשְׁמַ֥ע לָהֶ֖ם לַדָּבָ֣ר הַזֶּ֑ה וַיְנַסֵּ֖ם יָמִ֥ים עֲשָׂרָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So he consented to them in this matter, and put them to the proof for ten days.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he listened to them in this thing, and he tried them for ten days.


###### Daniel 1:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:15)


וּמִקְצָת֙ יָמִ֣ים עֲשָׂרָ֔ה נִרְאָ֤ה מַרְאֵיהֶם֙ ט֔וֹב וּבְרִיאֵ֖י בָּשָׂ֑ר מִ֨ן־כׇּל־הַיְלָדִ֔ים הָאֹ֣כְלִ֔ים אֵ֖ת פַּת־בַּ֥ג הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And at the end of ten days they appeared fairer and fatter in flesh than all the youths who did eat the portion of the king’s fare.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And, at the end of ten days, their appearance looked better and fuller fleshed than (that of) all of the boys who had eaten the pathbag of the king.


###### Daniel 1:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:16)


וַיְהִ֣י הַמֶּלְצַ֗ר נֹשֵׂא֙ אֶת־פַּת־בָּגָ֔ם וְיֵ֖ין מִשְׁתֵּיהֶ֑ם וְנֹתֵ֥ן לָהֶ֖ם זֵרְעֹנִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So the steward took away the food and the wine that they should have drunk; and gave them vegetables.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the steward took their pathbag and their wine to drink [for himself] and gave them legumes.


###### Daniel 1:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:17)


וְהַיְלָדִ֤ים הָאֵ֙לֶּה֙ אַרְבַּעְתָּ֔ם נָתַ֨ן לָהֶ֧ם הָֽאֱלֹהִ֛ים מַדָּ֥ע וְהַשְׂכֵּ֖ל בְּכׇל־סֵ֣פֶר וְחׇכְמָ֑ה וְדָנִיֵּ֣אל הֵבִ֔ין בְּכׇל־חָז֖וֹן וַחֲלֹמֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As for these four youths, God gave them knowledge and skill in all learning and wisdom: and Daniyyel had understanding in all visions and dreams.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And these boys, the four of them, were given understanding and knowledge of all writing and wisdom by G-d, and Daniel understood all visions and dreams.


###### Daniel 1:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:18)


וּלְמִקְצָת֙ הַיָּמִ֔ים אֲשֶׁר־אָמַ֥ר הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ לַהֲבִיאָ֑ם וַיְבִיאֵם֙ שַׂ֣ר הַסָּרִיסִ֔ים לִפְנֵ֖י נְבֻכַדְנֶצַּֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now at the end of the days that the king had said he should bring them in, then the chief of the eunuchs brought them in before Nevukhadneżżar.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And at the end of the days that the king had said to bring them [before him], the chief of the eunuchs brought them before Nevuchadnezzar.


###### Daniel 1:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:19)


וַיְדַבֵּ֣ר אִתָּם֮ הַמֶּ֒לֶךְ֒ וְלֹ֤א נִמְצָא֙ מִכֻּלָּ֔ם כְּדָנִיֵּ֣אל חֲנַנְיָ֔ה מִֽישָׁאֵ֖ל וַעֲזַרְיָ֑ה וַיַּֽעַמְד֖וּ לִפְנֵ֥י הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the king spoke with them; and among them all was found none like Daniyyel, Ḥananya, Misha᾽el, and ῾Azarya: therefore they stood before the king.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king spoke with them, and there were not found in all of them [(the other boys)] the likes of Daniel, Chananiah, Mishael, and Azaryah. And they stood [in attendance] before the king.


###### Daniel 1:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:20)


וְכֹ֗ל דְּבַר֙ חׇכְמַ֣ת בִּינָ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־בִּקֵּ֥שׁ מֵהֶ֖ם הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וַֽיִּמְצָאֵ֞ם עֶ֣שֶׂר יָד֗וֹת עַ֤ל כׇּל־הַֽחַרְטֻמִּים֙ הָֽאַשָּׁפִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֖ר בְּכׇל־מַלְכוּתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And in all matters of wisdom and understanding that the king inquired of them, he found them ten times better than all the magicians and conjurers who were in all his realm.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And anything requiring the wisdom of understanding which the king asked of them, he found in them ten times as much as in all the bone-conjurers and astrologers in all of his kingdom.


###### Daniel 1:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/1:21)


וַֽיְהִי֙ דָּֽנִיֵּ֔אל עַד־שְׁנַ֥ת אַחַ֖ת לְכ֥וֹרֶשׁ הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Daniyyel continued to the first year of Koresh the king.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Daniel was [i.e., he officiated in grandeur in Bavel] until the first year of King Koresh.



## Chapter 2

###### Daniel 2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:1)


וּבִשְׁנַ֣ת שְׁתַּ֗יִם לְמַלְכוּת֙ נְבֻֽכַדְנֶצַּ֔ר חָלַ֥ם נְבֻֽכַדְנֶצַּ֖ר חֲלֹמ֑וֹת וַתִּתְפָּ֣עֶם רוּח֔וֹ וּשְׁנָת֖וֹ נִהְיְתָ֥ה עָלָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And in the second year of the reign of Nevukhadneżżar, Nevukhadneżżar dreamed dreams, wherewith his spirit was troubled, and his sleep was gone from him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And in the second year of the kingdom of Nevuchadnezzar [i.e., the second year after the destruction of the Temple], Nevuchadnezzar dreamed dreams and his spirit throbbed within him, [(both the dream and its meaning evading him)], and his sleep was broken upon him.


###### Daniel 2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:2)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר הַ֠מֶּ֠לֶךְ לִקְרֹ֨א לַֽחַרְטֻמִּ֜ים וְלָֽאַשָּׁפִ֗ים וְלַֽמְכַשְּׁפִים֙ וְלַכַּשְׂדִּ֔ים לְהַגִּ֥יד לַמֶּ֖לֶךְ חֲלֹמֹתָ֑יו וַיָּבֹ֕אוּ וַיַּֽעַמְד֖וּ לִפְנֵ֥י הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then the king commanded to call the magicians, and the conjurers, and the sorcerers, and the Kasdians, to tell the king his dreams. So they came and stood before the king.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king bade (them) to call the bone-conjurers, the astrologers, the magicians, and the Kasdim [(diviners)] to tell the king [the import of] his dreams. And they stood before the king.


###### Daniel 2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:3)


וַיֹּ֧אמֶר לָהֶ֛ם הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ חֲל֣וֹם חָלָ֑מְתִּי וַתִּפָּ֣עֶם רוּחִ֔י לָדַ֖עַת אֶֽת־הַחֲלֽוֹם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the king said to them, I have dreamed a dream, and my spirit was troubled to know the dream.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king said to them: I dreamed a dream, and my spirit was agitated [in my craving] to know the dream.


###### Daniel 2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:4)


וַֽיְדַבְּר֧וּ הַכַּשְׂדִּ֛ים לַמֶּ֖לֶךְ אֲרָמִ֑ית מַלְכָּא֙ לְעָלְמִ֣ין חֱיִ֔י אֱמַ֥ר חֶלְמָ֛א (לעבדיך) [לְעַבְדָ֖ךְ] וּפִשְׁרָ֥א נְחַוֵּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then the Kasdians spoke to the king in the language of Aram, O king, live for ever: tell thy servants the dream, and we will declare the interpretation.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Kasdians spoke to the king in the language of Aram: O king, live for ever; tell thy servants the dream, and we will declare the interpretation.


###### Daniel 2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:5)


עָנֵ֤ה מַלְכָּא֙ וְאָמַ֣ר (לכשדיא) [לְכַשְׂדָּאֵ֔י] מִלְּתָ֖ה מִנִּ֣י אַזְדָּ֑א הֵ֣ן לָ֤א תְהֽוֹדְעוּנַּ֙נִי֙ חֶלְמָ֣א וּפִשְׁרֵ֔הּ הַדָּמִין֙ תִּתְעַבְד֔וּן וּבָתֵּיכ֖וֹן נְוָלִ֥י יִתְּשָׂמֽוּן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The king answered and said to the Kasdians, The thing is gone from me: if you will not make known to me the dream, with its interpretation, you shall be cut in pieces, and your houses shall be made a dunghill.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The king answered and said to the Kasdians: The thing is gone from me. If you will not make known to me the dream with its interpretation, you will be cut in pieces and your houses will be made dunghills.


###### Daniel 2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:6)


וְהֵ֨ן חֶלְמָ֤א וּפִשְׁרֵהּ֙ תְּֽהַחֲוֺ֔ן מַתְּנָ֤ן וּנְבִזְבָּה֙ וִיקָ֣ר שַׂגִּ֔יא תְּקַבְּל֖וּן מִן־קֳדָמָ֑י לָהֵ֕ן חֶלְמָ֥א וּפִשְׁרֵ֖הּ הַחֲוֺֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But if you declare the dream, and its meaning, you shall receive of me gifts and rewards and great honour: therefore declare to me the dream, and its meaning.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But if you declare the dream and its meaning, you shall receive of me gifts and rewards and great honor; but, declare to me the dream and its meaning.


###### Daniel 2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:7)


עֲנ֥וֹ תִנְיָנ֖וּת וְאָמְרִ֑ין מַלְכָּ֕א חֶלְמָ֛א יֵאמַ֥ר לְעַבְד֖וֹהִי וּפִשְׁרָ֥ה נְהַחֲוֵֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They answered again and said, Let the king tell his servants the dream, and we will declare its meaning.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They answered a second time and said: Let the king tell his servants the dream, and we will declare its meaning.


###### Daniel 2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:8)


עָנֵ֤ה מַלְכָּא֙ וְאָמַ֔ר מִן־יַצִּיב֙ יָדַ֣ע אֲנָ֔ה דִּ֥י עִדָּנָ֖א אַנְתּ֣וּן זָבְנִ֑ין כׇּל־קֳבֵל֙ דִּ֣י חֲזֵית֔וֹן דִּ֥י אַזְדָּ֖א מִנִּ֥י מִלְּתָֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The king answered and said, I know for a certainty that you wish to gain time, because you see the thing is determined by me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The king answered and said: I know for a certainty that this time you are relegated [to death] for you see that the thing has gone forth from me [and yet you ask me a second time.]


###### Daniel 2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:9)


דִּ֣י הֵן־חֶלְמָא֩ לָ֨א תְהֽוֹדְעֻנַּ֜נִי חֲדָה־הִ֣יא דָֽתְכ֗וֹן וּמִלָּ֨ה כִדְבָ֤ה וּשְׁחִיתָה֙ (הזמנתון) [הִזְדְּמִנְתּוּן֙] לְמֵאמַ֣ר קׇֽדָמַ֔י עַ֛ד דִּ֥י עִדָּנָ֖א יִשְׁתַּנֵּ֑א לָהֵ֗ן חֶלְמָא֙ אֱמַ֣רוּ לִ֔י וְֽאִנְדַּ֕ע דִּ֥י פִשְׁרֵ֖הּ תְּהַחֲוֻנַּֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But if you will not make known to me the dream, there is but one law for you: for you have prepared lying and corrupt words to speak before me, till the time be changed: therefore tell me the dream, and I shall know that you can declare its interpretation to me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Now if you will not make known to me the dream [and its interpretation], there is but one law for you [i.e., to die]. And (if) you prepare lying and corrupt words to speak before me, before the time changes [from day to night], you will die. Therefore, tell me the dream, and I shall know that you can declare its interpretation to me.


###### Daniel 2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:10)


עֲנ֨וֹ (כשדיא) [כַשְׂדָּאֵ֤י] קֳדָם־מַלְכָּא֙ וְאָ֣מְרִ֔ין לָֽא־אִיתַ֤י אֱנָשׁ֙ עַל־יַבֶּשְׁתָּ֔א דִּ֚י מִלַּ֣ת מַלְכָּ֔א יוּכַ֖ל לְהַחֲוָיָ֑ה כׇּל־קֳבֵ֗ל דִּ֚י כׇּל־מֶ֙לֶךְ֙ רַ֣ב וְשַׁלִּ֔יט מִלָּ֤ה כִדְנָה֙ לָ֣א שְׁאֵ֔ל לְכׇל־חַרְטֹ֖ם וְאָשַׁ֥ף וְכַשְׂדָּֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The Kasdians answered before the king, and said, There is not a man upon the earth who can declare the king’s matter: for no king, lord, nor ruler, has asked such things of any magician, or conjurer, or Kasdian.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The Kasdians answered and said: There is not a man upon the earth who can declare the king's matter; for no king, lord, nor ruler has asked such things of bone-conjurer, or astrologer, or Kasdian.


###### Daniel 2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:11)


וּמִלְּתָ֨א דִֽי־מַלְכָּ֤ה שָׁאֵל֙ יַקִּירָ֔ה וְאׇחֳרָן֙ לָ֣א אִיתַ֔י דִּ֥י יְחַוִּנַּ֖הּ קֳדָ֣ם מַלְכָּ֑א לָהֵ֣ן אֱלָהִ֔ין דִּ֚י מְדָ֣רְה֔וֹן עִם־בִּשְׂרָ֖א לָ֥א אִיתֽוֹהִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it is a hard thing that the king demands, and there is none other that can declare it before the king, except the gods, whose dwelling is not with flesh.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it is a hard thing that the king demands, and there is none other that can declare it before the king, except the angels, whose dwelling is not with flesh.


###### Daniel 2:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:12)


כׇּל־קֳבֵ֣ל דְּנָ֔ה מַלְכָּ֕א בְּנַ֖ס וּקְצַ֣ף שַׂגִּ֑יא וַאֲמַר֙ לְה֣וֹבָדָ֔ה לְכֹ֖ל חַכִּימֵ֥י בָבֶֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For this cause the king was angry and very furious, and commanded to destroy all the wise men of Bavel.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
This roused the king's ire and wrath, and he commanded to destroy all the wise men of Bavel.


###### Daniel 2:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:13)


וְדָתָ֣א נֶפְקַ֔ת וְחַכִּֽימַיָּ֖א מִֽתְקַטְּלִ֑ין וּבְע֛וֹ דָּנִיֵּ֥אל וְחַבְר֖וֹהִי לְהִתְקְטָלָֽה׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the decree went forth that the wise men should be slain; and they sought Daniyyel and his companions to be slain.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the decree went forth that the wise men should be slain; and Daniel and his companions were sought to be slain.


###### Daniel 2:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:14)


בֵּאדַ֣יִן דָּנִיֵּ֗אל הֲתִיב֙ עֵטָ֣א וּטְעֵ֔ם לְאַרְי֕וֹךְ רַב־טַבָּחַיָּ֖א דִּ֣י מַלְכָּ֑א דִּ֚י נְפַ֣ק לְקַטָּלָ֔ה לְחַכִּימֵ֖י בָּבֶֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Daniyyel answered with counsel and discretion to Aryokh the captain of the king’s guard, who had gone forth to slay the wise men of Bavel:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Daniel answered with counsel and discretion to Aryoch, the king's chief executioner, who had gone forth to slay the wise men of Bavel.


###### Daniel 2:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:15)


עָנֵ֣ה וְאָמַ֗ר לְאַרְיוֹךְ֙ שַׁלִּיטָ֣א דִֽי־מַלְכָּ֔א עַל־מָ֥ה דָתָ֛א מְהַחְצְפָ֖ה מִן־קֳדָ֣ם מַלְכָּ֑א אֱדַ֣יִן מִלְּתָ֔א הוֹדַ֥ע אַרְי֖וֹךְ לְדָנִיֵּֽאל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
he answered and said to Aryokh the king’s captain, Why is the decree from the king so severe? Then Aryokh made the thing known to Daniyyel.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He answered and said to Aryoch, the king's captain: Why is the king's decree so severe? Then Aryoch made the thing known to Daniel.


###### Daniel 2:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:16)


וְדָ֣נִיֵּ֔אל עַ֖ל וּבְעָ֣ה מִן־מַלְכָּ֑א דִּ֚י זְמָ֣ן יִנְתִּן־לֵ֔הּ וּפִשְׁרָ֖א לְהַֽחֲוָיָ֥ה לְמַלְכָּֽא׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Daniyyel went in, and desired of the king that he would give him time, so that he might show the king the interpretation.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Daniel went in and desired of the king that he give him time to tell the king the interpretation.


###### Daniel 2:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:17)


אֱדַ֥יִן דָּנִיֵּ֖אל לְבַיְתֵ֣הּ אֲזַ֑ל וְ֠לַחֲנַנְיָ֠ה מִֽישָׁאֵ֧ל וַעֲזַרְיָ֛ה חַבְר֖וֹהִי מִלְּתָ֥א הוֹדַֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Daniyyel went to his house, and made the thing known to Ḥananya, Misha᾽el, and ῾Azarya, his companions:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Daniel went to his house and made the thing known to Chananiah, Mishael, and Azaryah, his companions.


###### Daniel 2:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:18)


וְרַחֲמִ֗ין לְמִבְעֵא֙ מִן־קֳדָם֙ אֱלָ֣הּ שְׁמַיָּ֔א עַל־רָזָ֖א דְּנָ֑ה דִּ֣י לָ֤א יְהֽוֹבְדוּן֙ דָּנִיֵּ֣אל וְחַבְר֔וֹהִי עִם־שְׁאָ֖ר חַכִּימֵ֥י בָבֶֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that they would beg mercy from the God of heaven concerning this secret; that Daniyyel and his companions should not perish with the rest of the wise men of Bavel.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
That they beg mercy of the G-d of heaven concerning this secret; that Daniel and his companions not perish with the rest of the wise men of Bavel.


###### Daniel 2:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:19)


אֱדַ֗יִן לְדָנִיֵּ֛אל בְּחֶזְוָ֥א דִֽי־לֵילְיָ֖א רָזָ֣א גְלִ֑י אֱדַ֙יִן֙ דָּֽנִיֵּ֔אל בָּרִ֖ךְ לֶאֱלָ֥הּ שְׁמַיָּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then was the secret revealed to Daniyyel in a night vision. So Daniyyel blessed the God of heaven.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then was the secret revealed to Daniel in a night vision. So Daniel blessed the G-d of heaven.


###### Daniel 2:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:20)


עָנֵ֤ה דָֽנִיֵּאל֙ וְאָמַ֔ר לֶהֱוֵ֨א שְׁמֵ֤הּ דִּֽי־אֱלָהָא֙ מְבָרַ֔ךְ מִן־עָלְמָ֖א וְעַ֣ד עָלְמָ֑א דִּ֧י חׇכְמְתָ֛א וּגְבוּרְתָ֖א דִּ֥י לֵֽהּ־הִֽיא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Daniyyel spoke and said, Blessed be the name of God for ever and ever: for wisdom and might are his:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Daniel spoke and said: Blessed be the name of G-d for ever and ever, for wisdom and might are His.


###### Daniel 2:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:21)


וְ֠ה֠וּא מְהַשְׁנֵ֤א עִדָּנַיָּא֙ וְזִמְנַיָּ֔א מְהַעְדֵּ֥ה מַלְכִ֖ין וּמְהָקֵ֣ים מַלְכִ֑ין יָהֵ֤ב חׇכְמְתָא֙ לְחַכִּימִ֔ין וּמַנְדְּעָ֖א לְיָדְעֵ֥י בִינָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he changes the times and the seasons: he removes kings, and sets up kings: he gives wisdom to the wise, and knowledge to those who have understanding:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And He changes the times and the seasons; He removes kings and sets up kings [(Daniel understood from the dream that Nevuchadnezzar's kingdom would be superseded by another)]; He gives wisdom to the wise, and knowledge to those who have understanding.


###### Daniel 2:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:22)


ה֛וּא גָּלֵ֥א עַמִּיקָתָ֖א וּמְסַתְּרָתָ֑א יָדַע֙ מָ֣ה בַחֲשׁוֹכָ֔א (ונהירא) [וּנְהוֹרָ֖א] עִמֵּ֥הּ שְׁרֵֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
he reveals the deep and secret things: he knows what is in the darkness, and the light dwells with him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He reveals the deep and secret things; He knows what is in the darkness and the light dwells with Him.


###### Daniel 2:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:23)


לָ֣ךְ**׀** אֱלָ֣הּ אֲבָהָתִ֗י מְהוֹדֵ֤א וּמְשַׁבַּח֙ אֲנָ֔ה דִּ֧י חׇכְמְתָ֛א וּגְבוּרְתָ֖א יְהַ֣בְתְּ לִ֑י וּכְעַ֤ן הֽוֹדַעְתַּ֙נִי֙ דִּֽי־בְעֵ֣ינָא מִנָּ֔ךְ דִּֽי־מִלַּ֥ת מַלְכָּ֖א הוֹדַעְתֶּֽנָא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I thank Thee, O Thou God of my fathers, who hast given me wisdom and might, and hast made known to me now what we desired of Thee: for Thou hast now made known to us the king’s matter.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I thank Thee and praise Thee, O, Thou, G-d of my fathers, who has given me wisdom and might, and has now made known to me what I desired of Thee; for Thou hast made known to me the king's matter.


###### Daniel 2:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:24)


כׇּל־קֳבֵ֣ל דְּנָ֗ה דָּֽנִיֵּאל֙ עַ֣ל עַל־אַרְי֔וֹךְ דִּ֚י מַנִּ֣י מַלְכָּ֔א לְהוֹבָדָ֖ה לְחַכִּימֵ֣י בָבֶ֑ל אֲזַ֣ל**׀** וְכֵ֣ן אֲמַר־לֵ֗הּ לְחַכִּימֵ֤י בָבֶל֙ אַל־תְּהוֹבֵ֔ד הַעֵ֙לְנִי֙ קֳדָ֣ם מַלְכָּ֔א וּפִשְׁרָ֖א לְמַלְכָּ֥א אֲחַוֵּֽא׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore Daniyyel went in to Aryokh, whom the king had ordained to destroy the wise men of Bavel: he went and said thus to him; Do not destroy the wise men of Bavel: bring me in before the king, and I will show the king the interpretation.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Thereupon, Daniel went in to Aryoch, whom the king had ordained to destroy the wise men of Bavel. He went and said thus to him: Do not destroy the wise men of Bavel. Bring me in before the king, and I will tell the king the interpretation.


###### Daniel 2:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:25)


אֱדַ֤יִן אַרְיוֹךְ֙ בְּהִתְבְּהָלָ֔ה הַנְעֵ֥ל לְדָנִיֵּ֖אל קֳדָ֣ם מַלְכָּ֑א וְכֵ֣ן אֲמַר־לֵ֗הּ דִּֽי־הַשְׁכַּ֤חַת גְּבַר֙ מִן־בְּנֵ֤י גָֽלוּתָא֙ דִּ֣י יְה֔וּד דִּ֥י פִשְׁרָ֖א לְמַלְכָּ֥א יְהוֹדַֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Aryokh brought in Daniyyel before the king in haste, and said thus to him, I have found a man of the exiles of Yehuda, who will make known to the king the interpretation.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Aryoch brought in Daniel before the king in haste, and said thus to him: I have found a man of the exiles of Judah, who will make known to the king the interpretation.


###### Daniel 2:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:26)


עָנֵ֤ה מַלְכָּא֙ וְאָמַ֣ר לְדָנִיֵּ֔אל דִּ֥י שְׁמֵ֖הּ בֵּלְטְשַׁאצַּ֑ר (האיתיך) [הַֽאִיתָ֣ךְ] כָּהֵ֗ל לְהוֹדָעֻתַ֛נִי חֶלְמָ֥א דִֽי־חֲזֵ֖ית וּפִשְׁרֵֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The king answered and said to Daniyyel, whose name was Belteshażżar, Art thou able to make known unto me the dream which I have seen, and its meaning?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The king answered and said to Daniel, whose name was Belshazzar: Art thou able to make known unto me the dream that I have seen, and its meaning?

עָנֵ֧ה דָנִיֵּ֛אל קֳדָ֥ם מַלְכָּ֖א וְאָמַ֑ר רָזָא֙ דִּֽי־מַלְכָּ֣א שָׁאֵ֔ל לָ֧א חַכִּימִ֣ין אָֽשְׁפִ֗ין חַרְטֻמִּין֙ גָּזְרִ֔ין יָכְלִ֖ין לְהַֽחֲוָיָ֥ה לְמַלְכָּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Daniyyel answered in the presence of the king, and said, The secret which the king has demanded is one that the wise men, the conjurers, the magicians, and the astrologers cannot declare to the king;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Daniel answered in the presence of the king and said: The secret which the king has demanded is one that the wise men, the astrologers, the bone-conjurers, and the snake charmers cannot declare to the king.


###### Daniel 2:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:28)


בְּרַ֡ם אִיתַ֞י אֱלָ֤הּ בִּשְׁמַיָּא֙ גָּלֵ֣א רָזִ֔ין וְהוֹדַ֗ע לְמַלְכָּא֙ נְבֽוּכַדְנֶצַּ֔ר מָ֛ה דִּ֥י לֶהֱוֵ֖א בְּאַחֲרִ֣ית יוֹמַיָּ֑א חֶלְמָ֨ךְ וְחֶזְוֵ֥י רֵאשָׁ֛ךְ עַֽל־מִשְׁכְּבָ֖ךְ דְּנָ֥ה הֽוּא׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but there is a God in heaven who reveals secret things, and makes known to the king Nevukhadneżżar what shall be in the latter days. Thy dream, and the visions of thy head upon thy bed, are these;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But there is a G-d in heaven who reveals secret things, and makes known to King Nevuchadnezzar what shall be in the latter days. Thy dreams, and the visions of thy head upon thy bed are these:


###### Daniel 2:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:29)


(אנתה) [אַ֣נְתְּ] מַלְכָּ֗א רַעְיוֹנָךְ֙ עַל־מִשְׁכְּבָ֣ךְ סְלִ֔קוּ מָ֛ה דִּ֥י לֶהֱוֵ֖א אַחֲרֵ֣י דְנָ֑ה וְגָלֵ֧א רָזַיָּ֛א הוֹדְעָ֖ךְ מָה־דִ֥י לֶהֱוֵֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As for thee, O king, thy thoughts came to thee upon thy bed, as to what should come to pass hereafter: and he who reveals secret things makes known to thee what shall come to pass.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As for thee, O king, thy [daytime] thoughts came up to thee upon thy bed, as to what should come to pass hereafter. And He who reveals secret things has made known to thee what shall come to pass.


###### Daniel 2:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:30)


וַאֲנָ֗ה לָ֤א בְחׇכְמָה֙ דִּֽי־אִיתַ֥י בִּי֙ מִן־כׇּל־חַיַּיָּ֔א רָזָ֥א דְנָ֖ה גֱּלִ֣י לִ֑י לָהֵ֗ן עַל־דִּבְרַת֙ דִּ֤י פִשְׁרָא֙ לְמַלְכָּ֣א יְהוֹדְע֔וּן וְרַעְיוֹנֵ֥י לִבְבָ֖ךְ תִּנְדַּֽע׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But as for me, this secret is not revealed to me for any wisdom that I have more than any living, but in order that the interpretation shall be made known to the king, and that thou mightest know the thoughts of thy heart.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But as for me, this secret is not revealed to me for any wisdom that I have more than any living being, but in order that the interpretation shall be made known to the king, and that thou mightest know [the answer to] the [constant] thoughts of thy heart [i.e., Who shall succeed me?]

This image’s head was of fine gold, its breast and its arms of silver, its belly and its thighs of brass,

its legs of iron, its feet partly of iron and partly of clay.

As thou didst look, a stone was cut out without hands, which smote the image upon its feet which were of iron and clay, and broke them to pieces.

בֵּאדַ֣יִן דָּ֣קוּ כַחֲדָ֡ה פַּרְזְלָא֩ חַסְפָּ֨א נְחָשָׁ֜א כַּסְפָּ֣א וְדַהֲבָ֗א וַהֲווֹ֙ כְּע֣וּר מִן־אִדְּרֵי־קַ֔יִט וּנְשָׂ֤א הִמּוֹן֙ רוּחָ֔א וְכׇל־אֲתַ֖ר לָא־הִשְׁתְּכַ֣ח לְה֑וֹן וְאַבְנָ֣א**׀** דִּֽי־מְחָ֣ת לְצַלְמָ֗א הֲוָ֛ת לְט֥וּר רַ֖ב וּמְלָ֥את כׇּל־אַרְעָֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then was the iron, the clay, the brass, the silver, and the gold, broken in pieces together, and became like the chaff of the summer threshing floors; and the wind carried them away, so that no place was found for them: and the stone that smote the image became a great mountain, and filled the whole earth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then was the iron, the clay, the brass, the silver, and the gold, broken in pieces together, and became like the chaff of the summer threshing floors; and the wind carried them away so that no place was found for them [i.e., their original place was devoid of them]. And the stone that smote the image became a great mountain, and filled the whole earth.

This is the dream, and we will state its meaning before the king.

(אנתה) [אַ֣נְתְּ] מַלְכָּ֔א מֶ֖לֶךְ מַלְכַיָּ֑א דִּ֚י אֱלָ֣הּ שְׁמַיָּ֔א מַלְכוּתָ֥א חִסְנָ֛א וְתׇקְפָּ֥א וִֽיקָרָ֖א יְהַב־לָֽךְ׃

###### Daniel 2:38

וּבְכׇל־דִּ֣י (דארין) [דָֽיְרִ֣ין] בְּֽנֵי־אֲ֠נָשָׁ֠א חֵיוַ֨ת בָּרָ֤א וְעוֹף־שְׁמַיָּא֙ יְהַ֣ב בִּידָ֔ךְ וְהַשְׁלְטָ֖ךְ בְּכׇלְּה֑וֹן (אנתה) [אַ֨נְתְּ־]ה֔וּא רֵאשָׁ֖ה דִּ֥י דַהֲבָֽא׃

and wherever the children of men dwell, the beasts of the field and the birds of the sky, he has given them into thy hand, and has made thee ruler over them all; thou art this head of gold.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And wherever the children of men dwell, the beasts of the field and the birds of the sky, He has given them into thy hand, and has made thee ruler over them all; thou art the head of gold.


###### Daniel 2:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:39)


וּבָתְרָ֗ךְ תְּק֛וּם מַלְכ֥וּ אׇחֳרִ֖י (ארעא) [אֲרַ֣ע] מִנָּ֑ךְ וּמַלְכ֨וּ (תליתיא) [תְלִיתָאָ֤ה] אׇחֳרִי֙ דִּ֣י נְחָשָׁ֔א דִּ֥י תִשְׁלַ֖ט בְּכׇל־אַרְעָֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And after thee shall arise another kingdom inferior to thee, and another third kingdom of brass, which shall bear rule over all the earth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And after thee [(after your son Belshazzar)] shall arise another kingdom [(Paras and Madai, who shall depose your seed, a kingdom)] inferior to thee [(as silver is inferior to gold)], and another, third, kingdom of brass, [(as powerful as brass. The kingdom of Greece, of Alexander of Macedonia)] which shall bear rule over the earth.


###### Daniel 2:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:40)


וּמַלְכוּ֙ (רביעיה) [רְבִ֣יעָאָ֔ה] תֶּהֱוֵ֥א תַקִּיפָ֖ה כְּפַרְזְלָ֑א כׇּל־קֳבֵ֗ל דִּ֤י פַרְזְלָא֙ מְהַדֵּ֤ק וְחָשֵׁל֙ כֹּ֔לָּא וּֽכְפַרְזְלָ֛א דִּֽי־מְרָעַ֥ע כׇּל־אִלֵּ֖ן תַּדִּ֥ק וְתֵרֹֽעַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the fourth kingdom shall be strong as iron: because iron breaks to pieces and subdues all things: and like iron that shatters, so shall it shatter and crush all these other.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the fourth kingdom [(Rome, the iron legs, which are "fourth" to gold, silver and brass)] shall be strong as iron in that iron [i.e., an iron hammer] crushes and subdues all things. And like iron, that destroys all [(these metals that you saw in the image)], so shall it crush and shatter (them).


###### Daniel 2:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:41)


וְדִֽי־חֲזַ֜יְתָה רַגְלַיָּ֣א וְאֶצְבְּעָתָ֗א (מנהון) [מִנְּהֵ֞ן] חֲסַ֤ף דִּֽי־פֶחָר֙ (ומנהון) [וּמִנְּהֵ֣ין] פַּרְזֶ֔ל מַלְכ֤וּ פְלִיגָה֙ תֶּהֱוֵ֔ה וּמִן־נִצְבְּתָ֥א דִֽי־פַרְזְלָ֖א לֶֽהֱוֵא־בַ֑הּ כׇּל־קֳבֵל֙ דִּ֣י חֲזַ֔יְתָה פַּ֨רְזְלָ֔א מְעָרַ֖ב בַּחֲסַ֥ף טִינָֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And whereas thou didst see the feet and toes, partly of potters’ clay, and partly of iron, so it shall be a divided kingdom; but there shall be in it of the strength of the iron, just as thou didst see the iron mixed with miry clay.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And whereas thou didst see the feet and toes, partly of potter's clay, and partly of iron, so it shall be a divided kingdom [(of two kings, one strong; the other weak)]; but there shall be in it [(the weaker, the clay)], of the strength of iron [(the stronger, which abets it)], just as thou didst see the iron mixed with miry clay.


###### Daniel 2:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:42)


וְאֶצְבְּעָת֙ רַגְלַיָּ֔א (מנהון) [מִנְּהֵ֥ין] פַּרְזֶ֖ל (ומנהון) [וּמִנְּהֵ֣ין] חֲסַ֑ף מִן־קְצָ֤ת מַלְכוּתָא֙ תֶּהֱוֵ֣ה תַקִּיפָ֔ה וּמִנַּ֖הּ תֶּהֱוֵ֥א תְבִירָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And as the toes of the feet were partly of iron, and partly of clay, so the kingdom shall be partly strong, and partly brittle.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And as the toes of the feet were partly of iron and partly of clay, so the kingdom shall be partly strong and partly brittle.


###### Daniel 2:43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:43)


(די) [וְדִ֣י] חֲזַ֗יְתָ פַּרְזְלָא֙ מְעָרַב֙ בַּחֲסַ֣ף טִינָ֔א מִתְעָרְבִ֤ין לֶהֱוֺן֙ בִּזְרַ֣ע אֲנָשָׁ֔א וְלָֽא־לֶהֱוֺ֥ן דָּבְקִ֖ין דְּנָ֣ה עִם־דְּנָ֑ה הֵֽא־כְדִ֣י פַרְזְלָ֔א לָ֥א מִתְעָרַ֖ב עִם־חַסְפָּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And whereas thou didst see iron mixed with miry clay, they shall mingle themselves with the seed of men: but they shall not cleave one to another, just as iron does not mix with clay.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
and whereas thou didst see iron mixed with miry clay, so they [i.e., Rome] shall mingle themselves [(by intermarriage)] with the seed of men [i.e., with other nations]; but they shall not cleave one to another, just as iron does not mix with clay.


###### Daniel 2:44

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:44](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:44)


וּֽבְיוֹמֵיה֞וֹן דִּ֧י מַלְכַיָּ֣א אִנּ֗וּן יְקִים֩ אֱלָ֨הּ שְׁמַיָּ֤א מַלְכוּ֙ דִּ֤י לְעָלְמִין֙ לָ֣א תִתְחַבַּ֔ל וּמַ֨לְכוּתָ֔ה לְעַ֥ם אׇחֳרָ֖ן לָ֣א תִשְׁתְּבִ֑ק תַּדִּ֤ק וְתָסֵיף֙ כׇּל־אִלֵּ֣ין מַלְכְוָתָ֔א וְהִ֖יא תְּק֥וּם לְעָלְמַיָּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And in the days of these kings shall the God of heaven set up a kingdom, which shall never be destroyed: and the kingdom shall not be left to another people, but it shall break in pieces and consume all these kingdoms, and it shall stand for ever:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And in the days of these [Roman] kings shall the G-d of heaven set up a kingdom, which shall never be destroyed [(that of the Messiah)]. And the kingdom shall not be left to another people, but it shall break in pieces and consume all these kingdoms, and it shall stand for ever.


###### Daniel 2:45

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:45](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:45)


כׇּל־קֳבֵ֣ל דִּֽי־חֲזַ֡יְתָ דִּ֣י מִטּוּרָא֩ אִתְגְּזֶ֨רֶת אֶ֜בֶן דִּי־לָ֣א בִידַ֗יִן וְ֠הַדֵּ֠קֶת פַּרְזְלָ֨א נְחָשָׁ֤א חַסְפָּא֙ כַּסְפָּ֣א וְדַהֲבָ֔א אֱלָ֥הּ רַב֙ הוֹדַ֣ע לְמַלְכָּ֔א מָ֛ה דִּ֥י לֶהֱוֵ֖א אַחֲרֵ֣י דְנָ֑ה וְיַצִּ֥יב חֶלְמָ֖א וּמְהֵימַ֥ן פִּשְׁרֵֽהּ׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
just as thou didst see that a stone was cut out of the mountain without hands, and that it broke in pieces the iron, the brass, the clay, the silver, and the gold. The great God has made known to the king what shall come to pass hereafter: and the dream is certain, and the meaning of it sure.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Just as thou didst see that a stone was cut out of the mountain without hands, and that it broke in pieces the iron, the brass, the clay, the silver, and the gold. The great G-d has made known to the king what shall come to pass after this [i.e., after your kingdom]; and the dream is true, and its interpretation sure.


###### Daniel 2:46

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:46](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:46)


בֵּ֠אדַ֠יִן מַלְכָּ֤א נְבֽוּכַדְנֶצַּר֙ נְפַ֣ל עַל־אַנְפּ֔וֹהִי וּלְדָנִיֵּ֖אל סְגִ֑ד וּמִנְחָה֙ וְנִ֣יחֹחִ֔ין אֲמַ֖ר לְנַסָּ֥כָה לֵֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then the king Nevukhadneżżar fell upon his face, and bowed down to Daniyyel, and commanded that they should offer an offering and sweet odours to him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then the king Nevuchadnezzar fell upon his face, and bowed down to Daniel [(wanting to make him a god)], and commanded that they should offer an offering and sweet odors to him.


###### Daniel 2:47

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:47](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:47)


עָנֵה֩ מַלְכָּ֨א לְדָנִיֵּ֜אל וְאָמַ֗ר מִן־קְשֹׁט֙ דִּ֣י אֱלָהֲכ֗וֹן ה֣וּא אֱלָ֧הּ אֱלָהִ֛ין וּמָרֵ֥א מַלְכִ֖ין וְגָלֵ֣ה רָזִ֑ין דִּ֣י יְכֵ֔לְתָּ לְמִגְלֵ֖א רָזָ֥א דְנָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The king spoke to Daniyyel, and said, Of a truth it is, that your God is the God of gods, and the Lord of kings, and a revealer of secrets, seeing thou couldst reveal this secret.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The king spoke to Daniel and said: Of a truth it is that your G-d is the G-d of gods, and the L-rd of lords, and a revealer of secrets, seeing that thou couldst reveal this secret.


###### Daniel 2:48

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:48](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:48)


אֱדַ֨יִן מַלְכָּ֜א לְדָנִיֵּ֣אל רַבִּ֗י וּמַתְּנָ֨ן רַבְרְבָ֤ן שַׂגִּיאָן֙ יְהַב־לֵ֔הּ וְהַ֨שְׁלְטֵ֔הּ עַ֖ל כׇּל־מְדִינַ֣ת בָּבֶ֑ל וְרַ֨ב־סִגְנִ֔ין עַ֖ל כׇּל־חַכִּימֵ֥י בָבֶֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then the king made Daniyyel great, and gave him many great gifts, and made him ruler over the whole province of Bavel, and the chief prefect over all the wise men of Bavel.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then the king made Daniel great [(over all the other nobles)] and gave him many costly gifts and made him ruler over the whole province of Bavel and the chief prefect over all the wise men of Bavel.


###### Daniel 2:49

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:49](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/2:49)


וְדָנִיֵּאל֙ בְּעָ֣א מִן־מַלְכָּ֔א וּמַנִּ֗י עַ֤ל עֲבִֽידְתָּא֙ דִּ֚י מְדִינַ֣ת בָּבֶ֔ל לְשַׁדְרַ֥ךְ מֵישַׁ֖ךְ וַעֲבֵ֣ד נְג֑וֹ וְדָנִיֵּ֖אל בִּתְרַ֥ע מַלְכָּֽא׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Daniyyel requested of the king, and he set Shadrakh, Meshakh, and ῾Aved-nego over the affairs of the province of Bavel: but Daniyyel remained in the gate of the king.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Daniel requested of the king, and he set Shadrach, Meshach, and Aved-nego over the affairs of the province of Bavel. And Daniel remained in the gate of the king.



## Chapter 3

###### Daniel 3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:1)


נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּ֣ר מַלְכָּ֗א עֲבַד֙ צְלֵ֣ם דִּֽי־דְהַ֔ב רוּמֵהּ֙ אַמִּ֣ין שִׁתִּ֔ין פְּתָיֵ֖הּ אַמִּ֣ין שִׁ֑ת אֲקִימֵהּ֙ בְּבִקְעַ֣ת דּוּרָ֔א בִּמְדִינַ֖ת בָּבֶֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Nevukhadneżżar the king made an image of gold, whose height was sixty cubits, and the breadth of which was six cubits: he set it up in the plain of Dura, in the province of Bavel.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Nevuchadnezzar the king made an image of gold, whose height was sixty cubits and the breadth of which was six cubits. He set it up in the plain of Dura, in the province of Bavel.


###### Daniel 3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:2)


וּנְבוּכַדְנֶצַּ֣ר מַלְכָּ֡א שְׁלַ֡ח לְמִכְנַ֣שׁ**׀** לַֽאֲחַשְׁדַּרְפְּנַיָּ֡א סִגְנַיָּ֣א וּֽפַחֲוָתָ֡א אֲדַרְגָּזְרַיָּא֩ גְדָ֨בְרַיָּ֤א דְּתָבְרַיָּא֙ תִּפְתָּיֵ֔א וְכֹ֖ל שִׁלְטֹנֵ֣י מְדִֽינָתָ֑א לְמֵתֵא֙ לַחֲנֻכַּ֣ת צַלְמָ֔א דִּ֥י הֲקֵ֖ים נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּ֥ר מַלְכָּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Nevukhadneżżar the king sent to gather together the satraps, the prefects, and the governors, the counsellors, the treasurers, the justices, the magistrates, and all the rulers of the provinces, to come to the dedication of the image which Nevukhadneżżar the king had set up.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Nevuchadnezzar the king sent to gather together the satraps, the prefects, and the governors, the counselors, the treasurers, the justices, and magistrates, and all the rulers of the provinces to come to the dedication of the image [(the beginning of its worship)] which Nevuchadnezzar the king had set up.


###### Daniel 3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:3)


בֵּאדַ֡יִן מִֽתְכַּנְּשִׁ֡ין אֲחַשְׁדַּרְפְּנַיָּ֡א סִגְנַיָּ֣א וּֽפַחֲוָתָ֡א אֲדַרְגָּזְרַיָּ֣א גְדָבְרַיָּא֩ דְּתָ֨בְרַיָּ֜א תִּפְתָּיֵ֗א וְכֹל֙ שִׁלְטֹנֵ֣י מְדִֽינָתָ֔א לַחֲנֻכַּ֣ת צַלְמָ֔א דִּ֥י הֲקֵ֖ים נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּ֣ר מַלְכָּ֑א (וקאמין) [וְקָֽיְמִין֙] לׇקֳבֵ֣ל צַלְמָ֔א דִּ֥י הֲקֵ֖ים נְבֻכַדְנֶצַּֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then the satraps, the prefects, and the governors, the counsellors, the treasurers, the justices, the magistrates, and all the rulers of the provinces, were gathered together to the dedication of the image that Nevukhadneżżar the king had set up and they stood before the image that Nevukhadneżżar had set up.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then the satraps, the prefects, and the governors, the counselors, the treasurers, the justices, the magistrates, and all the rulers of the provinces were gathered together to the dedication of the image that Nevuchadnezzar the king had set up; and they stood before the image that Nevuchadnezzar had set up.

וְכָרוֹזָ֖א קָרֵ֣א בְחָ֑יִל לְכ֤וֹן אָֽמְרִין֙ עַֽמְמַיָּ֔א אֻמַּיָּ֖א וְלִשָּׁנַיָּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then a herald cried aloud, To you it is commanded, O peoples, nations, and tongues,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then a herald cried aloud: To you it is commanded, O peoples, nations, and tongues,


###### Daniel 3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:5)


בְּעִדָּנָ֡א דִּֽי־תִשְׁמְע֡וּן קָ֣ל קַרְנָ֣א מַ֠שְׁרוֹקִיתָ֠א (קיתרס) [קַתְר֨וֹס] שַׂבְּכָ֤א פְסַנְתֵּרִין֙ סוּמְפֹּ֣נְיָ֔א וְכֹ֖ל זְנֵ֣י זְמָרָ֑א תִּפְּל֤וּן וְתִסְגְּדוּן֙ לְצֶ֣לֶם דַּהֲבָ֔א דִּ֥י הֲקֵ֖ים נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּ֥ר מַלְכָּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that when you hear the sound of the horn, pipe, lyre, trigon, harp, bagpipe, and all kinds of music, you fall down and worship the golden image that Nevukhadneżżar the king has set up:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
That when you hear the sound of the horn, pipe, lyre, trigon, harp, bagpipe, and all kinds of music, you fall down and worship the golden image that Nevuchadnezzar the king has set up.


###### Daniel 3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:6)


וּמַן־דִּי־לָ֥א יִפֵּ֖ל וְיִסְגֻּ֑ד בַּהּ־שַׁעֲתָ֣א יִתְרְמֵ֔א לְגֽוֹא־אַתּ֥וּן נוּרָ֖א יָקִֽדְתָּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and whoever does not fall down and worship shall be cast in that same hour into the midst of a burning fiery furnace.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And whoever does not fall down and worship shall be cast in that same hour into the midst of a burning fiery furnace.


###### Daniel 3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:7)


כׇּל־קֳבֵ֣ל דְּנָ֡ה בֵּהּ־זִמְנָ֡א כְּדִ֣י שָֽׁמְעִ֣ין כׇּֽל־עַמְמַיָּ֡א קָ֣ל קַרְנָא֩ מַשְׁר֨וֹקִיתָ֜א (קיתרס) [קַתְר֤וֹס] שַׂבְּכָא֙ פְּסַנְטֵרִ֔ין וְכֹ֖ל זְנֵ֣י זְמָרָ֑א נָֽפְלִ֨ין כׇּֽל־עַֽמְמַיָּ֜א אֻמַּיָּ֣א וְלִשָּׁנַיָּ֗א סָֽגְדִין֙ לְצֶ֣לֶם דַּהֲבָ֔א דִּ֥י הֲקֵ֖ים נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּ֥ר מַלְכָּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore at that time, when all the people heard the sound of the horn, pipe, lyre, trigon, harp, and all kinds of music, all the peoples, nations, and tongues, fell down and worshipped the golden image that Nevukhadneżżar the king had set up.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Therefore, at that time, when all the people heard the sound of the horn, pipe, lyre, trigon, harp, and all kinds of music, all the peoples, nations, and tongues, fell down and worshipped the golden image that Nevuchadnezzar the king had set up.


###### Daniel 3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:8)


כׇּל־קֳבֵ֤ל דְּנָה֙ בֵּהּ־זִמְנָ֔א קְרִ֖בוּ גֻּבְרִ֣ין כַּשְׂדָּאִ֑ין וַאֲכַ֥לוּ קַרְצֵיה֖וֹן דִּ֥י יְהוּדָיֵֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore at that time certain Kasdians came near, and accused the Jews.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### Daniel 3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:9)


עֲנוֹ֙ וְאָ֣מְרִ֔ין לִנְבוּכַדְנֶצַּ֖ר מַלְכָּ֑א מַלְכָּ֖א לְעָלְמִ֥ין חֱיִֽי׃

They spoke and said to the king Nevukhadneżżar, O king, live for ever.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They spoke up and said to the king Nevuchadnezzar: O king, live for ever.


###### Daniel 3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:10)


(אנתה) [אַ֣נְתְּ] מַלְכָּא֮ שָׂ֣מְתָּ טְּעֵם֒ דִּ֣י כׇל־אֱנָ֡שׁ דִּֽי־יִשְׁמַ֡ע קָ֣ל קַרְנָ֣א מַ֠שְׁרֹקִיתָ֠א (קיתרס) [קַתְר֨וֹס] שַׂבְּכָ֤א פְסַנְתֵּרִין֙ (וסיפניה) [וְסוּפֹּ֣נְיָ֔ה] וְכֹ֖ל זְנֵ֣י זְמָרָ֑א יִפֵּ֥ל וְיִסְגֻּ֖ד לְצֶ֥לֶם דַּהֲבָֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou, O king, hast made a decree, that every man that shall hear the sound of the horn, pipe, lyre, trigon, harp, and bagpipe, and all kinds of music, shall fall down and worship the golden image:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Thou, O king, hast made a decree that every man that shall hear the sound of the horn, pipe, lyre, trigon, harp, and bagpipe, and all kinds of music, shall fall down and worship the golden image.


###### Daniel 3:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:11)


וּמַן־דִּי־לָ֥א יִפֵּ֖ל וְיִסְגֻּ֑ד יִתְרְמֵ֕א לְגֽוֹא־אַתּ֥וּן נוּרָ֖א יָקִֽדְתָּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and whoever does not fall down and worship, he should be cast into the midst of a burning fiery furnace.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And whoever does not fall down and worship, he should be cast into the midst of a burning fiery furnace.


###### Daniel 3:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:12)


אִיתַ֞י גֻּבְרִ֣ין יְהוּדָאיִ֗ן דִּֽי־מַנִּ֤יתָ יָתְהוֹן֙ עַל־עֲבִידַת֙ מְדִינַ֣ת בָּבֶ֔ל שַׁדְרַ֥ךְ מֵישַׁ֖ךְ וַעֲבֵ֣ד נְג֑וֹ גֻּבְרַיָּ֣א אִלֵּ֗ךְ לָא־שָׂ֨מֽוּ (עליך) [עֲלָ֤ךְ] מַלְכָּא֙ טְעֵ֔ם (לאלהיך) [לֵֽאלָהָךְ֙] לָ֣א פָלְחִ֔ין וּלְצֶ֧לֶם דַּהֲבָ֛א דִּ֥י הֲקֵ֖ימְתָּ לָ֥א סָגְדִֽין׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There are certain Jews whom thou hast set over the affairs of the province of Bavel, Shadrakh, Meshakh, and ῾Aved-nego; these men, O king, have not regarded thee: they do not serve thy gods, nor worship the golden image which thou hast set up.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There are certain men of Judah whom thou hast set over the affairs of the province of Bavel: Shadrach, Meshach, and Aved-nego. These men, O king, have not regarded thee. They do not serve thy gods nor worship the golden image which thou hast set up.


###### Daniel 3:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:13)


בֵּאדַ֤יִן נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר֙ בִּרְגַ֣ז וַחֲמָ֔א אֲמַר֙ לְהַיְתָיָ֔ה לְשַׁדְרַ֥ךְ מֵישַׁ֖ךְ וַעֲבֵ֣ד נְג֑וֹ בֵּאדַ֙יִן֙ גֻּבְרַיָּ֣א אִלֵּ֔ךְ הֵיתָ֖יוּ קֳדָ֥ם מַלְכָּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Nevukhadneżżar in his rage and fury commanded to bring Shadrakh, Meshakh, and ῾Aved-nego. Then they brought these men before the king.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Nevuchadnezzar in his rage and fury commanded to bring Shadrach, Meshach, and Aved-nego. Then these men were brought before the king.


###### Daniel 3:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:14)


עָנֵ֤ה נְבֽוּכַדְנֶצַּר֙ וְאָמַ֣ר לְה֔וֹן הַצְדָּ֕א שַׁדְרַ֥ךְ מֵישַׁ֖ךְ וַעֲבֵ֣ד נְג֑וֹ לֵֽאלָהַ֗י לָ֤א אִֽיתֵיכוֹן֙ פָּֽלְחִ֔ין וּלְצֶ֧לֶם דַּהֲבָ֛א דִּ֥י הֲקֵ֖ימֶת לָ֥א סָֽגְדִֽין׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Nevukhadneżżar spoke and said to them, Is it true, O Shadrakh, Meshakh, and ῾Aved-nego, that you do not serve my god, nor worship the golden image which I have set up?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Nevuchadnezzar spoke and said to them: Is [my decree] vain [in your eyes], Shadrach, Meshach, and Aved-nego, that you do not serve my god, nor worship the golden image which I have set up?


###### Daniel 3:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:15)


כְּעַ֞ן הֵ֧ן אִֽיתֵיכ֣וֹן עֲתִידִ֗ין דִּ֣י בְעִדָּנָ֡א דִּֽי־תִשְׁמְע֡וּן קָ֣ל קַרְנָ֣א מַשְׁרוֹקִיתָ֣א (קיתרס) [קַתְר֣וֹס] שַׂבְּכָ֡א פְּסַנְתֵּרִין֩ וְסוּמְפֹּ֨נְיָ֜ה וְכֹ֣ל**׀** זְנֵ֣י זְמָרָ֗א תִּפְּל֣וּן וְתִסְגְּדוּן֮ לְצַלְמָ֣א דִֽי־עַבְדֵת֒ וְהֵן֙ לָ֣א תִסְגְּד֔וּן בַּהּ־שַׁעֲתָ֣א תִתְרְמ֔וֹן לְגֽוֹא־אַתּ֥וּן נוּרָ֖א יָקִֽדְתָּ֑א וּמַן־ה֣וּא אֱלָ֔הּ דִּ֥י יְשֵֽׁיזְבִנְכ֖וֹן מִן־יְדָֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now if you are ready when you hear the sound of the horn, pipe, lyre, trigon, harp, bagpipe, and all kinds of music, to fall down and worship the image which I have made; well and good: but if you do not worship, you shall be cast in that same hour into the midst of a burning fiery furnace; and who is the god who shall deliver you out of my hands?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Now, you will perforce [accept it upon you] that when you hear the sound of the horn, pipe, lyre, trigon, harp, bagpipe, and all kinds of music, you will fall down and worship the image which I have made. And if you do not worship, you shall be cast in that same hour into the midst of a burning fiery furnace, and who is the god that shall deliver you out of my hands?

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:16)


עֲנ֗וֹ שַׁדְרַ֤ךְ מֵישַׁךְ֙ וַעֲבֵ֣ד נְג֔וֹ וְאָמְרִ֖ין לְמַלְכָּ֑א נְבֽוּכַדְנֶצַּ֔ר לָֽא־חַשְׁחִ֨ין אֲנַ֧חְנָא עַל־דְּנָ֛ה פִּתְגָ֖ם לַהֲתָבוּתָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Shadrakh, Meshakh, and ῾Aved-nego, answered and said to the king, O Nevukhadneżżar, we have no need to answer thee in this matter.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Shadrach, Meshach, and Aved-nego answered and said to the king: O Nevuchadnezzar, we have no need to [take counsel] to answer thee in this matter.


###### Daniel 3:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:17)


הֵ֣ן אִיתַ֗י אֱלָהַ֙נָא֙ דִּֽי־אֲנַ֣חְנָא פָֽלְחִ֔ין יָכִ֖ל לְשֵׁיזָבוּתַ֑נָא מִן־אַתּ֨וּן נוּרָ֧א יָקִֽדְתָּ֛א וּמִן־יְדָ֥ךְ מַלְכָּ֖א יְשֵׁיזִֽב׃

Behold, our God whom we serve is able to deliver us; he can deliver us from the burning fiery furnace, and out of thy hand, O king.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Behold, our G-d whom we serve is able to deliver us. He can deliver us from the burning fiery furnace, and out of thy hand, O king.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:18)


וְהֵ֣ן לָ֔א יְדִ֥יעַ לֶהֱוֵא־לָ֖ךְ מַלְכָּ֑א דִּ֤י (לאלהיך) [לֵֽאלָהָךְ֙] לָא־[אִיתַ֣נָא] (איתינא) פָֽלְחִ֔ין וּלְצֶ֧לֶם דַּהֲבָ֛א דִּ֥י הֲקֵ֖ימְתָּ לָ֥א נִסְגֻּֽד׃{פ}

###### Daniel 3:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:19)


בֵּאדַ֨יִן נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּ֜ר הִתְמְלִ֣י חֱמָ֗א וּצְלֵ֤ם אַנְפּ֙וֹהִי֙ (אשתנו) [אֶשְׁתַּנִּ֔י] עַל־שַׁדְרַ֥ךְ מֵישַׁ֖ךְ וַעֲבֵ֣ד נְג֑וֹ עָנֵ֤ה וְאָמַר֙ לְמֵזֵ֣א לְאַתּוּנָ֔א חַ֨ד־שִׁבְעָ֔ה עַ֛ל דִּ֥י חֲזֵ֖ה לְמֵזְיֵֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then was Nevukhadneżżar full of fury, and the expression of his visage was changed against Shadrakh, Meshakh, and ῾Aved-nego: therefore he spoke, and commanded that they should heat the furnace seven times more than it was wont to be heated.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then was Nevuchadnezzar full of fury, and the expression of his visage was changed against Shadrach, Meshach, and Aved-nego, whereupon he spoke up and commanded that they should heat the furnace seven times more than it was wont to be heated.

וּלְגֻבְרִ֤ין גִּבָּֽרֵי־חַ֙יִל֙ דִּ֣י בְחַיְלֵ֔הּ אֲמַר֙ לְכַפָּתָ֔ה לְשַׁדְרַ֥ךְ מֵישַׁ֖ךְ וַעֲבֵ֣ד נְג֑וֹ לְמִרְמֵ֕א לְאַתּ֥וּן נוּרָ֖א יָקִֽדְתָּֽא׃

בֵּאדַ֜יִן גֻּבְרַיָּ֣א אִלֵּ֗ךְ כְּפִ֙תוּ֙ בְּסַרְבָּלֵיהוֹן֙ (פטישיהון) [פַּטְּשֵׁיה֔וֹן] וְכַרְבְּלָתְה֖וֹן וּלְבֻשֵׁיה֑וֹן וּרְמִ֕יו לְגֽוֹא־אַתּ֥וּן נוּרָ֖א יָקִֽדְתָּֽא׃

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:22)


כׇּל־קֳבֵ֣ל דְּנָ֗ה מִן־דִּ֞י מִלַּ֤ת מַלְכָּא֙ מַחְצְפָ֔ה וְאַתּוּנָ֖א אֵזֵ֣ה יַתִּ֑ירָה גֻּבְרַיָּ֣א אִלֵּ֗ךְ דִּ֤י הַסִּ֙קוּ֙ לְשַׁדְרַ֤ךְ מֵישַׁךְ֙ וַעֲבֵ֣ד נְג֔וֹ קַטִּ֣ל הִמּ֔וֹן שְׁבִיבָ֖א דִּ֥י נוּרָֽא׃

Therefore because the king’s commandment was urgent, and the furnace exceedingly hot, the flame of the fire slew those men who had seized upon Shadrakh, Meshakh, and ῾Aved-nego.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And, because the king's commandment was urgent, and the furnace exceedingly hot, the flame of the fire slew those men who had seized upon Shadrach, Meshach, and Aved-nego.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:23)


וְגֻבְרַיָּ֤א אִלֵּךְ֙ תְּלָ֣תֵּה֔וֹן שַׁדְרַ֥ךְ מֵישַׁ֖ךְ וַעֲבֵ֣ד נְג֑וֹ נְפַ֛לוּ לְגֽוֹא־אַתּוּן־נוּרָ֥א יָֽקִדְתָּ֖א מְכַפְּתִֽין׃{פ}

###### Daniel 3:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:24)


אֱדַ֙יִן֙ נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּ֣ר מַלְכָּ֔א תְּוַ֖הּ וְקָ֣ם בְּהִתְבְּהָלָ֑ה עָנֵ֨ה וְאָמַ֜ר לְהַדָּֽבְר֗וֹהִי הֲלָא֩ גֻבְרִ֨ין תְּלָתָ֜ה רְמֵ֤ינָא לְגוֹא־נוּרָא֙ מְכַפְּתִ֔ין עָנַ֤יִן וְאָמְרִין֙ לְמַלְכָּ֔א יַצִּיבָ֖א מַלְכָּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Nevukhadneżżar the king was astonished, and rose up in haste, and spoke, and said to his counsellors, Did we not cast three men bound into the midst of the fire? They answered and said to the king, True, O king.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Nevuchadnezzar the king was astonished, and rose up in haste, and spoke, and said to his counselors: Did we not cast three men bound into the midst of the fire? They answered and said to the king: True, O king.


###### Daniel 3:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:25)


עָנֵ֣ה וְאָמַ֗ר הָֽא־אֲנָ֨ה חָזֵ֜ה גֻּבְרִ֣ין אַרְבְּעָ֗ה שְׁרַ֙יִן֙ מַהְלְכִ֣ין בְּגֽוֹא־נוּרָ֔א וַחֲבָ֖ל לָא־אִיתַ֣י בְּה֑וֹן וְרֵוֵהּ֙ דִּ֣י (רביעיא) [רְֽבִיעָאָ֔ה] דָּמֵ֖ה לְבַר־אֱלָהִֽין׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He answered and said, Lo, I see four men unbound, walking in the midst of the fire, and they have no hurt; and the appearance of the fourth is like an angel.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He answered and said: Lo, I see [(from afar)] four men unbound, walking in the midst of the fire, and they have no hurt; and the appearance of the fourth is like an angel [(that I saw smiting Sancherev's host [viz. Isaiah 37:36)]


###### Daniel 3:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:26)


בֵּאדַ֜יִן קְרֵ֣ב נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּ֗ר לִתְרַע֮ אַתּ֣וּן נוּרָ֣א יָקִֽדְתָּא֒ עָנֵ֣ה וְאָמַ֗ר שַׁדְרַ֨ךְ מֵישַׁ֧ךְ וַעֲבֵד־נְג֛וֹ עַבְד֛וֹהִי דִּֽי־אֱלָהָ֥א (עליא) [עִלָּאָ֖ה] פֻּ֣קוּ וֶאֱת֑וֹ בֵּאדַ֣יִן נָֽפְקִ֗ין שַׁדְרַ֥ךְ מֵישַׁ֛ךְ וַעֲבֵ֥ד נְג֖וֹ מִן־גּ֥וֹא נוּרָֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Nevukhadneżżar came near to the mouth of the burning fiery furnace, and spoke, and said, Shadrakh, Meshakh, and ῾Aved-nego, servants of the most high God, come out, and come here. Then Shadrakh, Meshakh, and ῾Aved-nego, came out of the midst of the fire.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Nevuchadnezzar came near to the gate [of the court] of the burning fiery furnace and spoke, and said: Shadrach, Meshach, and Aved-nego, servants of the most high G-d, come out and come here. Then [(not having come out before receiving the royal bidding)], Shadrach, Meshach, and Aved-nego came out of the midst of the fire.


###### Daniel 3:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:27)


וּ֠מִֽתְכַּנְּשִׁ֠ין אֲחַשְׁדַּרְפְּנַיָּ֞א סִגְנַיָּ֣א וּפַחֲוָתָא֮ וְהַדָּבְרֵ֣י מַלְכָּא֒ חָזַ֣יִן לְגֻבְרַיָּ֣א אִלֵּ֡ךְ דִּי֩ לָֽא־שְׁלֵ֨ט נוּרָ֜א בְּגֶשְׁמְה֗וֹן וּשְׂעַ֤ר רֵֽאשְׁהוֹן֙ לָ֣א הִתְחָרַ֔ךְ וְסַרְבָּלֵיה֖וֹן לָ֣א שְׁנ֑וֹ וְרֵ֣יחַ נ֔וּר לָ֥א עֲדָ֖ת בְּהֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the satraps, the prefects, the governors, and the king’s counsellors, being gathered together, saw these men, upon whose bodies the fire had no power, nor was a hair of their head singed, nor were their mantles damaged, nor had the smell of fire passed over them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the satraps, the prefects, the governors, and the king's counselors, being gathered together [(viz. 3 above. Four are missing here — the four who were killed by the fire [viz. 22])], saw these men, upon whose bodies the fire had no power; nor was a hair of their head singed; nor were their mantles damaged; nor had the smell of the fire entered them.


###### Daniel 3:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:28)


עָנֵ֨ה נְבֽוּכַדְנֶצַּ֜ר וְאָמַ֗ר בְּרִ֤יךְ אֱלָהֲהוֹן֙ דִּֽי־שַׁדְרַ֤ךְ מֵישַׁךְ֙ וַעֲבֵ֣ד נְג֔וֹ דִּֽי־שְׁלַ֤ח מַלְאֲכֵהּ֙ וְשֵׁיזִ֣ב לְעַבְד֔וֹהִי דִּ֥י הִתְרְחִ֖צוּ עֲל֑וֹהִי וּמִלַּ֤ת מַלְכָּא֙ שַׁנִּ֔יו וִיהַ֣בוּ (גשמיהון) [גֶשְׁמְה֗וֹן] דִּ֠י לָֽא־יִפְלְח֤וּן וְלָֽא־יִסְגְּדוּן֙ לְכׇל־אֱלָ֔הּ לָהֵ֖ן לֵאלָֽהֲהֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Nevukhadneżżar spoke, and said, Blessed be the God of Shadrakh, Meshakh, and ῾Aved-nego, who has sent his angel, and delivered his servants who trusted in him, setting aside the king’s word, and yielding their bodies, that they might not serve or bow down to any god, except their own God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Nevuchadnezzar called out and said: Blessed be the G-d of Shadrach, Meshach, and Aved-nego, who has sent His angel, and delivered His servants who trusted in Him, turning aside the king's command, and yielding their bodies, that they might not serve or bow down to any god, except their own G-d.


###### Daniel 3:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:29)


וּמִנִּי֮ שִׂ֣ים טְעֵם֒ דִּי֩ כׇל־עַ֨ם אֻמָּ֜ה וְלִשָּׁ֗ן דִּֽי־יֵאמַ֤ר (שלה) [שָׁלוּ֙] עַ֣ל אֱלָהֲה֗וֹן דִּֽי־שַׁדְרַ֤ךְ מֵישַׁךְ֙ וַעֲבֵ֣ד נְג֔וֹא הַדָּמִ֣ין יִתְעֲבֵ֔ד וּבַיְתֵ֖הּ נְוָלִ֣י יִשְׁתַּוֵּ֑ה כׇּל־קֳבֵ֗ל דִּ֣י לָ֤א אִיתַי֙ אֱלָ֣הּ אׇחֳרָ֔ן דִּֽי־יִכֻּ֥ל לְהַצָּלָ֖ה כִּדְנָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore I made a decree, That every people, nation, and tongue, that speaks anything amiss against the God of Shadrakh, Meshakh, and ῾Aved-nego, shall be cut in pieces, and their houses shall be made a dunghill: because there is no other God that can deliver after this sort.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Therefore, I let it be decreed that every people, nation, and tongue that speaks anything amiss against the G-d of Shadrach, Meshach, and Aved-nego shall be cut in pieces, and their houses shall be made a dunghill; because there is no other god that can deliver after this sort.


###### Daniel 3:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:30)


בֵּאדַ֣יִן מַלְכָּ֗א הַצְלַ֛ח לְשַׁדְרַ֥ךְ מֵישַׁ֛ךְ וַעֲבֵ֥ד נְג֖וֹ בִּמְדִינַ֥ת בָּבֶֽל׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then the king promoted Shad­rakh, Meshakh, and ῾Aved-nego, in the province of Bavel.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then the king promoted Shadrach, Meshach, and Aved-nego in the province of Bavel.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:31)


נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּ֣ר מַלְכָּ֗א לְֽכׇל־עַֽמְמַיָּ֞א אֻמַּיָּ֧א וְלִשָּׁנַיָּ֛א דִּֽי־[דָיְרִ֥ין] (דארין) בְּכׇל־אַרְעָ֖א שְׁלָמְכ֥וֹן יִשְׂגֵּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Nevukhadneżżar the king, to all the peoples, nations, and tongues, that dwell in all the earth; Peace be multiplied to you.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(a letter of Nevuchadnezzar:)] Nevuchadnezzar the king, to all the peoples, nations, and tongues that dwell in all the earth: Peace be multiplied to you.


###### Daniel 3:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:32)


אָֽתַיָּא֙ וְתִמְהַיָּ֔א דִּ֚י עֲבַ֣ד עִמִּ֔י אֱלָהָ֖א (עליא) [עִלָּאָ֑ה] שְׁפַ֥ר קׇֽדָמַ֖י לְהַחֲוָיָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I thought it good to report the signs and wonders that high God has wrought towards me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/3:33)


אָת֙וֹהִי֙ כְּמָ֣ה רַבְרְבִ֔ין וְתִמְה֖וֹהִי כְּמָ֣ה תַקִּיפִ֑ין מַלְכוּתֵהּ֙ מַלְכ֣וּת עָלַ֔ם וְשׇׁלְטָנֵ֖הּ עִם־דָּ֥ר וְדָֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
How great are his signs! and how mighty are his wonders! his kingdom is an everlasting kingdom, and his dominion is from generation to generation.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
How great are His signs and how mighty are His wonders! His kingdom is an everlasting kingdom, and His dominion is from generation to generation.

אֲנָ֣ה נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּ֗ר שְׁלֵ֤ה הֲוֵית֙ בְּבֵיתִ֔י וְרַעְנַ֖ן בְּהֵיכְלִֽי׃

I Nevukhadneżżar was at rest in my house, and flourishing in my palace:

I saw a dream which made me afraid, and the thoughts upon my bed and the visions of my head troubled me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I saw a dream which made me afraid, and the thoughts upon my bed and the visions of my head troubled me.

וּמִנִּי֙ שִׂ֣ים טְעֵ֔ם לְהַנְעָלָ֣ה קׇֽדָמַ֔י לְכֹ֖ל חַכִּימֵ֣י בָבֶ֑ל דִּֽי־פְשַׁ֥ר חֶלְמָ֖א יְהֽוֹדְעֻנַּֽנִי׃

בֵּאדַ֣יִן (עללין) [עׇלִּ֗ין] חַרְטֻמַּיָּא֙ אָֽשְׁפַיָּ֔א (כשדיא) [כַּשְׂדָּאֵ֖י] וְגָזְרַיָּ֑א וְחֶלְמָ֗א אָמַ֤ר אֲנָה֙ קֳדָ֣מֵיה֔וֹן וּפִשְׁרֵ֖הּ לָא־מְהוֹדְעִ֥ין לִֽי׃

Then came in the magicians, the conjurers, the Kasdians and the astrologers, and I told the dream before them; but they could not make known to me its interpretation.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then came in the magicians, the conjurers, the Kasdians, and the astrologers, and I told the dream before them; but they could not make known to me its interpretation.

וְעַ֣ד אׇחֳרֵ֡ין עַל֩ קׇֽדָמַ֨י דָּנִיֵּ֜אל דִּֽי־שְׁמֵ֤הּ בֵּלְטְשַׁאצַּר֙ כְּשֻׁ֣ם אֱלָהִ֔י וְדִ֛י רֽוּחַ־אֱלָהִ֥ין קַדִּישִׁ֖ין בֵּ֑הּ וְחֶלְמָ֖א קׇֽדָמ֥וֹהִי אַמְרֵֽת׃

בֵּלְטְשַׁאצַּר֮ רַ֣ב חַרְטֻמַּיָּא֒ דִּ֣י**׀** אֲנָ֣ה יִדְעֵ֗ת דִּ֠י ר֣וּחַ אֱלָהִ֤ין קַדִּישִׁין֙ בָּ֔ךְ וְכׇל־רָ֖ז לָא־אָנֵ֣ס לָ֑ךְ חֶזְוֵ֨י חֶלְמִ֧י דִֽי־חֲזֵ֛ית וּפִשְׁרֵ֖הּ אֱמַֽר׃

O Belshazzar, chief of the magicians, because I know that the spirit of the holy gods is in thee, and no secret is hidden from thee, tell me the vision of my dream that I have seen, and its meaning.

עׇפְיֵ֤הּ שַׁפִּיר֙ וְאִנְבֵּ֣הּ שַׂגִּ֔יא וּמָז֨וֹן לְכֹ֖לָּא־בֵ֑הּ תְּחֹת֜וֹהִי תַּטְלֵ֣ל**׀** חֵיוַ֣ת בָּרָ֗א וּבְעַנְפ֙וֹהִי֙ (ידרון) [יְדוּרָן֙] צִפְּרֵ֣י שְׁמַיָּ֔א וּמִנֵּ֖הּ יִתְּזִ֥ין כׇּל־בִּשְׂרָֽא׃

קָרֵ֨א בְחַ֜יִל וְכֵ֣ן אָמַ֗ר גֹּ֤דּוּ אִֽילָנָא֙ וְקַצִּ֣צוּ עַנְפ֔וֹהִי אַתַּ֥רוּ עׇפְיֵ֖הּ וּבַדַּ֣רוּ אִנְבֵּ֑הּ תְּנֻ֤ד חֵֽיוְתָא֙ מִן־תַּחְתּ֔וֹהִי וְצִפְּרַיָּ֖א מִן־עַנְפֽוֹהִי׃

he cried aloud, and said thus, Hew down the tree, and cut off its branches, shake off its leaves, and scatter its fruit: let the beasts flee from under it, and the birds from its branches:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### Daniel 4:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/4:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/4:12)


בְּרַ֨ם עִקַּ֤ר שׇׁרְשׁ֙וֹהִי֙ בְּאַרְעָ֣א שְׁבֻ֔קוּ וּבֶֽאֱסוּר֙ דִּֽי־פַרְזֶ֣ל וּנְחָ֔שׁ בְּדִתְאָ֖א דִּ֣י בָרָ֑א וּבְטַ֤ל שְׁמַיָּא֙ יִצְטַבַּ֔ע וְעִם־חֵיוְתָ֥א חֲלָקֵ֖הּ בַּעֲשַׂ֥ב אַרְעָֽא׃

but leave the stump of its roots in the earth, in a band of iron and brass, amid the tender grass of the field. Let him be wet with the dew of heaven, and let his portion be with the beasts in the grass of the earth:

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/4:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/4:14)


בִּגְזֵרַ֤ת עִירִין֙ פִּתְגָמָ֔א וּמֵאמַ֥ר קַדִּישִׁ֖ין שְׁאֵֽלְתָ֑א עַד־דִּבְרַ֡ת דִּ֣י יִנְדְּע֣וּן חַ֠יַּיָּ֠א דִּֽי־שַׁלִּ֨יט (עליא) [עִלָּאָ֜ה] בְּמַלְכ֣וּת (אנושא) [אֲנָשָׁ֗א] וּלְמַן־דִּ֤י יִצְבֵּא֙ יִתְּנִנַּ֔הּ וּשְׁפַ֥ל אֲנָשִׁ֖ים יְקִ֥ים (עליה) [עֲלַֽהּ]׃

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
This matter is by the decree of the angels, and the sentence by the word of the holy ones, so that the living may know that the most High rules in the kingdom of men, and gives it to whomever He will, and sets up over it the basest of men.


###### Daniel 4:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/4:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/4:15)


דְּנָה֙ חֶלְמָ֣א חֲזֵ֔ית אֲנָ֖ה מַלְכָּ֣א נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּ֑ר (ואנתה) [וְאַ֨נְתְּ] בֵּלְטְשַׁאצַּ֜ר פִּשְׁרֵ֣א**׀** אֱמַ֗ר כׇּל־קֳבֵל֙ דִּ֣י**׀** כׇּל־חַכִּימֵ֣י מַלְכוּתִ֗י לָֽא־יָכְלִ֤ין פִּשְׁרָא֙ לְהוֹדָ֣עוּתַ֔נִי (ואנתה) [וְאַ֣נְתְּ] כָּהֵ֔ל דִּ֛י רֽוּחַ־אֱלָהִ֥ין קַדִּישִׁ֖ין בָּֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
This dream I, king Nevukhadneżżar, have seen. Now thou, O Belteshażżar, declare its interpretation, seeing that all the wise men of my kingdom are not able to make known to me the interpretation: but thou art able; for the spirit of the holy gods is in thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
This dream, I, King Nevuchadnezzar, have seen. Now, thou, O Belshazzar, declare its interpretation, seeing that all the wise men of my kingdom are not able to make known to me the interpretation; but thou art able, for the spirit of the holy gods is in thee.


###### Daniel 4:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/4:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/4:16)


אֱדַ֨יִן דָּֽנִיֵּ֜אל דִּֽי־שְׁמֵ֣הּ בֵּלְטְשַׁאצַּ֗ר אֶשְׁתּוֹמַם֙ כְּשָׁעָ֣ה חֲדָ֔ה וְרַעְיֹנֹ֖הִי יְבַהֲלֻנֵּ֑הּ עָנֵ֨ה מַלְכָּ֜א וְאָמַ֗ר בֵּלְטְשַׁאצַּר֙ חֶלְמָ֤א וּפִשְׁרֵא֙ אַֽל־יְבַהֲלָ֔ךְ עָנֵ֤ה בֵלְטְשַׁאצַּר֙ וְאָמַ֔ר מָרִ֕אי חֶלְמָ֥א (לשנאיך) [לְשָֽׂנְאָ֖ךְ] וּפִשְׁרֵ֥הּ (לעריך) [לְעָרָֽךְ]׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Daniyyel, whose name was Belteshażżar, was appalled for one hour, and his thoughts terrified him. The king spoke, and said, Belteshażżar, let not the dream, or its interpretation terrify thee. Belteshażżar answered and said, My lord, the dream be for those who hate thee, and its interpretation for thy enemies.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Daniel, whose name was Belshazzar, was silent for one hour, and his thoughts [i.e., fear of revealing the interpretation] confounded him. The king spoke and said: Belshazzar, let not the dream and its interpretation confound thee. Belshazzar answered and said: My lord, the dream be for those who hate thee, and its interpretation for they enemies.

The tree that thou sawest, which grew and was strong, whose top reached to the sky, and which was visible to all the earth,

וְעׇפְיֵ֤הּ שַׁפִּיר֙ וְאִנְבֵּ֣הּ שַׂגִּ֔יא וּמָז֨וֹן לְכֹ֖לָּא־בֵ֑הּ תְּחֹת֗וֹהִי תְּדוּר֙ חֵיוַ֣ת בָּרָ֔א וּבְעַנְפ֕וֹהִי יִשְׁכְּנָ֖ן צִפְּרֵ֥י שְׁמַיָּֽא׃

the leaves of which were fair, and the fruit of which abundant, and on it was food for all; under which the beasts of the field dwelt, and upon the branches of which the birds of the sky had their habitation:

the boughs of which were fair, and the fruit of which, abundant, and on it was food for all; under which the beasts of the field dwelt, and upon the boughs of which the birds of the sky had their habitation,

(אנתה) [אַנְתְּ־]ה֣וּא מַלְכָּ֔א דִּ֥י (רבית) [רְבַ֖ת] וּתְקֵ֑פְתְּ וּרְבוּתָ֤ךְ רְבָת֙ וּמְטָ֣ת לִשְׁמַיָּ֔א וְשׇׁלְטָנָ֖ךְ לְס֥וֹף אַרְעָֽא׃

###### Daniel 4:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/4:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/4:20)


וְדִ֣י חֲזָ֣ה מַלְכָּ֡א עִ֣יר וְקַדִּ֣ישׁ נָחִ֣ת׀מִן־שְׁמַיָּ֡א וְאָמַר֩ גֹּ֨דּוּ אִֽילָנָ֜א וְחַבְּל֗וּהִי בְּרַ֨ם עִקַּ֤ר שׇׁרְשׁ֙וֹהִי֙ בְּאַרְעָ֣א שְׁבֻ֔קוּ וּבֶאֱסוּר֙ דִּֽי־פַרְזֶ֣ל וּנְחָ֔שׁ בְּדִתְאָ֖א דִּ֣י בָרָ֑א וּבְטַ֧ל שְׁמַיָּ֣א יִצְטַבַּ֗ע וְעִם־חֵיוַ֤ת בָּרָא֙ חֲלָקֵ֔הּ עַ֛ד דִּֽי־שִׁבְעָ֥ה עִדָּנִ֖ין יַחְלְפ֥וּן עֲלֽוֹהִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And whereas the king saw a watcher and a holy one coming down from heaven, and saying, Hew down the tree, and destroy it; yet leave the stump of its roots in the earth, in a band of iron and brass, amid the tender grass of the field; and let him be wet with the dew of heaven, and let his portion be with the beasts of the field, till seven seasons pass over him;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And whereas the king saw a waker and a holy one coming down from heaven and saying: Hew down the tree and destroy it; yet leave the stump of its roots in the earth, in a band of iron and brass, amid the tender grass of the field; and let it be steeped in the dew of heaven, and let its portion be with the beasts of the field, until seven years pass over it,

this is the interpretation, O king, and this is the decree of the Most High, which is come upon my lord the king:

###### Daniel 4:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/4:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/4:22)


וְלָ֣ךְ טָֽרְדִ֣ין מִן־אֲנָשָׁ֡א וְעִם־חֵיוַ֣ת בָּרָא֩ לֶהֱוֵ֨ה מְדֹרָ֜ךְ וְעִשְׂבָּ֥א כְתוֹרִ֣ין**׀** לָ֣ךְ יְטַֽעֲמ֗וּן וּמִטַּ֤ל שְׁמַיָּא֙ לָ֣ךְ מְצַבְּעִ֔ין וְשִׁבְעָ֥ה עִדָּנִ֖ין יַחְלְפ֣וּן (עליך) [עֲלָ֑ךְ] עַ֣ד דִּֽי־תִנְדַּ֗ע דִּֽי־שַׁלִּ֤יט (עליא) [עִלָּאָה֙] בְּמַלְכ֣וּת אֲנָשָׁ֔א וּלְמַן־דִּ֥י יִצְבֵּ֖א יִתְּנִנַּֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
That thou shalt be driven from men, and thy dwelling shall be with the beasts of the field, and thou shalt be made to eat grass like oxen, and thou shalt be wet with the dew of heaven, and seven seasons shall pass over thee, in order that thou mayest know that the Most High rules in the kingdom of men, and gives it to whomever he pleases.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
That thou shalt be driven from men, and thy dwelling shall be with the beasts of the field, and thou shalt be made to eat grass like oxen, and thou shalt be steeped in the dew of heaven, and seven years shall pass over thee [(retribution for his destruction of the Temple, which took seven years to build)],in order that thou mayest know that the most High rules in the kingdom of men, and gives it to whomever He pleases.

Therefore, O king, let my counsel be acceptable to thee, and break off [(from your neck)] thy sins by charity, and thy iniquities by showing mercy to the poor, that there may be an extension of thy tranquility [(and the evil not hasten to come)].
