###### Daniel 4:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/4:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/4:31)


וְלִקְצָ֣ת יֽוֹמַיָּא֩ אֲנָ֨ה נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּ֜ר עַיְנַ֣י**׀** לִשְׁמַיָּ֣א נִטְלֵ֗ת וּמַנְדְּעִי֙ עֲלַ֣י יְת֔וּב (ולעליא) [וּלְעִלָּאָה֙] בָּרְכֵ֔ת וּלְחַ֥י עָלְמָ֖א שַׁבְּחֵ֣ת וְהַדְּרֵ֑ת דִּ֤י שׇׁלְטָנֵהּ֙ שׇׁלְטָ֣ן עָלַ֔ם וּמַלְכוּתֵ֖הּ עִם־דָּ֥ר וְדָֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And at the end of the days I Nevukhadneżżar lifted up my eyes to heaven, and my understanding returned to me, and I blessed the Most High, and I praised and honoured him who lives for ever, whose dominion is an everlasting dominion, and his kingdom is from generation to generation:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
At the end of the days [i.e., the seven years], I, Nevuchadnezzar, lifted up my eyes to heaven, and my understanding returned to me, and I blessed the most High, and I praised and honored Him who lives for ever, whose dominion is an everlasting dominion, and His kingdom is from generation to generation.


###### Daniel 4:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/4:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/4:32)


וְכׇל־[דָּיְרֵ֤י] (דארי) אַרְעָא֙ כְּלָ֣ה חֲשִׁיבִ֔ין וּֽכְמִצְבְּיֵ֗הּ עָבֵד֙ בְּחֵ֣יל שְׁמַיָּ֔א (ודארי) [וְדָיְרֵ֖י] אַרְעָ֑א וְלָ֤א אִיתַי֙ דִּֽי־יְמַחֵ֣א בִידֵ֔הּ וְיֵ֥אמַר לֵ֖הּ מָ֥ה עֲבַֽדְתְּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and all the inhabitants of the earth are reputed as nothing: and he does according to his will in the host of heaven, and among the inhabitants of the earth: and none can stay his hand, or say to him, What doest Thou?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all the inhabitants of the earth are reputed as naught and He does according to His will in the host of heaven and among the inhabitants of the earth. And none can stay His hand or say to Him: What doest thou?


###### Daniel 4:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/4:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/4:33)


בֵּהּ־זִמְנָ֞א מַנְדְּעִ֣י**׀** יְת֣וּב עֲלַ֗י וְלִיקַ֨ר מַלְכוּתִ֜י הַדְרִ֤י וְזִיוִי֙ יְת֣וּב עֲלַ֔י וְלִ֕י הַדָּֽבְרַ֥י וְרַבְרְבָנַ֖י יְבַע֑וֹן וְעַל־מַלְכוּתִ֣י הׇתְקְנַ֔ת וּרְב֥וּ יַתִּירָ֖ה ה֥וּסְפַת לִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
At the same time my reason returned to me; and for the glory of my kingdom, my majesty and splendour returned to me; and my counsellors and my lords sought to me; and I was established in my kingdom, and still more greatness was added to me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
At that time, my reason returned to me, and I returned to the glory of my kingdom. And my majesty and splendor returned to me; and my counselors and lords sought me, and I was established in my kingdom. And still more greatness was added to me.


###### Daniel 4:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/4:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/4:34)


כְּעַ֞ן אֲנָ֣ה נְבֻכַדְנֶצַּ֗ר מְשַׁבַּ֨ח וּמְרוֹמֵ֤ם וּמְהַדַּר֙ לְמֶ֣לֶךְ שְׁמַיָּ֔א דִּ֤י כׇל־מַעֲבָד֙וֹהִי֙ קְשֹׁ֔ט וְאֹרְחָתֵ֖הּ דִּ֑ין וְדִי֙ מַהְלְכִ֣ין בְּגֵוָ֔ה יָכִ֖ל לְהַשְׁפָּלָֽה׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now I Nevukhadneżżar praise and extol and honour the King of heaven, all whose works are truth, and his ways justice: and he is able to abase those who walk in pride.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Now I, Nevuchadnezzar, praise and extol and honor the King of heaven, all whose works are truth, and His ways justice; and He is able to abase those who walk in pride.



## Chapter 5

###### Daniel 5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:1)


בֵּלְשַׁאצַּ֣ר מַלְכָּ֗א עֲבַד֙ לְחֶ֣ם רַ֔ב לְרַבְרְבָנ֖וֹהִי אֲלַ֑ף וְלׇקֳבֵ֥ל אַלְפָּ֖א חַמְרָ֥א שָׁתֵֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Belshażżar the king made a great feast for a thousand of his lords, and drank wine before the thousand.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Belshazzar the king [(Nevuchadnezzar's son)] made a great feast for a thousand of his lords, and drank wine before the thousand.


###### Daniel 5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:2)


בֵּלְשַׁאצַּ֞ר אֲמַ֣ר**׀** בִּטְעֵ֣ם חַמְרָ֗א לְהַיְתָיָה֙ לְמָאנֵי֙ דַּהֲבָ֣א וְכַסְפָּ֔א דִּ֤י הַנְפֵּק֙ נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּ֣ר אֲב֔וּהִי מִן־הֵיכְלָ֖א דִּ֣י בִירוּשְׁלֶ֑ם וְיִשְׁתּ֣וֹן בְּה֗וֹן מַלְכָּא֙ וְרַבְרְבָנ֔וֹהִי שֵׁגְלָתֵ֖הּ וּלְחֵנָתֵֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Belshażżar, while he tasted the wine, commanded to bring the golden and silver vessels which his father Nevukhadneżżar had taken out of the temple which was in Yerushalayim; that the king, and his lords, his wives, and his concubines, might drink from them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Belshazzar, in his cups, commanded to bring the golden and silver vessels which his father Nevuchadnezzar had taken out of the Temple which was in Jerusalem; that the king and his lords, his queen and his concubines might drink from them.


###### Daniel 5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:3)


בֵּאדַ֗יִן הַיְתִיו֙ מָאנֵ֣י דַהֲבָ֔א דִּ֣י הַנְפִּ֗קוּ מִן־הֵ֥יכְלָ֛א דִּֽי־בֵ֥ית אֱלָהָ֖א דִּ֣י בִירֽוּשְׁלֶ֑ם וְאִשְׁתִּ֣יו בְּה֗וֹן מַלְכָּא֙ וְרַבְרְבָנ֔וֹהִי שֵׁגְלָתֵ֖הּ וּלְחֵנָתֵֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then they brought the golden vessels that were taken out of the temple of the house of God which was at Yerushalayim; and the king, and his lords, his wives, and his concubines, drank from them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then were brought the golden vessels that they had taken out of the Temple of the house of G-d, which was at Jerusalem; and the king and his lords, his queen and his concubines, drank from them.


###### Daniel 5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:4)


אִשְׁתִּ֖יו חַמְרָ֑א וְ֠שַׁבַּ֠חוּ לֵֽאלָהֵ֞י דַּהֲבָ֧א וְכַסְפָּ֛א נְחָשָׁ֥א פַרְזְלָ֖א אָעָ֥א וְאַבְנָֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They drank wine, and praised the gods of gold, and of silver, of brass, of iron, of wood, and of stone.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They drank wine and praised the gods of gold and of silver, of brass, of iron, of wood, and of stone.


###### Daniel 5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:5)


בַּהּ־שַׁעֲתָ֗ה (נפקו) [נְפַ֙קָה֙] אֶצְבְּעָן֙ דִּ֣י יַד־אֱנָ֔שׁ וְכָֽתְבָן֙ לׇקֳבֵ֣ל נֶבְרַשְׁתָּ֔א עַל־גִּירָ֕א דִּֽי־כְתַ֥ל הֵיכְלָ֖א דִּ֣י מַלְכָּ֑א וּמַלְכָּ֣א חָזֵ֔ה פַּ֥ס יְדָ֖א דִּ֥י כָתְבָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In the same hour the fingers of a man’s hand appeared, and wrote over against the lampstand upon the plaster of the wall of the king’s palace: and the king saw the part of the hand that wrote.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the same hour, the fingers of a man's hand went forth [from heaven], and wrote over against the lampstand upon the plaster of the wall of the king's palace; and the king saw the part of the hand that wrote.


###### Daniel 5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:6)


אֱדַ֤יִן מַלְכָּא֙ זִיוֺ֣הִי שְׁנ֔וֹהִי וְרַעְיֹנֹ֖הִי יְבַהֲלוּנֵּ֑הּ וְקִטְרֵ֤י חַרְצֵהּ֙ מִשְׁתָּרַ֔יִן וְאַ֨רְכֻבָּתֵ֔הּ דָּ֥א לְדָ֖א נָֽקְשָֽׁן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then the king’s colour changed, and his thoughts terrified him, so that the joints of his loins were loosed, and his knees smote one against the other.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then the king's appearance changed, and his thoughts terrified him, so that the belt of his loins was loosed [(his bowels being emptied in his fear)], and his knees smote one against the other.


###### Daniel 5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:7)


קָרֵ֤א מַלְכָּא֙ בְּחַ֔יִל לְהֶֽעָלָה֙ לְאָ֣שְׁפַיָּ֔א (כשדיא) [כַּשְׂדָּאֵ֖י] וְגָזְרַיָּ֑א עָנֵ֨ה מַלְכָּ֜א וְאָמַ֣ר**׀** לְחַכִּימֵ֣י בָבֶ֗ל דִּ֣י כׇל־אֱ֠נָ֠שׁ דִּֽי־יִקְרֵ֞ה כְּתָבָ֣ה דְנָ֗ה וּפִשְׁרֵהּ֙ יְחַוִּנַּ֔נִי אַרְגְּוָנָ֣א יִלְבַּ֗שׁ (והמנוכא) [וְהַֽמְנִיכָ֤א] דִֽי־דַהֲבָא֙ עַֽל־צַוְּארֵ֔הּ וְתַלְתִּ֥י בְמַלְכוּתָ֖א יִשְׁלַֽט׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The king cried aloud to bring in the conjurers, the Kasdians, and the astrologers. And the king spoke, and said to the wise men of Bavel, Whoever shall read this writing, and declare to me the meaning of it, shall be clothed with scarlet, and have a chain of gold about his neck, and shall rule as the third in the kingdom.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The king cried aloud to bring in the astrologers, the Kasdim, and the snake charmers. And the king spoke and said to the wise men of Bavel: Whoever shall read this writing and declare to me the meaning of it shall be clothed with scarlet and have a chain of gold about his neck, and shall rule over a third of the kingdom.


###### Daniel 5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:8)


אֱדַ֙יִן֙ (עללין) [עָֽלִּ֔ין] כֹּ֖ל חַכִּימֵ֣י מַלְכָּ֑א וְלָֽא־כָהֲלִ֤ין כְּתָבָא֙ לְמִקְרֵ֔א (ופשרא) [וּפִשְׁרֵ֖הּ] לְהוֹדָעָ֥ה לְמַלְכָּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then all the king’s wise men came in, but they could not read the writing, nor make known to the king the meaning of it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then all the king's wise men came in, but they could not read the writing, nor make known to the king the meaning of it.


###### Daniel 5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:9)


אֱ֠דַ֠יִן מַלְכָּ֤א בֵלְשַׁאצַּר֙ שַׂגִּ֣יא מִתְבָּהַ֔ל וְזִיוֺ֖הִי שָׁנַ֣יִן עֲל֑וֹהִי וְרַבְרְבָנ֖וֹהִי מִֽשְׁתַּבְּשִֽׁין׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then king Belshażżar was greatly alarmed, and his colour changed, and his lords were perplexed.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then King Belshazzar was greatly alarmed, and his appearance changed, and his lords were confounded.


###### Daniel 5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:10)


מַלְכְּתָ֕א לׇקֳבֵ֨ל מִלֵּ֤י מַלְכָּא֙ וְרַבְרְבָנ֔וֹהִי לְבֵ֥ית מִשְׁתְּיָ֖א (עללת) [עַלַּ֑ת] עֲנָ֨ת מַלְכְּתָ֜א וַאֲמֶ֗רֶת מַלְכָּא֙ לְעָלְמִ֣ין חֱיִ֔י אַֽל־יְבַהֲלוּךְ֙ רַעְיוֹנָ֔ךְ (וזיויך) [וְזִיוָ֖ךְ] אַל־יִשְׁתַּנּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now the queen, by reason of the words of the king and his lords, came into the banquet house: the queen spoke and said, O king, live for ever: let not thy thoughts affright thee, nor let thy colour change:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Now the queen, having heard the words of the king and his lords, came into the banquet house. The queen spoke and said: O king, live for ever. Let not thy thoughts affright thee, nor let thy appearance change.


###### Daniel 5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:11)


אִיתַ֨י גְּבַ֜ר בְּמַלְכוּתָ֗ךְ דִּ֠י ר֣וּחַ אֱלָהִ֣ין קַדִּישִׁין֮ בֵּהּ֒ וּבְיוֹמֵ֣י אֲב֗וּךְ נַהִיר֧וּ וְשׇׂכְלְתָנ֛וּ וְחׇכְמָ֥ה כְּחׇכְמַת־אֱלָהִ֖ין הִשְׁתְּכַ֣חַת בֵּ֑הּ וּמַלְכָּ֤א נְבֻֽכַדְנֶצַּר֙ אֲב֔וּךְ רַ֧ב חַרְטֻמִּ֣ין אָֽשְׁפִ֗ין כַּשְׂדָּאִין֙ גָּזְרִ֔ין הֲקִימֵ֖הּ אֲב֥וּךְ מַלְכָּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
there is a man in thy kingdom, in whom is the spirit of the holy gods; and in the days of thy father, light and understanding and wisdom, like the wisdom of the gods, was found in him; whom the king Nevukhadneżżar thy father, the king, I say, thy father, made chief of the magicians, conjurers, Kasdians, and astrologers;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There is a man in thy kingdom, in whom is the spirit of the holy gods, and in the days of thy father, light and understanding and wisdom, like the wisdom of the angels was found in him, whom the king Nevuchadnezzar thy father, the king, I say, thy father, made chief of the bone-conjurers, astrologers, Kasdim, and snake charmers,


###### Daniel 5:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:12)


כׇּל־קֳבֵ֡ל דִּ֣י ר֣וּחַ׀יַתִּירָ֡ה וּמַנְדַּ֡ע וְשׇׂכְלְתָנ֡וּ מְפַשַּׁ֣ר חֶלְמִין֩ וְֽאַֽחֲוָיַ֨ת אֲחִידָ֜ן וּמְשָׁרֵ֣א קִטְרִ֗ין הִשְׁתְּכַ֤חַת בֵּהּ֙ בְּדָ֣נִיֵּ֔אל דִּֽי־מַלְכָּ֥א שָׂם־שְׁמֵ֖הּ בֵּלְטְשַׁאצַּ֑ר כְּעַ֛ן דָּנִיֵּ֥אל יִתְקְרֵ֖י וּפִשְׁרָ֥ה יְהַֽחֲוֵֽה׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
seeing that an excellent spirit, and knowledge, and understanding to interpret dreams, explain riddles, and solve problems, were found in the same Daniyyel, whom the king named Belteshażżar: now let Daniyyel be called, and he will declare the interpretation.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
seeing that a superflux of spirit, and knowledge, and understanding to interpret dreams, explain riddles, and unravel the hidden, were found in the same Daniel, whom the king named Belshazzar. Now let Daniel be called, and he will declare the interpretation.


###### Daniel 5:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:13)


בֵּאדַ֙יִן֙ דָּֽנִיֵּ֔אל הֻעַ֖ל קֳדָ֣ם מַלְכָּ֑א עָנֵ֨ה מַלְכָּ֜א וְאָמַ֣ר לְדָנִיֵּ֗אל (אנתה) [אַנְתְּ־]ה֤וּא דָנִיֵּאל֙ דִּֽי־מִן־בְּנֵ֤י גָלוּתָא֙ דִּ֣י יְה֔וּד דִּ֥י הַיְתִ֛י מַלְכָּ֥א אַ֖בִי מִן־יְהֽוּד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then was Daniyyel brought in before the king. The king spoke and said to Daniyyel. Art thou that Daniyyel who art of the children of the exiles of Yehuda, whom the king my father brought out of Yehuda?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then was Daniel brought in before the king. The king spoke and said to Daniel: Art thou that Daniel who art of the children of the exiles of Judah, whom the king my father brought out of Judah?


###### Daniel 5:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:14)


וְשִׁמְעֵ֣ת (עליך) [עֲלָ֔ךְ] דִּ֛י ר֥וּחַ אֱלָהִ֖ין בָּ֑ךְ וְנַהִיר֧וּ וְשׇׂכְלְתָנ֛וּ וְחׇכְמָ֥ה יַתִּירָ֖ה הִשְׁתְּכַ֥חַת בָּֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I have heard of thee, that the spirit of the gods is in thee, and that light and understanding and excellent wisdom is found in thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I have heard of thee, that the spirit of the gods is in thee, and that light and understanding and excellent wisdom is found in thee.


###### Daniel 5:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/5:15)


וּכְעַ֞ן הֻעַ֣לּוּ קׇֽדָמַ֗י חַכִּֽימַיָּא֙ אָֽשְׁפַיָּ֔א דִּֽי־כְתָבָ֤ה דְנָה֙ יִקְר֔וֹן וּפִשְׁרֵ֖הּ לְהוֹדָעֻתַ֑נִי וְלָֽא־כָהֲלִ֥ין פְּשַֽׁר־מִלְּתָ֖א לְהַחֲוָיָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And now the wise men, the conjurers, have been brought in before me, that they should read this writing, and make known to me its meaning: but they could not declare the interpretation of the thing:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, the wise men, the astrologers, have been brought in before me, that they should read this writing, and make known to me its meaning; but they could not declare the interpretation of the thing.

וַאֲנָה֙ שִׁמְעֵ֣ת (עליך) [עֲלָ֔ךְ] דִּֽי־[תִכּ֥וּל] (תוכל) פִּשְׁרִ֛ין לְמִפְשַׁ֖ר וְקִטְרִ֣ין לְמִשְׁרֵ֑א כְּעַ֡ן הֵן֩ (תוכל) [תִּכּ֨וּל] כְּתָבָ֜א לְמִקְרֵ֗א וּפִשְׁרֵהּ֙ לְהוֹדָ֣עוּתַ֔נִי אַרְגְּוָנָ֣א תִלְבַּ֗שׁ (והמונכא) [וְהַֽמְנִיכָ֤א] דִֽי־דַהֲבָא֙ עַֽל־צַוְּארָ֔ךְ וְתַלְתָּ֥א בְמַלְכוּתָ֖א תִּשְׁלַֽט׃{פ}

and I have heard of thee, that thou canst give interpretations, and solve problems: now if thou canst read the writing, and make known to me the interpretation of it, thou shalt be clothed with scarlet, and have a chain of gold about thy neck, and shalt rule as third in the kingdom.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

and he was driven from the sons of men; and his heart was made like the beasts, and his dwelling was with the wild asses: he was fed with grass like oxen, and his body was wet with the dew of heaven; until he came to know that the most high God rules in the kingdom of men, and that he appoints over it whomever he pleases.

He was fed with grass like oxen, and his body was steeped in the dew of heaven; until he came to know that the most high G-d rules in the kingdom of men, and that He appoints over it whomever He pleases.

but thou hast lifted up thyself against the L-rd of heaven; and they have brought the vessels of His house before thee, and thou, and thy lords, thy queen, and thy concubines have drunk wine in them; and thou hast praised the gods of silver, and gold, of brass, iron, wood, and stone, which do not see, hear, or know. And the G-d in whose hand thy breath is, and whose are all thy ways, thou hast not glorified.

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

and over these were three presidents; of whom Daniyyel was one: that these satraps might give account to them, so that the king should have no loss.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And over these were three presidents, of whom Daniel was one; that these satraps might give account to them, so that the king should have no loss.


###### Daniel 6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/6:4)


אֱדַ֙יִן֙ דָּנִיֵּ֣אל דְּנָ֔ה הֲוָ֣א מִתְנַצַּ֔ח עַל־סָרְכַיָּ֖א וַאֲחַשְׁדַּרְפְּנַיָּ֑א כׇּל־קֳבֵ֗ל דִּ֣י ר֤וּחַ יַתִּירָא֙ בֵּ֔הּ וּמַלְכָּ֣א עֲשִׁ֔ית לַהֲקָמוּתֵ֖הּ עַל־כׇּל־מַלְכוּתָֽא׃

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### Daniel 6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/6:5)


אֱדַ֨יִן סָֽרְכַיָּ֜א וַאֲחַשְׁדַּרְפְּנַיָּ֗א הֲו֨וֹ בָעַ֧יִן עִלָּ֛ה לְהַשְׁכָּחָ֥ה לְדָנִיֵּ֖אל מִצַּ֣ד מַלְכוּתָ֑א וְכׇל־עִלָּ֨ה וּשְׁחִיתָ֜ה לָא־יָכְלִ֣ין לְהַשְׁכָּחָ֗ה כׇּל־קֳבֵל֙ דִּֽי־מְהֵימַ֣ן ה֔וּא וְכׇל־שָׁלוּ֙ וּשְׁחִיתָ֔ה לָ֥א הִשְׁתְּכַ֖חַת עֲלֽוֹהִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then the presidents and satraps sought to find a pretext against Daniyyel concerning the kingdom; but they could find no pretext or fault; seeing that he was faithful, nor was any error or fault to be found in him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then the presidents and satraps sought to find a pretext against Daniel concerning the kingdom; but they could find no pretext or fault, seeing that he was faithful; nor was any error or fault to be found in him.

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
All the presidents of the kingdom, the prefects, and the satraps, the counsellors, and the governors, have consulted together to establish a royal statute, and to make a firm decree, that whoever makes petition of any God or man for thirty days, save of thee, O king, he shall be cast into the lion’s den.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
All the presidents of the kingdom, the prefects, and the satraps, the counselors and the governors, have consulted together to strengthen the king's ordinance [(since you are newly crowned and from a different land)], and to make a firm decree, that whoever makes petition of any god or man for thirty days, save of thee, O king, he shall be cast into the lions' den.

###### Daniel 6:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/6:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/6:24)


בֵּאדַ֣יִן מַלְכָּ֗א שַׂגִּיא֙ טְאֵ֣ב עֲל֔וֹהִי וּלְדָ֣נִיֵּ֔אל אֲמַ֖ר לְהַנְסָקָ֣ה מִן־גֻּבָּ֑א וְהֻסַּ֨ק דָּנִיֵּ֜אל מִן־גֻּבָּ֗א וְכׇל־חֲבָל֙ לָא־הִשְׁתְּכַ֣ח בֵּ֔הּ דִּ֖י הֵימִ֥ן בֵּאלָהֵֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then the king was exceedingly glad for him, and commanded that they should take Daniyyel up out of the den. So Daniyyel was taken up out of the den, and no manner of hurt was found upon him, because he had trusted in his God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then the king was exceedingly glad, and commanded that they should take Daniel up out of the den; and no manner of hurt was found upon him, because he had trusted in his G-d.


###### Daniel 6:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/6:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/6:25)


וַאֲמַ֣ר מַלְכָּ֗א וְהַיְתִ֞יו גֻּבְרַיָּ֤א אִלֵּךְ֙ דִּֽי־אֲכַ֤לוּ קַרְצ֙וֹהִי֙ דִּ֣י דָֽנִיֵּ֔אל וּלְג֤וֹב אַרְיָוָתָא֙ רְמ֔וֹ אִנּ֖וּן בְּנֵיה֣וֹן וּנְשֵׁיה֑וֹן וְלָֽא־מְט֞וֹ לְאַרְעִ֣ית גֻּבָּ֗א עַ֠ד דִּֽי־שְׁלִ֤טֽוּ בְהוֹן֙ אַרְיָ֣וָתָ֔א וְכׇל־גַּרְמֵיה֖וֹן הַדִּֽקוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the king commanded, and they brought those men who had accused Daniyyel, and they cast them into the lions’ den, them, their children, and their wives; and the lions had the mastery of them, and broke all their bones in pieces before they reached the bottom of the den.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king commanded, and they brought those men who had slandered Daniel, and they cast them into the lions' den; them, their children, and their wives; and the lions overmastered them, and broke all their bones in pieces before they reached the bottom of the den.


###### Daniel 6:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/6:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/6:26)


בֵּאדַ֜יִן דָּרְיָ֣וֶשׁ מַלְכָּ֗א כְּ֠תַ֠ב לְֽכׇל־עַֽמְמַיָּ֞א אֻמַּיָּ֧א וְלִשָּׁנַיָּ֛א דִּֽי־[דָיְרִ֥ין] (דארין) בְּכׇל־אַרְעָ֖א שְׁלָמְכ֥וֹן יִשְׂגֵּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then king Daryavesh wrote to all the peoples, nations, and tongues, that dwell in all the earth; Peace be multiplied to you.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then King Darius wrote to all the peoples, nations, and tongues, that dwell in all the earth: May your peace increase!


###### Daniel 6:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/6:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/6:27)


מִן־קֳדָמַי֮ שִׂ֣ים טְעֵם֒ דִּ֣י**׀** בְּכׇל־שׇׁלְטָ֣ן מַלְכוּתִ֗י לֶהֱוֺ֤ן (זאעין) [זָיְעִין֙] וְדָ֣חֲלִ֔ין מִן־קֳדָ֖ם אֱלָהֵ֣הּ דִּי־דָֽנִיֵּ֑אל דִּי־ה֣וּא**׀** אֱלָהָ֣א חַיָּ֗א וְקַיָּם֙ לְעָ֣לְמִ֔ין וּמַלְכוּתֵהּ֙ דִּֽי־לָ֣א תִתְחַבַּ֔ל וְשׇׁלְטָנֵ֖הּ עַד־סוֹפָֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I make a decree, That in every dominion of my kingdom men tremble and fear before the God of Daniyyel: for he is the living God, and steadfast forever, and his kingdom is one which shall not be destroyed, and his dominion shall be even to the end.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I make a decree that in every dominion of my kingdom men tremble and fear before the G-d of Daniel; for He is the living G-d, and steadfast forever, and His kingdom is one which shall not be destroyed, and His dominion [shall endure] to the end [of time].


###### Daniel 6:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/6:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/6:28)


מְשֵׁיזִ֣ב וּמַצִּ֔ל וְעָבֵד֙ אָתִ֣ין וְתִמְהִ֔ין בִּשְׁמַיָּ֖א וּבְאַרְעָ֑א דִּ֚י שֵׁיזִ֣ב לְדָֽנִיֵּ֔אל מִן־יַ֖ד אַרְיָוָתָֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He delivers and rescues, and he works signs and wonders in heaven and in earth, who has delivered Daniyyel from the power of the lions.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He delivers, and rescues, and He works signs and wonders in heaven and on earth, who has delivered Daniel from the power of the lions.


###### Daniel 6:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/6:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/6:29)


וְדָנִיֵּ֣אל דְּנָ֔ה הַצְלַ֖ח בְּמַלְכ֣וּת דָּרְיָ֑וֶשׁ וּבְמַלְכ֖וּת כּ֥וֹרֶשׁ (פרסיא) [פָּרְסָאָֽה]׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So this Daniyyel prospered in the reign of Daryavesh, and in the reign of Koresh the Parsian.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
So this Daniel prospered in the [(one-year)] reign of Darius and in the reign of Koresh the Parsaian [(who followed him)].



## Chapter 7

###### Daniel 7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:1)


בִּשְׁנַ֣ת חֲדָ֗ה לְבֵלְאשַׁצַּר֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ בָּבֶ֔ל דָּנִיֵּאל֙ חֵ֣לֶם חֲזָ֔ה וְחֶזְוֵ֥י רֵאשֵׁ֖הּ עַֽל־מִשְׁכְּבֵ֑הּ בֵּאדַ֙יִן֙ חֶלְמָ֣א כְתַ֔ב רֵ֥אשׁ מִלִּ֖ין אֲמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In the first year of Belshażżar king of Bavel, Daniyyel had a dream and visions of his head as he lay upon his bed: then he wrote the dream, and told the sum of the matter.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the first year of Belshazzar king of Bavel, Daniel had a dream and visions of his head as he lay upon his bed; then he wrote the dream, and told the sum of the matter.


###### Daniel 7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:2)


עָנֵ֤ה דָנִיֵּאל֙ וְאָמַ֔ר חָזֵ֥ה הֲוֵ֛ית בְּחֶזְוִ֖י עִם־לֵֽילְיָ֑א וַאֲר֗וּ אַרְבַּע֙ רוּחֵ֣י שְׁמַיָּ֔א מְגִיחָ֖ן לְיַמָּ֥א רַבָּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Daniyyel spoke and said, I saw in my vision by night, and, behold, the four winds of the heaven stirred up the great sea.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Daniel spoke and said: I saw in my vision by night, and behold, the four winds of the heaven stirred up the great sea.


###### Daniel 7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:3)


וְאַרְבַּ֤ע חֵיוָן֙ רַבְרְבָ֔ן סָלְקָ֖ן מִן־יַמָּ֑א שָׁנְיָ֖ן דָּ֥א מִן־דָּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And four great beasts came up from the sea, diverse from one another.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And four great beasts came up from the sea, diverse from one another.


###### Daniel 7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:4)


קַדְמָיְתָ֣א כְאַרְיֵ֔ה וְגַפִּ֥ין דִּֽי־נְשַׁ֖ר לַ֑הּ חָזֵ֣ה הֲוֵ֡ית עַד֩ דִּי־מְּרִ֨יטוּ (גפיה) [גַפַּ֜הּ] וּנְטִ֣ילַת מִן־אַרְעָ֗א וְעַל־רַגְלַ֙יִן֙ כֶּאֱנָ֣שׁ הֳקִימַ֔ת וּלְבַ֥ב אֱנָ֖שׁ יְהִ֥יב לַֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The first was like a lion, and had eagle’s wings: I beheld till its wings were plucked off, and it was lifted up from the earth, and made to stand upon two feet like a man, and a man’s heart was given to it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The first was like a lion, and had eagle's wings [(the kingdom of Bavel [viz. Jeremiah 4:7 and 48:10])]. I beheld until its wings were plucked off, and it was lifted up [i.e., removed] from the earth, and made to stand upon two feet like a [puny] man, and a man's [puny] heart was given to it.


###### Daniel 7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:5)


וַאֲר֣וּ חֵיוָה֩ אׇחֳרִ֨י תִנְיָנָ֜ה דָּמְיָ֣ה לְדֹ֗ב וְלִשְׂטַר־חַד֙ הֳקִמַ֔ת וּתְלָ֥ת עִלְעִ֛ין בְּפֻמַּ֖הּ בֵּ֣ין (שניה) [שִׁנַּ֑הּ] וְכֵן֙ אָמְרִ֣ין לַ֔הּ ק֥וּמִֽי אֲכֻ֖לִי בְּשַׂ֥ר שַׂגִּֽיא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And behold, another beast, a second one, like a bear, and it raised up itself on one side, and it had three ribs in its mouth between its teeth: and thus it was said to it, Arise, devour much flesh.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And behold, another beast, a second one [i.e., rising second from the sea], like a bear [(Paras, reigning after Bavel)]. And it raised itself on one side [(Madai reigning for one year immediately after Bavel)], and it had three ribs in its mouth between its teeth [(an allusion to three kings of Paras: Koresh, Achashverosh and Darius)]. And it was told: Arise, devour much flesh!


###### Daniel 7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:6)


בָּאתַ֨ר דְּנָ֜ה חָזֵ֣ה הֲוֵ֗ית וַאֲר֤וּ אׇֽחֳרִי֙ כִּנְמַ֔ר וְלַ֨הּ גַּפִּ֥ין אַרְבַּ֛ע דִּי־ע֖וֹף עַל־[גַּבַּ֑הּ] (גביה) וְאַרְבְּעָ֤ה רֵאשִׁין֙ לְחֵ֣יוְתָ֔א וְשׇׁלְטָ֖ן יְהִ֥יב לַֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
After this I beheld, and lo another, like a leopard, which had upon the back of it four wings of a bird; the beast had also four heads; and dominion was given to it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
After this I beheld, and lo, another like a leopard [(the kingdom of Antiochus)], which had upon the back of it four wings of a bird. The beast had also four heads [(Alexander of Macedonia dividing his kingdom among four rulers at his death)], and dominion was given to it.


###### Daniel 7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:7)


בָּאתַ֣ר דְּנָה֩ חָזֵ֨ה הֲוֵ֜ית בְּחֶזְוֵ֣י לֵֽילְיָ֗א וַאֲר֣וּ חֵיוָ֣ה (רביעיה) [רְֽבִיעָאָ֡ה] דְּחִילָה֩ וְאֵֽימְתָנִ֨י וְתַקִּיפָ֜א יַתִּ֗ירָה וְשִׁנַּ֨יִן דִּֽי־פַרְזֶ֥ל לַהּ֙ רַבְרְבָ֔ן אָֽכְלָ֣ה וּמַדֱּקָ֔ה וּשְׁאָרָ֖א (ברגליה) [בְּרַגְלַ֣הּ] רָפְסָ֑ה וְהִ֣יא מְשַׁנְּיָ֗ה מִן־כׇּל־חֵֽיוָתָא֙ דִּ֣י (קדמיה) [קׇֽדָמַ֔הּ] וְקַרְנַ֥יִן עֲשַׂ֖ר לַֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
After this I saw in the night visions, and behold a fourth beast, dreadful and terrible, and strong exceedingly; and it had great iron teeth: it devoured and broke in pieces, and stamped the residue with its feet: and it was different from all the beasts that were before it; and it had ten horns.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
After this I saw in a night vision [(a different night, this countervailing the other three)], and behold, a fourth beast [(Rome)], dreadful and terrible, and exceedingly strong; and it had great iron teeth; it devoured and broke in pieces, and stamped the residue with its feet. And it was different from all the beasts that were before it, and it had ten horns [viz. verse 24].


###### Daniel 7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:8)


מִשְׂתַּכַּ֨ל הֲוֵ֜ית בְּקַרְנַיָּ֗א וַ֠אֲל֠וּ קֶ֣רֶן אׇחֳרִ֤י זְעֵירָה֙ סִלְקָ֣ת (ביניהון) [בֵּֽינֵיהֵ֔ן] וּתְלָ֗ת מִן־קַרְנַיָּא֙ קַדְמָ֣יָתָ֔א (אתעקרו) [אֶתְעֲקַ֖רָה] מִן־[קֳדָמַ֑הּ] (קדמיה) וַאֲל֨וּ עַיְנִ֜ין כְּעַיְנֵ֤י אֲנָשָׁא֙ בְּקַרְנָא־דָ֔א וּפֻ֖ם מְמַלִּ֥ל רַבְרְבָֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I considered the horns, and behold, there came up among them another little horn, before which three of the first horns were plucked up by the roots: and, behold, in this horn were eyes like the eyes of man, and a mouth speaking great things.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I considered the horns, and behold, there came up among them another little horn, before which three of the first horns were plucked by the roots. And, behold, in this horn [(Titus)] were eyes like the eyes of man, and a mouth speaking great [i.e., audacious] things [(Titus cursing and blaspheming and entering the sanctuary with brazenness)].


###### Daniel 7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:9)


חָזֵ֣ה הֲוֵ֗ית עַ֣ד דִּ֤י כׇרְסָוָן֙ רְמִ֔יו וְעַתִּ֥יק יוֹמִ֖ין יְתִ֑ב לְבוּשֵׁ֣הּ**׀** כִּתְלַ֣ג חִוָּ֗ר וּשְׂעַ֤ר רֵאשֵׁהּ֙ כַּעֲמַ֣ר נְקֵ֔א כׇּרְסְיֵהּ֙ שְׁבִבִ֣ין דִּי־נ֔וּר גַּלְגִּלּ֖וֹהִי נ֥וּר דָּלִֽק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As I looked, thrones were placed, and an ancient of days did sit, whose garment was white as snow, and the hair of whose head was like the pure wool: his throne was fiery flames, its wheels being burning fire.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As I looked, thrones were placed [(one for judgment and one for charity for Israel)], and the Ancient of Days did sit [(in judgment against the cruelty of the Roman tyrants)], whose garment was white as snow [(to "whiten" the sins of His people)], and the hair of whose head was like pure wool. His throne was fiery flames, its wheels being burning fire.


###### Daniel 7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:10)


נְהַ֣ר דִּי־נ֗וּר נָגֵ֤ד וְנָפֵק֙ מִן־קֳדָמ֔וֹהִי אֶ֤לֶף (אלפים) [אַלְפִין֙] יְשַׁמְּשׁוּנֵּ֔הּ וְרִבּ֥וֹ (רבון) [רִבְבָ֖ן] קָֽדָמ֣וֹהִי יְקוּמ֑וּן דִּינָ֥א יְתִ֖ב וְסִפְרִ֥ין פְּתִֽיחוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A fiery stream issued and came forth from before him: a thousand thousands served him, and ten thousand times ten thousand stood before him: they sat in judgment, and the books were opened.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A fiery stream issued and came forth from before Him. A thousand thousands served Him, and ten thousand times ten thousand stood before Him. Judgment began and the books [(of their transgressions and evils)] were opened.


###### Daniel 7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:11)


חָזֵ֣ה הֲוֵ֔ית בֵּאדַ֗יִן מִן־קָל֙ מִלַּיָּ֣א רַבְרְבָתָ֔א דִּ֥י קַרְנָ֖א מְמַלֱּלָ֑ה חָזֵ֣ה הֲוֵ֡ית עַד֩ דִּ֨י קְטִילַ֤ת חֵֽיוְתָא֙ וְהוּבַ֣ד גִּשְׁמַ֔הּ וִיהִיבַ֖ת לִיקֵדַ֥ת אֶשָּֽׁא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I looked then because of the sound of the great words which the horn spoke: and as I looked the beast was slain, and its body destroyed, and given to the burning flame.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I looked then because of the sound of the great words [viz. 8] which the horn spoke; and as I looked, the beast was slain and its body destroyed, and given to the burning flame [(of Jacob viz. Ovadiah 1:18)]


###### Daniel 7:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:12)


וּשְׁאָר֙ חֵֽיוָתָ֔א הֶעְדִּ֖יו שׇׁלְטָנְה֑וֹן וְאַרְכָ֧ה בְחַיִּ֛ין יְהִ֥יבַת לְה֖וֹן עַד־זְמַ֥ן וְעִדָּֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And as for the rest of the beasts, their dominion was taken away: yet their lives were prolonged for a season and a time.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And, as for the rest of the beasts [i.e., the kingdoms], their dominion was taken away; yet their lives were prolonged for a season and a time [(until the war of Gog and Magog)].


###### Daniel 7:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:13)


חָזֵ֤ה הֲוֵית֙ בְּחֶזְוֵ֣י לֵֽילְיָ֔א וַאֲרוּ֙ עִם־עֲנָנֵ֣י שְׁמַיָּ֔א כְּבַ֥ר אֱנָ֖שׁ אָתֵ֣ה הֲוָ֑א וְעַד־עַתִּ֤יק יֽוֹמַיָּא֙ מְטָ֔ה וּקְדָמ֖וֹהִי הַקְרְבֽוּהִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I saw in the night visions, and, behold, one like a son of man came with the clouds of heaven, and came to the ancient of days, and they brought him near before him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I saw in the night visions, and, behold, one like a son of man [(the Messiah)] came with the clouds of heaven, and came to the Ancient of Days [(who was judging the peoples)], and they brought him near before Him.


###### Daniel 7:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:14)


וְלֵ֨הּ יְהִ֤ב שׇׁלְטָן֙ וִיקָ֣ר וּמַלְכ֔וּ וְכֹ֣ל עַֽמְמַיָּ֗א אֻמַּיָּ֛א וְלִשָּׁנַיָּ֖א לֵ֣הּ יִפְלְח֑וּן שׇׁלְטָנֵ֞הּ שׇׁלְטָ֤ן עָלַם֙ דִּֽי־לָ֣א יֶעְדֵּ֔ה וּמַלְכוּתֵ֖הּ דִּי־לָ֥א תִתְחַבַּֽל׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And there was given him dominion, and glory, and a kingdom, that all the peoples, nations, and tongues, should serve him: his dominion is an everlasting dominion, which shall not pass away, and his kingdom one that shall not be destroyed.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And there was given him dominion, and glory, and a kingdom, that all the peoples, nations, and tongues should serve him. His dominion is an everlasting dominion, which shall not pass away, and his kingdom one that shall not be destroyed.


###### Daniel 7:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:15)


אֶתְכְּרִיַּ֥ת רוּחִ֛י אֲנָ֥ה דָנִיֵּ֖אל בְּג֣וֹא נִדְנֶ֑ה וְחֶזְוֵ֥י רֵאשִׁ֖י יְבַהֲלֻנַּֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As for me Daniyyel, my spirit was grieved in the midst of my body, and the visions of my head affrighted me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As for me, Daniel, my spirit was in turmoil in the midst of my body, and the visions of my head confounded me.


###### Daniel 7:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:16)


קִרְבֵ֗ת עַל־חַד֙ מִן־קָ֣אֲמַיָּ֔א וְיַצִּיבָ֥א אֶבְעֵֽא־מִנֵּ֖הּ עַֽל־כׇּל־דְּנָ֑ה וַאֲמַר־לִ֕י וּפְשַׁ֥ר מִלַּיָּ֖א יְהוֹדְעִנַּֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I came near to one of them that stood by, and asked him the truth of all this. So he told me, and made known to me the meaning of the things.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I came near to one of them [(one of the angels)] that stood by [the Ancient of Days] and asked him the truth of all this. So he told me, and made known to me the meaning of the things.


###### Daniel 7:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:17)


אִלֵּין֙ חֵיוָתָ֣א רַבְרְבָתָ֔א דִּ֥י אִנִּ֖ין אַרְבַּ֑ע אַרְבְּעָ֥ה מַלְכִ֖ין יְקוּמ֥וּן מִן־אַרְעָֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
These great beasts, which are four, are four kings, who shall arise out of the earth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
These great beasts, which are four, are four kingdoms, which shall arise out of the earth.


###### Daniel 7:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:18)


וִֽיקַבְּלוּן֙ מַלְכוּתָ֔א קַדִּישֵׁ֖י עֶלְיוֹנִ֑ין וְיַחְסְנ֤וּן מַלְכוּתָא֙ עַד־עָ֣לְמָ֔א וְעַ֖ד עָלַ֥ם עָלְמַיָּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the holy ones of the Most High shall take the kingdom, and possess the kingdom forever, even forever and ever.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But the holy ones [(Israel)] of the Most High shall take the kingdom [(from the fourth)], and possess the kingdom forever and for all eternity.


###### Daniel 7:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:19)


אֱדַ֗יִן צְבִית֙ לְיַצָּבָ֗א עַל־חֵֽיוְתָא֙ רְבִיעָ֣יְתָ֔א דִּֽי־הֲוָ֥ת שָֽׁנְיָ֖ה מִן־[כׇּלְּהֵ֑ין] (כלהון) דְּחִילָ֣ה יַתִּ֗ירָה (שניה) [שִׁנַּ֤הּ] דִּֽי־פַרְזֶל֙ (וטפריה) [וְטִפְרַ֣הּ] דִּֽי־נְחָ֔שׁ אָֽכְלָ֣ה מַדְּקָ֔ה וּשְׁאָרָ֖א (ברגליה) [בְּרַגְלַ֥הּ] רָֽפְסָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then I wished to know the truth of the fourth beast, which was different from all the others, exceedingly dreadful, the teeth of which were of iron, and its nails of brass; which devoured, broke in pieces, and stamped the residue with his feet;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then I wished to know the truth of the fourth beast, which was different from all the others, exceedingly dreadful, the teeth of which were of iron, and its nails of brass; which devoured, broke in pieces, and stamped the residue with his feet.


###### Daniel 7:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:20)


וְעַל־קַרְנַיָּ֤א עֲשַׂר֙ דִּ֣י בְרֵאשַׁ֔הּ וְאׇחֳרִי֙ דִּ֣י סִלְקַ֔ת (ונפלו) [וּנְפַ֥לָה] מִן־[קֳדָמַ֖הּ] (קדמיה) תְּלָ֑ת וְקַרְנָ֨א דִכֵּ֜ן וְעַיְנִ֣ין לַ֗הּ וּפֻם֙ מְמַלִּ֣ל רַבְרְבָ֔ן וְחֶזְוַ֖הּ רַ֥ב מִן־חַבְרָתַֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and of the ten horns that were on its head, and of the other horn which came up, and before which three fell; that horn which had eyes, and a mouth that spoke very great things, and which appeared greater than its fellows.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And ["I wished to know, etc."] of the ten horns that were on its head, and of the other horn which came up, and before which three fell; that horn which had eyes, and a mouth that spoke very great things, and which appeared greater than its fellows.


###### Daniel 7:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:21)


חָזֵ֣ה הֲוֵ֔ית וְקַרְנָ֣א דִכֵּ֔ן עָבְדָ֥ה קְרָ֖ב עִם־קַדִּישִׁ֑ין וְיָכְלָ֖ה לְהֹֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I beheld, and the same horn made war with the holy ones, and prevailed against them;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I beheld, and the same horn made war with the holy ones [(Israel)], and prevailed against them,


###### Daniel 7:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:22)


עַ֣ד דִּֽי־אֲתָ֗ה עַתִּיק֙ יֽוֹמַיָּ֔א וְדִינָ֣א יְהִ֔ב לְקַדִּישֵׁ֖י עֶלְיוֹנִ֑ין וְזִמְנָ֣א מְטָ֔ה וּמַלְכוּתָ֖א הֶחֱסִ֥נוּ קַדִּישִֽׁין׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
until the ancient of days came, and judgment was given for the holy ones of the Most High; and the time came that the holy ones possessed the kingdom.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
until the Ancient of Days came, and judgment was given [i.e., vengeance was taken] for the holy ones of the Most High; and the time came that the holy ones possessed the kingdom.


###### Daniel 7:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:23)


כֵּן֮ אֲמַר֒ חֵֽיוְתָא֙ רְבִיעָ֣יְתָ֔א מַלְכ֤וּ (רביעיה) [רְבִיעָאָה֙] תֶּהֱוֵ֣א בְאַרְעָ֔א דִּ֥י תִשְׁנֵ֖א מִן־כׇּל־מַלְכְוָתָ֑א וְתֵאכֻל֙ כׇּל־אַרְעָ֔א וּתְדוּשִׁנַּ֖הּ וְתַדְּקִנַּֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thus he said, The fourth beast shall be a fourth kingdom upon earth, which shall be different from all kingdoms, and shall devour the whole earth, and shall tread it down, and break it in pieces.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Thus he [(the angel interpreting the dream)] said: The fourth beast shall be a fourth kingdom upon earth, which shall be different from all kingdoms, and shall devour the whole earth, and shall tread it down, and break it in pieces.


###### Daniel 7:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:24)


וְקַרְנַיָּ֣א עֲשַׂ֔ר מִנַּהּ֙ מַלְכוּתָ֔א עַשְׂרָ֥ה מַלְכִ֖ין יְקֻמ֑וּן וְאׇחֳרָ֞ן יְק֣וּם אַחֲרֵיהֹ֗ן וְה֤וּא יִשְׁנֵא֙ מִן־קַדְמָיֵ֔א וּתְלָתָ֥ה מַלְכִ֖ין יְהַשְׁפִּֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the ten horns are ten kings that shall arise out of this kingdom: and another shall rise after them; and he shall be different from the former ones, and he shall subdue three kings.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the ten horns [(that I saw)] are ten kings that shall arise out of this kingdom; and another [(Titus)] shall rise after them. And he shall be different from the former ones, and he shall subdue three kings.


###### Daniel 7:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:25)


וּמִלִּ֗ין לְצַ֤ד (עליא) [עִלָּאָה֙] יְמַלִּ֔ל וּלְקַדִּישֵׁ֥י עֶלְיוֹנִ֖ין יְבַלֵּ֑א וְיִסְבַּ֗ר לְהַשְׁנָיָה֙ זִמְנִ֣ין וְדָ֔ת וְיִתְיַהֲב֣וּן בִּידֵ֔הּ עַד־עִדָּ֥ן וְעִדָּנִ֖ין וּפְלַ֥ג עִדָּֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall speak great words against the Most High, and shall wear out the holy ones of the Most High, and think to change times and laws: and they shall be given into his hand for a season and seasons and half a season.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall speak great words against the most High, and shall wear out the holy ones of the Most High, and think to change times and laws [i.e., to make them apostates]; and they shall be given into his hand for a time and times and half a time [(The "times" are concealed from us)].


###### Daniel 7:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:26)


וְדִינָ֖א יִתִּ֑ב וְשׇׁלְטָנֵ֣הּ יְהַעְדּ֔וֹן לְהַשְׁמָדָ֥ה וּלְהוֹבָדָ֖ה עַד־סוֹפָֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But they shall sit in judgment, and his dominion shall be taken away, to be consumed and destroyed to the end.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But judgment was convened, and his dominion shall be removed, to be consumed and destroyed to the end.


###### Daniel 7:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:27)


וּמַלְכוּתָ֨א וְשׇׁלְטָנָ֜א וּרְבוּתָ֗א דִּ֚י מַלְכְוָת֙ תְּח֣וֹת כׇּל־שְׁמַיָּ֔א יְהִיבַ֕ת לְעַ֖ם קַדִּישֵׁ֣י עֶלְיוֹנִ֑ין מַלְכוּתֵהּ֙ מַלְכ֣וּת עָלַ֔ם וְכֹל֙ שׇׁלְטָ֣נַיָּ֔א לֵ֥הּ יִפְלְח֖וּן וְיִֽשְׁתַּמְּעֽוּן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the kingdom and dominion, and the greatness of the kingdoms under the whole heaven, shall be given to the people of the holy ones of the Most High, whose kingdom is an everlasting kingdom, and all dominions shall serve and obey him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the kingdom and dominion, and the greatness of the kingdoms under the whole heaven, shall be given to the people, to the holy ones of the Most High, whose kingdom is an everlasting kingdom, and all dominions shall serve and obey Him.


###### Daniel 7:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/7:28)


עַד־כָּ֖ה סוֹפָ֣א דִֽי־מִלְּתָ֑א אֲנָ֨ה דָֽנִיֵּ֜אל שַׂגִּ֣יא**׀** רַעְיוֹנַ֣י יְבַהֲלֻנַּ֗נִי וְזִיוַי֙ יִשְׁתַּנּ֣וֹן עֲלַ֔י וּמִלְּתָ֖א בְּלִבִּ֥י נִטְרֵֽת׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Here is the end of the matter. As for me Daniyyel, my cogitations much affrighted me, and my colour changed: but I kept the matter in my heart.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Here is the end of the matter. As for me, Daniel, my cogitations confounded me, and my aspect altered; but I kept the matter in my heart.



## Chapter 8

###### Daniel 8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:1)


בִּשְׁנַ֣ת שָׁל֔וֹשׁ לְמַלְכ֖וּת בֵּלְאשַׁצַּ֣ר הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ חָז֞וֹן נִרְאָ֤ה אֵלַי֙ אֲנִ֣י דָנִיֵּ֔אל אַחֲרֵ֛י הַנִּרְאָ֥ה אֵלַ֖י בַּתְּחִלָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In the third year of the reign of king Belshażżar a vision appeared to me, even to me Daniyyel, after that which appeared to me at the first.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the third year of the reign of King Belshazzar, a vision appeared to me — I, Daniel — (after the vision that had appeared to me in the beginning), [(in the first year of Belshazzar's reign].


###### Daniel 8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:2)


וָֽאֶרְאֶה֮ בֶּחָזוֹן֒ וַֽיְהִי֙ בִּרְאֹתִ֔י וַאֲנִי֙ בְּשׁוּשַׁ֣ן הַבִּירָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֖ר בְּעֵילָ֣ם הַמְּדִינָ֑ה וָאֶרְאֶה֙ בֶּֽחָז֔וֹן וַאֲנִ֥י הָיִ֖יתִי עַל־אוּבַ֥ל אוּלָֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I saw in a vision; and it came to pass, when I saw, that I was at Shushan the capital, which is in the province of ῾Elam; and I saw in a vision, and I was by the river of Ulay.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I saw in the vision. And it was, when I saw, I was in Shushan, the capital, in the province of Eilam. And I saw in the vision that I was on the river Ulai.


###### Daniel 8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:3)


וָאֶשָּׂ֤א עֵינַי֙ וָאֶרְאֶ֔ה וְהִנֵּ֣ה**׀** אַ֣יִל אֶחָ֗ד עֹמֵ֛ד לִפְנֵ֥י הָאֻבָ֖ל וְל֣וֹ קְרָנָ֑יִם וְהַקְּרָנַ֣יִם גְּבֹה֗וֹת וְהָאַחַת֙ גְּבֹהָ֣ה מִן־הַשֵּׁנִ֔ית וְהַ֨גְּבֹהָ֔ה עֹלָ֖ה בָּאַחֲרֹנָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then I lifted up my eyes, and saw, and, behold, there stood before the river a ram which had two horns: and the two horns were high; but one was higher than the other, and the higher came up last.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I lifted my eyes, and I saw, and, behold, a two-horned ram [(the kingdoms of Paras, and Madai)] standing before the river. The horns were high, and one [(Paras)] was higher than the other, [(Madai reigning only one year)]; and the higher rose afterwards [(after the one-year kingdom of Madai)].


###### Daniel 8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:4)


רָאִ֣יתִי אֶת־הָאַ֡יִל מְנַגֵּ֩חַ֩ יָ֨מָּה וְצָפ֜וֹנָה וָנֶ֗גְבָּה וְכׇל־חַיּוֹת֙ לֹֽא־יַֽעַמְד֣וּ לְפָנָ֔יו וְאֵ֥ין מַצִּ֖יל מִיָּד֑וֹ וְעָשָׂ֥ה כִרְצֹנ֖וֹ וְהִגְדִּֽיל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I saw the ram pushing westward, and northward, and southward; so that no beasts might stand before him, was not there any that could deliver out of his hand; but he did according to his will, and magnified himself.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I saw the ram butting: west, north, and south. And all the animals could not withstand him, and there was no deliverance from him, and he did as he willed and grew great.


###### Daniel 8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:5)


וַאֲנִ֣י**׀** הָיִ֣יתִי מֵבִ֗ין וְהִנֵּ֤ה צְפִיר־הָֽעִזִּים֙ בָּ֤א מִן־הַֽמַּעֲרָב֙ עַל־פְּנֵ֣י כׇל־הָאָ֔רֶץ וְאֵ֥ין נוֹגֵ֖עַ בָּאָ֑רֶץ וְהַ֨צָּפִ֔יר קֶ֥רֶן חָז֖וּת בֵּ֥ין עֵינָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And as I was considering, behold, a he goat came from the west across the face of the whole earth, and did not touch the ground: and the goat had a conspicuous horn between its eyes.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I observed, and, behold, a he-goat coming from the west over all the earth and not touching the earth [(seeming to be skipping in the air)]. And the he-goat had a highly prominent horn between his eyes.


###### Daniel 8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:6)


וַיָּבֹ֗א עַד־הָאַ֙יִל֙ בַּ֣עַל הַקְּרָנַ֔יִם אֲשֶׁ֣ר רָאִ֔יתִי עֹמֵ֖ד לִפְנֵ֣י הָאֻבָ֑ל וַיָּ֥רׇץ אֵלָ֖יו בַּחֲמַ֥ת כֹּחֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it came to the ram that had two horns, which I had seen standing before the river, and ran at him in the fury of his power.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he came to the two-horned ram that I saw standing before the river and he charged him furiously.


###### Daniel 8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:7)


וּרְאִיתִ֞יו מַגִּ֣יעַ**׀** אֵ֣צֶל הָאַ֗יִל וַיִּתְמַרְמַ֤ר אֵלָיו֙ וַיַּ֣ךְ אֶת־הָאַ֗יִל וַיְשַׁבֵּר֙ אֶת־שְׁתֵּ֣י קְרָנָ֔יו וְלֹא־הָ֥יָה כֹ֛חַ בָּאַ֖יִל לַעֲמֹ֣ד לְפָנָ֑יו וַיַּשְׁלִיכֵ֤הוּ אַ֙רְצָה֙ וַֽיִּרְמְסֵ֔הוּ וְלֹא־הָיָ֥ה מַצִּ֛יל לָאַ֖יִל מִיָּדֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I saw it come close to the ram, and it was enraged against him, and smote the ram, and broke his two horns: and the ram had no power to stand before it, but he cast him down to the ground, and trampled upon him: and there was none that could deliver the ram out of his hand.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I saw him reaching the ram and battling with him. And he smote the ram and broke his two horns. And the ram had not strength to withstand him. And he thrust him to the earth and trod him down, and there was no saving the ram from him.


###### Daniel 8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:8)


וּצְפִ֥יר הָעִזִּ֖ים הִגְדִּ֣יל עַד־מְאֹ֑ד וּכְעׇצְמ֗וֹ נִשְׁבְּרָה֙ הַקֶּ֣רֶן הַגְּדֹלָ֔ה וַֽתַּעֲלֶ֜נָה חָז֤וּת אַרְבַּע֙ תַּחְתֶּ֔יהָ לְאַרְבַּ֖ע רוּח֥וֹת הַשָּׁמָֽיִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore the he goat grew very great: and when he was strong, the great horn was broken; and instead of it came up four conspicuous ones towards the four winds of heaven.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the he-goat grew very great, and at the height of his power, the great horn broke, and there appeared four (horns) rising in its place to the four sides of heaven.


###### Daniel 8:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:9)


וּמִן־הָאַחַ֣ת מֵהֶ֔ם יָצָ֥א קֶֽרֶן־אַחַ֖ת מִצְּעִירָ֑ה וַתִּגְדַּל־יֶ֛תֶר אֶל־הַנֶּ֥גֶב וְאֶל־הַמִּזְרָ֖ח וְאֶל־הַצֶּֽבִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And out of one of them came forth a little horn, which grew exceedingly great, towards the south, and towards the east, and towards the land of beauty.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And from one of them [i.e., from one of these kingdoms, Edom,] there came forth a diminutive horn [(Titus)] [viz. Ovadiah 1:2], and he grew exceedingly great: to the south [(Egypt)], to the east, and to the Tzevi [(Eretz Yisrael)].


###### Daniel 8:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:10)


וַתִּגְדַּ֖ל עַד־צְבָ֣א הַשָּׁמָ֑יִם וַתַּפֵּ֥ל אַ֛רְצָה מִן־הַצָּבָ֥א וּמִן־הַכּוֹכָבִ֖ים וַֽתִּרְמְסֵֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it grew great, even to the host of heaven; and it cast down some of the host and of the stars to the ground, and trampled upon them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he grew until the host of the heavens [(Israel)]. And he toppled to the ground (some) of the host and of the stars, and he trod them down.


###### Daniel 8:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:11)


וְעַ֥ד שַֽׂר־הַצָּבָ֖א הִגְדִּ֑יל וּמִמֶּ֙נּוּ֙ (הרים) [הוּרַ֣ם] הַתָּמִ֔יד וְהֻשְׁלַ֖ךְ מְכ֥וֹן מִקְדָּשֽׁוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yes, it magnified itself even to the prince of the host, and from him the daily sacrifice was taken away, and the place of his sanctuary was cast down.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And until [the abode (i.e., the Temple) of] the Chief of the Host did he grow, and by him [(and his troops)] was the continual [burnt-offering] abolished, and His holy abode cast [to the ground].


###### Daniel 8:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:12)


וְצָבָ֛א תִּנָּתֵ֥ן עַל־הַתָּמִ֖יד בְּפָ֑שַׁע וְתַשְׁלֵ֤ךְ אֱמֶת֙ אַ֔רְצָה וְעָשְׂתָ֖ה וְהִצְלִֽיחָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And for an appointed time it was flagrantly set against the daily sacrifice, and it cast down the truth to the ground; and it practised, and prospered.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a time will be set for the [abolition of] the continual [burnt-offering] by [displacement with] the "offense" [i.e., idolatry.] And it [(idolatry)] shall cast truth [(Torah)] to the ground; and it has wrought and prospered.


###### Daniel 8:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:13)


וָאֶשְׁמְעָ֥ה אֶֽחָד־קָד֖וֹשׁ מְדַבֵּ֑ר וַיֹּ֩אמֶר֩ אֶחָ֨ד קָד֜וֹשׁ לַפַּֽלְמוֹנִ֣י הַֽמְדַבֵּ֗ר עַד־מָתַ֞י הֶחָז֤וֹן הַתָּמִיד֙ וְהַפֶּ֣שַׁע שֹׁמֵ֔ם תֵּ֛ת וְקֹ֥דֶשׁ וְצָבָ֖א מִרְמָֽס׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then I heard one holy one speaking, and another holy one said to that certain one who spoke, How long shall be the vision concerning the daily sacrifice, and the transgression of desolation, to give both the sanctuary and the host to be trodden under foot?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I heard a holy one [(one of the angels)] speaking. And the holy one said to the one speaking: Until when will this vision (last) the [displacement of the] continual [burnt-offering] by "the mute offense" [(idolatry)], the relegating of the holy [abode] and the host [of heaven] to be trodden underfoot?


###### Daniel 8:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:14)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלַ֔י עַ֚ד עֶ֣רֶב בֹּ֔קֶר אַלְפַּ֖יִם וּשְׁלֹ֣שׁ מֵא֑וֹת וְנִצְדַּ֖ק קֹֽדֶשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he said to me, For two thousand and three hundred evenings and mornings; then shall the sanctuary be restored.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he said to me: Until "evening-morning" [(erev-boker - 574 in gematria)] (and) two thousand and three hundred [i.e., after 2,874 years]; and [then] the holy [people, Israel,] will be found righteous.


###### Daniel 8:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:15)


וַיְהִ֗י בִּרְאֹתִ֛י אֲנִ֥י דָנִיֵּ֖אל אֶת־הֶחָז֑וֹן וָאֲבַקְשָׁ֣ה בִינָ֔ה וְהִנֵּ֛ה עֹמֵ֥ד לְנֶגְדִּ֖י כְּמַרְאֵה־גָֽבֶר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it came to pass, when I, even I Daniyyel, had seen the vision, and sought for the meaning, then, behold, there stood before me what looked like a man.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was when I saw — I, Daniel — the vision, that I asked for understanding (of it); and, behold, there stood opposite me (a form) like the appearance of a man.


###### Daniel 8:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:16)


וָאֶשְׁמַ֥ע קוֹל־אָדָ֖ם בֵּ֣ין אוּלָ֑י וַיִּקְרָא֙ וַיֹּאמַ֔ר גַּבְרִיאֵ֕ל הָבֵ֥ן לְהַלָּ֖ז אֶת־הַמַּרְאֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I heard a man’s voice between the banks of Ulay, who called, and said, Gavri᾽el, make this man understand the vision.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I heard the voice of a man in the midst of [the river] Ulai. And he called and said: "Gabriel, explain the vision to this one."


###### Daniel 8:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:17)


וַיָּבֹא֙ אֵ֣צֶל עׇמְדִ֔י וּבְבֹא֣וֹ נִבְעַ֔תִּי וָאֶפְּלָ֖ה עַל־פָּנָ֑י וַיֹּ֤אמֶר אֵלַי֙ הָבֵ֣ן בֶּן־אָדָ֔ם כִּ֖י לְעֶת־קֵ֥ץ הֶחָזֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So he came near where I stood: and when he came, I was afraid, and fell upon my face: but he said to me, Understand, O son of man: for the vision is for the time of the end.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he [(Gabriel)] came near where I stood. And when he came, I was confounded, and I fell on my face. And he said to me: Understand, son of man, that the vision [will materialize] at the end of a [long] time.


###### Daniel 8:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:18)


וּבְדַבְּר֣וֹ עִמִּ֔י נִרְדַּ֥מְתִּי עַל־פָּנַ֖י אָ֑רְצָה וַיִּ֨גַּע־בִּ֔י וַיַּֽעֲמִידֵ֖נִי עַל־עׇמְדִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now as he was speaking with me, I fell into a deep sleep on my face towards the ground: but he touched me, and set me upright.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when he spoke with me, I foundered on my face to the ground. And he touched me, and he stood me up where I had stood.


###### Daniel 8:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:19)


וַיֹּ֙אמֶר֙ הִנְנִ֣י מוֹדִֽיעֲךָ֔ אֵ֥ת אֲשֶׁר־יִהְיֶ֖ה בְּאַחֲרִ֣ית הַזָּ֑עַם כִּ֖י לְמוֹעֵ֥ד קֵֽץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he said, Behold, I will make thee know what shall be in the latter end of the indignation: for it is for the appointed time of the end.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he said: Behold, I shall apprise you of what will transpire at the termination of the wrath; for it shall be at the end of a [long] time.


###### Daniel 8:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:20)


הָאַ֥יִל אֲשֶׁר־רָאִ֖יתָ בַּ֣עַל הַקְּרָנָ֑יִם מַלְכֵ֖י מָדַ֥י וּפָרָֽס׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The ram which thou sawest having two horns are the kings of Maday and Paras.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The two-horned ram that you saw are the kings of Madai and Paras [viz. verse 2, etc.].


###### Daniel 8:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:21)


וְהַצָּפִ֥יר הַשָּׂעִ֖יר מֶ֣לֶךְ יָוָ֑ן וְהַקֶּ֤רֶן הַגְּדוֹלָה֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בֵּין־עֵינָ֔יו ה֖וּא הַמֶּ֥לֶךְ הָרִאשֽׁוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the rough he goat is the king of Yavan: and the great horn that is between his eyes is the first king.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the he-goat is the king of Yavan [(Greece)], and the great horn between his eyes is the first king [(Alexander of Macedonia, who killed Darius, the son of Esther)].


###### Daniel 8:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:22)


וְהַ֨נִּשְׁבֶּ֔רֶת וַתַּֽעֲמֹ֥דְנָה אַרְבַּ֖ע תַּחְתֶּ֑יהָ אַרְבַּ֧ע מַלְכֻי֛וֹת מִגּ֥וֹי יַעֲמֹ֖דְנָה וְלֹ֥א בְכֹחֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As for the horn which was broken, in place of which four others arose, four kingdoms shall stand up out of the nation, but not with his power.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And its being broken, and four (horns rising) in its place — Four kings will arise from the nation [(divided by Alexander at his death)], and will not [attain to] his [(the first king's)] power.


###### Daniel 8:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:23)


וּֽבְאַחֲרִית֙ מַלְכוּתָ֔ם כְּהָתֵ֖ם הַפֹּשְׁעִ֑ים יַעֲמֹ֛ד מֶ֥לֶךְ עַז־פָּנִ֖ים וּמֵבִ֥ין חִידֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And in the latter time of their kingdom, when the transgressors are come to the full, a king of fierce countenance, and one who understands riddles, shall arise.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And in the end of their kingdom, when the [time comes for] the offenders [(the evildoers of Israel in the second Temple)] to be eliminated, there will arise a king [(Titus)], brazen-faced and wily.


###### Daniel 8:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:24)


וְעָצַ֤ם כֹּחוֹ֙ וְלֹ֣א בְכֹח֔וֹ וְנִפְלָא֥וֹת יַשְׁחִ֖ית וְהִצְלִ֣יחַ וְעָשָׂ֑ה וְהִשְׁחִ֥ית עֲצוּמִ֖ים וְעַם־קְדֹשִֽׁים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And his power shall be mighty, but not by his own power: and he shall destroy remarkably, and shall prosper, and practise, and shall destroy the mighty and the holy people.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And his power will grow, but not through his strength [(but through his canniness)]. And he will destroy [(the idolators)] wondrously; and he will prosper and do [his will]. And he will destroy [many] powerful [nations], and the holy people [(Israel, who believe in the Torah)].


###### Daniel 8:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:25)


וְעַל־שִׂכְל֗וֹ וְהִצְלִ֤יחַ מִרְמָה֙ בְּיָד֔וֹ וּבִלְבָב֣וֹ יַגְדִּ֔יל וּבְשַׁלְוָ֖ה יַשְׁחִ֣ית רַבִּ֑ים וְעַ֤ל שַׂר־שָׂרִים֙ יַעֲמֹ֔ד וּבְאֶ֥פֶס יָ֖ד יִשָּׁבֵֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And through his cunning also he shall cause craft to prosper in his hand, and shall magnify himself in his heart, and without difficulty he shall destroy many: he shall also stand up against the Prince of princes; but he shall be broken without hand.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And because he prospers and succeeds [in everything he does], he will [cling to] his craftiness, and grow haughty of heart, and will destroy many [(of his allies)] with equanimity. And against the Chief of chiefs will he rise [viz. verse 11], and by a powerless one [(a mosquito [viz. Gittin 56b])] will he be broken.


###### Daniel 8:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:26)


וּמַרְאֵ֨ה הָעֶ֧רֶב וְהַבֹּ֛קֶר אֲשֶׁ֥ר נֶאֱמַ֖ר אֱמֶ֣ת ה֑וּא וְאַתָּה֙ סְתֹ֣ם הֶֽחָז֔וֹן כִּ֖י לְיָמִ֥ים רַבִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the vision of the evenings and the mornings which was told is true: but seal up the vision; for it refers to many days hence.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the vision of "erev-boker" [viz. 14], which was stated (to you) is true. And you, seal up [(the vision in your heart and do not elucidate it)], for [it will materialize only after] many days.


###### Daniel 8:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/8:27)


וַאֲנִ֣י דָנִיֵּ֗אל נִהְיֵ֤יתִי וְנֶֽחֱלֵ֙יתִי֙ יָמִ֔ים וָאָק֕וּם וָאֶֽעֱשֶׂ֖ה אֶת־מְלֶ֣אכֶת הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וָאֶשְׁתּוֹמֵ֥ם עַל־הַמַּרְאֶ֖ה וְאֵ֥ין מֵבִֽין׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I Daniyyel fainted, and was sick certain days; afterwards I rose up, and did the king’s business; and I was astonished at the vision, but none understood it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I, Daniel, was broken [(by what I had seen in the vision)], and I took ill for days; but I arose and did the work of the king [viz. 2:48]. And I was confounded over the vision, but [(I dissembled and)] none perceived (it).



## Chapter 9

###### Daniel 9:1

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In the first year of Daryavesh the son of Aĥashverosh, of the seed of Maday, who was made king over the realm of the Kasdians;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the first year of Darius the son of Achashverosh of the seed of Madai, who was crowned over the kingdom of the Kasdim [(at the death of Belshazzar [viz. 6:1],


###### Daniel 9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:2)


בִּשְׁנַ֤ת אַחַת֙ לְמׇלְכ֔וֹ אֲנִי֙ דָּֽנִיֵּ֔אל בִּינֹ֖תִי בַּסְּפָרִ֑ים מִסְפַּ֣ר הַשָּׁנִ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֨ר הָיָ֤ה דְבַר־יְהֹוָה֙ אֶל־יִרְמְיָ֣ה הַנָּבִ֔יא לְמַלֹּ֛אות לְחׇרְב֥וֹת יְרוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם שִׁבְעִ֥ים שָׁנָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
in the first year of his reign I Daniyyel considered in the books the number of the years, whereof the word of the Lord came to Yirmeya the prophet, that he would accomplish seventy years in the desolations of Yerushalayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
in the first year of his reign, I Daniel, considered the accounts of the number of years [(for the redemption from the Babylonian exile)], of which the L-rd spoke to Jeremiah the prophet [viz. Jeremiah 29:10], [(that redemption to take place)] upon the completion of seventy years of [the ascendancy of Bavel] over the ruins of Jerusalem.


###### Daniel 9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:3)


וָאֶתְּנָ֣ה אֶת־פָּנַ֗י אֶל־אֲדֹנָי֙ הָֽאֱלֹהִ֔ים לְבַקֵּ֥שׁ תְּפִלָּ֖ה וְתַחֲנוּנִ֑ים בְּצ֖וֹם וְשַׂ֥ק וָאֵֽפֶר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I set my face to the Lord God, to seek by prayer and supplications, with fasting, and sackcloth, and ashes:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I set my face to the L-rd G-d to seek [to intercede with] prayer and supplications; with fasting, sack, and ashes.


###### Daniel 9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:4)


וָֽאֶתְפַּֽלְלָ֛ה לַיהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהַ֖י וָאֶתְוַדֶּ֑ה וָאֹֽמְרָ֗ה אָנָּ֤א אֲדֹנָי֙ הָאֵ֤ל הַגָּדוֹל֙ וְהַנּוֹרָ֔א שֹׁמֵ֤ר הַבְּרִית֙ וְֽהַחֶ֔סֶד לְאֹהֲבָ֖יו וּלְשֹׁמְרֵ֥י מִצְוֺתָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and I prayed to the Lord my God, and made my confession, and said, O Lord, the great and dreadful God, keeping covenant and troth to those who love him, and to those who keep his commandments;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I prayed to the L-rd my G-d, and I made confession [(over the sin of my people)]. And I said: I beseech You, My L-rd, the great and awesome G-d, keeper of the covenant and of the lovingkindness to His lovers and to the keepers of His mitzvoth.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:5)


חָטָ֥אנוּ וְעָוִ֖ינוּ (והרשענו) [הִרְשַׁ֣עְנוּ] וּמָרָ֑דְנוּ וְס֥וֹר מִמִּצְוֺתֶ֖ךָ וּמִמִּשְׁפָּטֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
we have sinned, and have committed iniquity, and have done wickedly, and have rebelled, and have turned aside from Thy precepts and from Thy judgments:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
We have sinned and transgressed and done evil and rebelled and turned away from Your mitzvoth and from Your judgments.


###### Daniel 9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:6)


וְלֹ֤א שָׁמַ֙עְנוּ֙ אֶל־עֲבָדֶ֣יךָ הַנְּבִיאִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֤ר דִּבְּרוּ֙ בְּשִׁמְךָ֔ אֶל־מְלָכֵ֥ינוּ שָׂרֵ֖ינוּ וַאֲבֹתֵ֑ינוּ וְאֶ֖ל כׇּל־עַ֥ם הָאָֽרֶץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for we have not hearkened to Thy servants the prophets, who spoke in Thy name to our kings, our princes, and our fathers, and to all the people of the land.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And we did not pay heed to Your servants, the prophets, who spoke in Your name to our kings, our leaders, and our fathers, and to all the people of the land.


###### Daniel 9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:7)


לְךָ֤ אֲדֹנָי֙ הַצְּדָקָ֔ה וְלָ֛נוּ בֹּ֥שֶׁת הַפָּנִ֖ים כַּיּ֣וֹם הַזֶּ֑ה לְאִ֤ישׁ יְהוּדָה֙ וּלְיֹשְׁבֵ֣י יְרֽוּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם וּֽלְכׇל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֞ל הַקְּרֹבִ֣ים וְהָרְחֹקִ֗ים בְּכׇל־הָֽאֲרָצוֹת֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר הִדַּחְתָּ֣ם שָׁ֔ם בְּמַעֲלָ֖ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר מָֽעֲלוּ־בָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
O Lord, righteousness belongs to Thee, but to us confusion of faces, as at this day; to the men of Yehuda, and to the inhabitants of Yerushalayim, and to all Yisra᾽el, who are near, and who are far off, through all the countries whither Thou hast driven them, because of their trespass which they have trespassed against Thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Yours, O L-rd, is the righteousness [i.e., You are right in the judgments and the punishments that You have brought upon us], and ours is the shame-facedness [i.e., it is fitting that we be ashamed over what is happening to us], as [we see] this day — [(what is happening)] to the men of Judah and the dwellers of Jerusalem and to all of Israel, the near and the far, in all the lands where You cast them for their faithlessness to You.


###### Daniel 9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:8)


יְהֹוָ֗ה לָ֚נוּ בֹּ֣שֶׁת הַפָּנִ֔ים לִמְלָכֵ֥ינוּ לְשָׂרֵ֖ינוּ וְלַאֲבֹתֵ֑ינוּ אֲשֶׁ֥ר חָטָ֖אנוּ לָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
O Lord, to us belongs confusion of face, to our kings, to our princes, and to our fathers, because we have sinned against Thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
O L-rd, ours is the shame-facedness, for our kings, for our leaders, and for our fathers, (for) what we have sinned against You.
