לַֽאדֹנָ֣י אֱלֹהֵ֔ינוּ הָרַחֲמִ֖ים וְהַסְּלִח֑וֹת כִּ֥י מָרַ֖דְנוּ בּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
To the Lord our God belongs mercies and forgiveness, for we have rebelled against him;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The mercies and the pardons are to be ascribed to the L-rd our G-d [(for not having destroyed us)], for we have rebelled against Him.


###### Daniel 9:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:10)


וְלֹ֣א שָׁמַ֔עְנוּ בְּק֖וֹל יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵ֑ינוּ לָלֶ֤כֶת בְּתֽוֹרֹתָיו֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר נָתַ֣ן לְפָנֵ֔ינוּ בְּיַ֖ד עֲבָדָ֥יו הַנְּבִיאִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for we have not obeyed the voice of the Lord our God, to follow his Torot, which he set before us by his servants the prophets.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And we did not pay heed to the voice of the L-rd our G-d to walk in His teachings that He placed before us by the hand of His servants, the prophets.


###### Daniel 9:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:11)


וְכׇל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל עָֽבְרוּ֙ אֶת־תּ֣וֹרָתֶ֔ךָ וְס֕וֹר לְבִלְתִּ֖י שְׁמ֣וֹעַ בְּקֹלֶ֑ךָ וַתִּתַּ֨ךְ עָלֵ֜ינוּ הָאָלָ֣ה וְהַשְּׁבֻעָ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֤ר כְּתוּבָה֙ בְּתוֹרַת֙ מֹשֶׁ֣ה עֶֽבֶד־הָֽאֱלֹהִ֔ים כִּ֥י חָטָ֖אנוּ לֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yea, all Yisra᾽el have transgressed Thy Tora, and have turned aside, so as not to obey Thy voice; therefore the curse is poured upon us, and the oath that is written in the Tora of Moshe the servant of God, because we have sinned against him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all of Israel transgressed Your Torah and turned away, not to heed Your voice, and there overtook us the curse and the oath written in the Torah of Moses, the servant of G-d [viz. Leviticus 26:14, etc.]


###### Daniel 9:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:12)


וַיָּ֜קֶם אֶת־[דְּבָר֣וֹ**׀**] (דבריו) אֲשֶׁר־דִּבֶּ֣ר עָלֵ֗ינוּ וְעַ֤ל שֹֽׁפְטֵ֙ינוּ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר שְׁפָט֔וּנוּ לְהָבִ֥יא עָלֵ֖ינוּ רָעָ֣ה גְדֹלָ֑ה אֲשֶׁ֣ר לֹֽא־נֶעֶשְׂתָ֗ה תַּ֚חַת כׇּל־הַשָּׁמַ֔יִם כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר נֶעֶשְׂתָ֖ה בִּירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he has confirmed his word, which he spoke against us, and against our judges who judged us, by bringing upon us a great evil: for under the whole heaven there has not been done the like of what has been done against Yerushalayim,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And He fulfilled His word which He spoke concerning us and concerning our judges who judged us, to bring upon us a great evil, which had not been wrought under all the heavens, as has been wrought in Jerusalem.


###### Daniel 9:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:13)


כַּאֲשֶׁ֤ר כָּתוּב֙ בְּתוֹרַ֣ת מֹשֶׁ֔ה אֵ֛ת כׇּל־הָרָעָ֥ה הַזֹּ֖את בָּ֣אָה עָלֵ֑ינוּ וְלֹֽא־חִלִּ֜ינוּ אֶת־פְּנֵ֣י**׀** יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵ֗ינוּ לָשׁוּב֙ מֵֽעֲוֺנֵ֔נוּ וּלְהַשְׂכִּ֖יל בַּאֲמִתֶּֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
as it is written in the Tora of Moshe, all this evil is come upon us: yet we did not offer our prayer before the Lord our God, that we might turn from our iniquities, and understand Thy truth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As written in the Torah of Moses, all this evil has come upon us. And we did not entreat the L-rd to [help us] to repent of our transgressions and to grow wise in Your truth.


###### Daniel 9:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:14)


וַיִּשְׁקֹ֤ד יְהֹוָה֙ עַל־הָ֣רָעָ֔ה וַיְבִיאֶ֖הָ עָלֵ֑ינוּ כִּֽי־צַדִּ֞יק יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵ֗ינוּ עַל־כׇּל־מַֽעֲשָׂיו֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשָׂ֔ה וְלֹ֥א שָׁמַ֖עְנוּ בְּקֹלֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore the Lord has watched over the evil, and brought it upon us: for the Lord our God is righteous in all his works which he has done: for we did not obey his voice.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd hastened with the evil and He brought it upon us. For the L-rd our G-d is righteous in all of His acts which He performs — but we did not heed His voice.


###### Daniel 9:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:15)


וְעַתָּ֣ה**׀** אֲדֹנָ֣י אֱלֹהֵ֗ינוּ אֲשֶׁר֩ הוֹצֵ֨אתָ אֶֽת־עַמְּךָ֜ מֵאֶ֤רֶץ מִצְרַ֙יִם֙ בְּיָ֣ד חֲזָקָ֔ה וַתַּֽעַשׂ־לְךָ֥ שֵׁ֖ם כַּיּ֣וֹם הַזֶּ֑ה חָטָ֖אנוּ רָשָֽׁעְנוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And now, O Lord our God, who hast brought Thy people out of the land of Miżrayim with a mighty hand, and hast gotten Thee renown, as at this day; we have sinned, we have done wickedly.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, O L-rd, our G-d, who took out Your people from the land of Egypt with a strong hand, and made for Yourself a [great] name as of this day, [we confess that] we have sinned [and] done evil.


###### Daniel 9:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:16)


אֲדֹנָ֗י כְּכׇל־צִדְקֹתֶ֙ךָ֙ יָֽשׇׁב־נָ֤א אַפְּךָ֙ וַחֲמָ֣תְךָ֔ מֵעִֽירְךָ֥ יְרוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם הַר־קׇדְשֶׁ֑ךָ כִּ֤י בַחֲטָאֵ֙ינוּ֙ וּבַעֲוֺנ֣וֹת אֲבֹתֵ֔ינוּ יְרוּשָׁלַ֧͏ִם וְעַמְּךָ֛ לְחֶרְפָּ֖ה לְכׇל־סְבִיבֹתֵֽינוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
O Lord, according to all Thy righteousness, I pray Thee, let Thy anger and Thy fury be turned away from Thy city of Yerushalayim Thy holy mountain: because for our sins, and for the iniquities of our fathers, Yerushalayim and Thy people are become a reproach to all that are about us.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
O L-rd, in all of Your righteousness, let Your anger and Your wrath turn away from Your city, Jerusalem, Your holy mountain. For in our sins and in the transgressions of our father, Jerusalem and Your people have become a [target of] insult to all who are around us.


###### Daniel 9:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:17)


וְעַתָּ֣ה**׀** שְׁמַ֣ע אֱלֹהֵ֗ינוּ אֶל־תְּפִלַּ֤ת עַבְדְּךָ֙ וְאֶל־תַּ֣חֲנוּנָ֔יו וְהָאֵ֣ר פָּנֶ֔יךָ עַל־מִקְדָּשְׁךָ֖ הַשָּׁמֵ֑ם לְמַ֖עַן אֲדֹנָֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now therefore, O our God, hear the prayer of Thy servant, and his supplications, and cause Thy face to shine upon Thy sanctuary that is desolate, for the Lord’s sake.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, hear our G-d, the prayer of Your servant and his supplications, and cause Your face to shine upon Your desolate Temple, for the sake of [the name of] "the L-rd," [(which is linked with Your Temple, viz. [Exodus 15:17])].


###### Daniel 9:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:18)


הַטֵּ֨ה אֱלֹהַ֥י׀אׇזְנְךָ֮ וּֽשְׁמָע֒ (פקחה) [פְּקַ֣ח] עֵינֶ֗יךָ וּרְאֵה֙ שֹֽׁמְמֹתֵ֔ינוּ וְהָעִ֕יר אֲשֶׁר־נִקְרָ֥א שִׁמְךָ֖ עָלֶ֑יהָ כִּ֣י**׀** לֹ֣א עַל־צִדְקֹתֵ֗ינוּ אֲנַ֨חְנוּ מַפִּילִ֤ים תַּחֲנוּנֵ֙ינוּ֙ לְפָנֶ֔יךָ כִּ֖י עַל־רַחֲמֶ֥יךָ הָרַבִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
O my God, incline Thy ear, and hear; open Thy eyes, and behold our desolations, and the city which is called by Thy name: for we do not present our supplications before Thee because of our righteousnesses, but because of Thy great mercies.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Incline, O G-d, Your ear, and hear; open Your eyes and see, our desolation and the city upon which Your name is called. For not upon our righteousness do we cast our supplications before You, but upon Your many mercies.


###### Daniel 9:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:19)


אֲדֹנָ֤י׀שְׁמָ֙עָה֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י׀סְלָ֔חָה אֲדֹנָ֛י הַֽקְשִׁ֥יבָה וַעֲשֵׂ֖ה אַל־תְּאַחַ֑ר לְמַֽעַנְךָ֣ אֱלֹהַ֔י כִּֽי־שִׁמְךָ֣ נִקְרָ֔א עַל־עִירְךָ֖ וְעַל־עַמֶּֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
O Lord, hear; O Lord, forgive; O Lord, hearken and act; delay not, for Thy own sake, O my God: for Thy city and Thy people are called by Thy name.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
O L-rd, hear [our supplications]; O L-rd, forgive [our sins]; O L-rd, listen [to our cries], and do [what we request of You]; do not delay. For You, my G-d. for Your name is called upon Your city and upon Your people.


###### Daniel 9:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:20)


וְע֨וֹד אֲנִ֤י מְדַבֵּר֙ וּמִתְפַּלֵּ֔ל וּמִתְוַדֶּה֙ חַטָּאתִ֔י וְחַטַּ֖את עַמִּ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וּמַפִּ֣יל תְּחִנָּתִ֗י לִפְנֵי֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהַ֔י עַ֖ל הַר־קֹ֥דֶשׁ אֱלֹהָֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And whilst I was speaking, and praying, and confessing my sin and the sin of my people Yisra᾽el, and presenting my supplication before the Lord my God for the holy mountain of my God;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And while I was yet speaking and praying and confessing my sin and the sin of my people Israel, and casting my plaint before the L-rd my G-d for the mountain of the Temple of my G-d,


###### Daniel 9:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:21)


וְע֛וֹד אֲנִ֥י מְדַבֵּ֖ר בַּתְּפִלָּ֑ה וְהָאִ֣ישׁ גַּבְרִיאֵ֡ל אֲשֶׁר֩ רָאִ֨יתִי בֶחָז֤וֹן בַּתְּחִלָּה֙ מֻעָ֣ף בִּיעָ֔ף נֹגֵ֣עַ אֵלַ֔י כְּעֵ֖ת מִנְחַת־עָֽרֶב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
whilst I was still speaking in prayer, the man Gavri᾽el, whom I had seen in the vision at the beginning, approached close to me in swift flight about the time of the evening sacrifice.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And while I was yet speaking in prayer, the man, Gabriel, whom I had seen in the vision in the beginning [(in the days of Belshazzar)], came flying (and) touched me at the time of the evening offering.


###### Daniel 9:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:22)


וַיָּ֖בֶן וַיְדַבֵּ֣ר עִמִּ֑י וַיֹּאמַ֕ר דָּנִיֵּ֕אל עַתָּ֥ה יָצָ֖אתִי לְהַשְׂכִּילְךָ֥ בִינָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he made me understand, and talked with me, and said, O Daniyyel, I am now come forth to give thee skill and understanding.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he made me understand, and he spoke with me. And he said: I have now gone forth to teach you understanding.


###### Daniel 9:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:23)


בִּתְחִלַּ֨ת תַּחֲנוּנֶ֜יךָ יָצָ֣א דָבָ֗ר וַאֲנִי֙ בָּ֣אתִי לְהַגִּ֔יד כִּ֥י חֲמוּד֖וֹת אָ֑תָּה וּבִין֙ בַּדָּבָ֔ר וְהָבֵ֖ן בַּמַּרְאֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
At the beginning of thy supplications the commandment went out, and I am come to declare it; for thou art greatly beloved: therefore look into the word, and consider the vision.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the beginning of your supplications the word [of truth] went forth [from the L-rd], and I have come to tell (it to you), for you are desirable [to the L-rd]. And consider the thing and understand the vision.


###### Daniel 9:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:24)


שָׁבֻעִ֨ים שִׁבְעִ֜ים נֶחְתַּ֥ךְ עַֽל־עַמְּךָ֣**׀** וְעַל־עִ֣יר קׇדְשֶׁ֗ךָ לְכַלֵּ֨א הַפֶּ֜שַׁע (ולחתם) [וּלְהָתֵ֤ם] (חטאות) [חַטָּאת֙] וּלְכַפֵּ֣ר עָוֺ֔ן וּלְהָבִ֖יא צֶ֣דֶק עֹֽלָמִ֑ים וְלַחְתֹּם֙ חָז֣וֹן וְנָבִ֔יא וְלִמְשֹׁ֖חַ קֹ֥דֶשׁ קׇֽדָשִֽׁים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Seventy weeks are decreed concerning thy people and concerning thy holy city, to finish the transgression, and to make an end to sins, and to atone for iniquity, and to bring in everlasting righteousness, and to seal up vision and prophet, and to anoint the most holy place.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Seventy sevens [i.e., 490 years] have been decreed upon your people and upon your holy city [(from the destruction of the first Temple in the days of Tzidkiyahu until the destruction of the second Temple — seventy years in the Babylonian exile and four hundred twenty in the second Temple —)], to consummate [purging of] the offense and to end and atone [their] transgressions [(in the exile of Titus and in subjugation)], in order to bring eternal righteousness and to consummate [the burdens of] vision and prophet, and to anoint the holy of holies [(the ark, the altars, and the holy vessels, which will be brought to them by the king, Messiah.)]


###### Daniel 9:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:25)


וְתֵדַ֨ע וְתַשְׂכֵּ֜ל מִן־מֹצָ֣א דָבָ֗ר לְהָשִׁיב֙ וְלִבְנ֤וֹת יְרֽוּשָׁלַ֙͏ִם֙ עַד־מָשִׁ֣יחַ נָגִ֔יד שָׁבֻעִ֖ים שִׁבְעָ֑ה וְשָׁבֻעִ֞ים שִׁשִּׁ֣ים וּשְׁנַ֗יִם תָּשׁוּב֙ וְנִבְנְתָה֙ רְח֣וֹב וְחָר֔וּץ וּבְצ֖וֹק הָעִתִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Know therefore and understand, that from the going forth of the commandment to restore and to build Yerushalayim until an anointed prince, shall be seven weeks: then for sixty two weeks it shall be built again, with squares and moat, but in a troubled time.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And know and understand from this word that has gone forth, to restore and to rebuild Jerusalem. [From the destruction of the first Temple] until [the coming of the anointed prince [(Koresh, king of Paras [viz. Isaiah 45:1])] seven sevens [i.e., seven shemitah periods will elapse]. And for sixty-two sevens [(434 years)], [the city] will be restored and rebuilt, with its streets and its entrenchments. And they will be troubled times [(in subservience to the kings of Paras)].


###### Daniel 9:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:26)


וְאַחֲרֵ֤י הַשָּׁבֻעִים֙ שִׁשִּׁ֣ים וּשְׁנַ֔יִם יִכָּרֵ֥ת מָשִׁ֖יחַ וְאֵ֣ין ל֑וֹ וְהָעִ֨יר וְהַקֹּ֜דֶשׁ יַ֠שְׁחִ֠ית עַ֣ם נָגִ֤יד הַבָּא֙ וְקִצּ֣וֹ בַשֶּׁ֔טֶף וְעַד֙ קֵ֣ץ מִלְחָמָ֔ה נֶחֱרֶ֖צֶת שֹׁמֵמֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And after sixty two weeks shall an anointed one be cut off, and none will be left to him: and the people of a prince that shall come shall destroy the city and the sanctuary; and his end shall be with a flood, and to the end of the war desolations are decreed.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And after the sixty-two sevens, the anointed one [(Agrippas, king of Judah, who reigned at the time of the destruction)] will be cut off and not be. And the city and the Temple will be destroyed by the people [i.e., the legions] of the prince [(Titus)] who comes [against them]. And his end [will be] to be swept away [(by the Messiah)], and until the end of the war [(of Gog and Magog, the city will be a trench of desolation)].


###### Daniel 9:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/9:27)


וְהִגְבִּ֥יר בְּרִ֛ית לָרַבִּ֖ים שָׁב֣וּעַ אֶחָ֑ד וַחֲצִ֨י הַשָּׁב֜וּעַ יַשְׁבִּ֣ית**׀** זֶ֣בַח וּמִנְחָ֗ה וְעַ֨ל כְּנַ֤ף שִׁקּוּצִים֙ מְשֹׁמֵ֔ם וְעַד־כָּלָה֙ וְנֶ֣חֱרָצָ֔ה תִּתַּ֖ךְ עַל־שֹׁמֵֽם׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall make a strong covenant with many for one week: and during half of the week he shall cause the sacrifice and the offering to cease; and upon the wing of abominations shall come one who makes desolate, until the decreed destruction is poured out on the desolator.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he [(Titus)] shall forge a covenant [of peace] with the chiefs [of Israel] for one seven-year period]; but in half of that seven he will abolish sacrifice and meal-offering [viz. 8:25]. And on the height of abominations, [he shall place] "the mute [offense" (idolatry, viz. 8:13)]. And it, [(the dominion of abomination, shall endure)] until [the days of the Messiah, when] complete destruction will descend upon "the mute" [i.e., upon idolatry and its worshippers].



## Chapter 10

###### Daniel 10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:1)


בִּשְׁנַ֣ת שָׁל֗וֹשׁ לְכ֙וֹרֶשׁ֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ פָּרַ֔ס דָּבָר֙ נִגְלָ֣ה לְדָֽנִיֵּ֔אל אֲשֶׁר־נִקְרָ֥א שְׁמ֖וֹ בֵּלְטְאשַׁצַּ֑ר וֶאֱמֶ֤ת הַדָּבָר֙ וְצָבָ֣א גָד֔וֹל וּבִין֙ אֶת־הַדָּבָ֔ר וּבִ֥ינָה ל֖וֹ בַּמַּרְאֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In the third year of Koresh king of Paras a thing was revealed to Daniyyel, whose name was called Belteshażżar; and the word was true, and for a long period ahead; and he understood the thing, and had understanding of the vision.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the third year of Koresh, king of Paras, a thing was revealed to Daniel, who was called Belshazzar. And the thing is true, and in a long time [(it will materialize)]. And to understand the thing [(the vision was revealed to me)], to understand it through the vision.


###### Daniel 10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:2)


בַּיָּמִ֖ים הָהֵ֑ם אֲנִ֤י דָֽנִיֵּאל֙ הָיִ֣יתִי מִתְאַבֵּ֔ל שְׁלֹשָׁ֥ה שָׁבֻעִ֖ים יָמִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In those days I Daniyyel was mourning three full weeks.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In those days, I, Daniel, was in mourning [(over the cessation of the rebuilding of the Temple, viz. Ezra 4:24)], twenty-one years.


###### Daniel 10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:3)


לֶ֣חֶם חֲמֻד֞וֹת לֹ֣א אָכַ֗לְתִּי וּבָשָׂ֥ר וָיַ֛יִן לֹא־בָ֥א אֶל־פִּ֖י וְס֣וֹךְ לֹא־סָ֑כְתִּי עַד־מְלֹ֕את שְׁלֹ֥שֶׁת שָׁבֻעִ֖ים יָמִֽים׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I ate no pleasant bread, nor did meat or wine come into my mouth, nor did I anoint myself at all, till three whole weeks were fulfilled.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Clean bread I did not eat, and meat and wine did not enter my mouth, and I did not anoint myself until the completion of twenty-three years.


###### Daniel 10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:4)


וּבְי֛וֹם עֶשְׂרִ֥ים וְאַרְבָּעָ֖ה לַחֹ֣דֶשׁ הָרִאשׁ֑וֹן וַאֲנִ֗י הָיִ֛יתִי עַ֣ל יַ֧ד הַנָּהָ֛ר הַגָּד֖וֹל ה֥וּא חִדָּֽקֶל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And on the twenty fourth day of the first month, as I was by the side of the great river, which is Ḥiddeqel;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And on the twenty-fourth day of the first month, I was near the great river, Chidekel.


###### Daniel 10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:5)


וָאֶשָּׂ֤א אֶת־עֵינַי֙ וָאֵ֔רֶא וְהִנֵּ֥ה אִישׁ־אֶחָ֖ד לָב֣וּשׁ בַּדִּ֑ים וּמׇתְנָ֥יו חֲגֻרִ֖ים בְּכֶ֥תֶם אוּפָֽז׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I lifted up my eyes, and looked, and behold a certain man clothed in linen, whose loins were girded with fine gold of Ufaz:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I lifted my eyes and I saw, and, behold, a certain man dressed in linen, and his thighs were girded with [a belt of] fine gold.


###### Daniel 10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:6)


וּגְוִיָּת֣וֹ כְתַרְשִׁ֗ישׁ וּפָנָ֞יו כְּמַרְאֵ֤ה בָרָק֙ וְעֵינָיו֙ כְּלַפִּ֣ידֵי אֵ֔שׁ וּזְרֹֽעֹתָיו֙ וּמַרְגְּלֹתָ֔יו כְּעֵ֖ין נְחֹ֣שֶׁת קָלָ֑ל וְק֥וֹל דְּבָרָ֖יו כְּק֥וֹל הָמֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
his body also was like the beryl, and his face like the appearance of lightning, and his eyes like torches of fire, and his arms and his feet in colour like burnished brass, and the sound of his words like the voice of a multitude.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And his body was as [vast as the sea of] Tarshish, and his face (was) like the appearance of lightning, and his eyes like torches of fire, and his arms and feet like burnished brass, and the sound of his words like the sound of a [distant] multitude.


###### Daniel 10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:7)


וְרָאִ֩יתִי֩ אֲנִ֨י דָנִיֵּ֤אל לְבַדִּי֙ אֶת־הַמַּרְאָ֔ה וְהָאֲנָשִׁים֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר הָי֣וּ עִמִּ֔י לֹ֥א רָא֖וּ אֶת־הַמַּרְאָ֑ה אֲבָ֗ל חֲרָדָ֤ה גְדֹלָה֙ נָפְלָ֣ה עֲלֵיהֶ֔ם וַֽיִּבְרְח֖וּ בְּהֵחָבֵֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I Daniyyel alone saw the vision: for the men who were with me did not see the vision; but a great trembling fell upon them, so that they fled to hide themselves.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I saw — I, Daniel, alone — the vision. And the men who were with me [(Chananiah, Mishael, and Azaryah)] did not see the vision; but a great fear fell upon them and they fled to hiding.


###### Daniel 10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:8)


וַאֲנִי֙ נִשְׁאַ֣רְתִּי לְבַדִּ֔י וָֽאֶרְאֶ֗ה אֶת־הַמַּרְאָ֤ה הַגְּדֹלָה֙ הַזֹּ֔את וְלֹ֥א נִשְׁאַר־בִּ֖י כֹּ֑חַ וְהוֹדִ֗י נֶהְפַּ֤ךְ עָלַי֙ לְמַשְׁחִ֔ית וְלֹ֥א עָצַ֖רְתִּי כֹּֽחַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So I was left alone, and saw this great vision, and there remained no strength in me: for my comely appearance was horribly changed, and I retained no strength.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I remained alone, and I saw this great vision, and no strength remained in me, and my beauty turned to ruin, and I could not summon strength.


###### Daniel 10:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:9)


וָאֶשְׁמַ֖ע אֶת־ק֣וֹל דְּבָרָ֑יו וּכְשׇׁמְעִי֙ אֶת־ק֣וֹל דְּבָרָ֔יו וַאֲנִ֗י הָיִ֛יתִי נִרְדָּ֥ם עַל־פָּנַ֖י וּפָנַ֥י אָֽרְצָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yet I heard the sound of his words: and when I heard the voice of his words, I was in a deep sleep on my face, and my face towards the ground.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I heard the sound of his words. And when I heard the sound of his words, I foundered on my face with my face to the ground.


###### Daniel 10:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:10)


וְהִנֵּה־יָ֖ד נָ֣גְעָה בִּ֑י וַתְּנִיעֵ֥נִי עַל־בִּרְכַּ֖י וְכַפּ֥וֹת יָדָֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And, behold, a hand touched me, which set me trembling on my knees and on the palms of my hands.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And, behold, a hand touched me and roused me to my knees and the palms of my hands.


###### Daniel 10:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:11)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלַ֡י דָּנִיֵּ֣אל אִישׁ־חֲ֠מֻד֠וֹת הָבֵ֨ן בַּדְּבָרִ֜ים אֲשֶׁר֩ אָנֹכִ֨י דֹבֵ֤ר אֵלֶ֙יךָ֙ וַעֲמֹ֣ד עַל־עׇמְדֶ֔ךָ כִּ֥י עַתָּ֖ה שֻׁלַּ֣חְתִּי אֵלֶ֑יךָ וּבְדַבְּר֥וֹ עִמִּ֛י אֶת־הַדָּבָ֥ר הַזֶּ֖ה עָמַ֥דְתִּי מַרְעִֽיד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he said to me, O Daniyyel, thou man greatly beloved, understand the words that I speak to thee, and stand upright: for to thee am I now sent. And when he had spoken this word to me, I stood trembling.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he said to me: Daniel, man of purity, understand the things that I speak to you and stand in your place, for I have now been sent to you [by G-d]. And when he spoke this thing to me, I stood up trembling.


###### Daniel 10:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:12)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלַי֮ אַל־תִּירָ֣א דָנִיֵּאל֒ כִּ֣י**׀** מִן־הַיּ֣וֹם הָרִאשׁ֗וֹן אֲשֶׁ֨ר נָתַ֧תָּ אֶֽת־לִבְּךָ֛ לְהָבִ֧ין וּלְהִתְעַנּ֛וֹת לִפְנֵ֥י אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ נִשְׁמְע֣וּ דְבָרֶ֑יךָ וַאֲנִי־בָ֖אתִי בִּדְבָרֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then said he to me, Fear not, Daniyyel: for from the first day that thou didst set thy heart to understand and to humble thyself before thy God, thy words were heard, and I am come because of thy words.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he said to me: Do not fear, Daniel. For from the first day that you set your heart upon understanding and fasting before your G-d, your words have been heard [and accepted], and I have come [(on this embassy)] because of your words.


###### Daniel 10:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:13)


וְשַׂ֣ר**׀** מַלְכ֣וּת פָּרַ֗ס עֹמֵ֤ד לְנֶגְדִּי֙ עֶשְׂרִ֣ים וְאֶחָ֣ד י֔וֹם וְהִנֵּ֣ה מִֽיכָאֵ֗ל אַחַ֛ד הַשָּׂרִ֥ים הָרִאשֹׁנִ֖ים בָּ֣א לְעׇזְרֵ֑נִי וַאֲנִי֙ נוֹתַ֣רְתִּי שָׁ֔ם אֵ֖צֶל מַלְכֵ֥י פָרָֽס׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the guardian angel of the kingdom of Paras withstood me twenty one days: but, lo, Mikha᾽el, one of the chief angels, came to help me; and I remained there with the kings of Paras.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the prince [(the tutelary angel)] of the kingdom of Paras stood against me for twenty-one days [(seeking to extend the reign of Paras over Israel)], and, behold, Michael, one of the foremost princes, came to my aid, and I remained there against the kings of Paras [(to silence the heavenly prince of Paras)].


###### Daniel 10:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:14)


וּבָ֙אתִי֙ לַהֲבִ֣ינְךָ֔ אֵ֛ת אֲשֶׁר־יִקְרָ֥ה לְעַמְּךָ֖ בְּאַחֲרִ֣ית הַיָּמִ֑ים כִּי־ע֥וֹד חָז֖וֹן לַיָּמִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now I am come to make thee understand what shall befall thy people in the latter days: for the vision is for days yet to come.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I have come to make you understand what will transpire with your people in the end of days, for there is yet a vision [(to tell you what you have not yet been told, and it will materialize)] within the [many] days [foretold for the appointed time].


###### Daniel 10:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:15)


וּבְדַבְּר֣וֹ עִמִּ֔י כַּדְּבָרִ֖ים הָאֵ֑לֶּה נָתַ֧תִּי פָנַ֛י אַ֖רְצָה וְנֶאֱלָֽמְתִּי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when he had spoken such words to me, I set my face towards the ground, and I became dumb.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when he spoke such words to me, I turned my face to the ground and I was mute.


###### Daniel 10:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:16)


וְהִנֵּ֗ה כִּדְמוּת֙ בְּנֵ֣י אָדָ֔ם נֹגֵ֖עַ עַל־שְׂפָתָ֑י וָאֶפְתַּח־פִּ֗י וָאֲדַבְּרָה֙ וָאֹֽמְרָה֙ אֶל־הָעֹמֵ֣ד לְנֶגְדִּ֔י אֲדֹנִ֗י בַּמַּרְאָה֙ נֶהֶפְכ֤וּ צִירַי֙ עָלַ֔י וְלֹ֥א עָצַ֖רְתִּי כֹּֽחַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And, behold, one in the likeness of the sons of men touched my lips: then I opened my mouth, and spoke, and said to him that stood before me, O my lord, by reason of the vision my pains have come upon me, and I retain no strength.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And, behold, a human likeness touching my lips. And I opened my mouth and spoke, and I said to the one [(the angel)] standing before me: My lord, in the vision, my limb-tips overturned upon me, and I could not summon up strength.


###### Daniel 10:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:17)


וְהֵ֣יךְ יוּכַ֗ל עֶ֤בֶד אֲדֹנִי֙ זֶ֔ה לְדַבֵּ֖ר עִם־אֲדֹ֣נִי זֶ֑ה וַאֲנִ֤י מֵעַ֙תָּה֙ לֹֽא־יַעֲמׇד־בִּ֣י כֹ֔חַ וּנְשָׁמָ֖ה לֹ֥א נִשְׁאֲרָה־בִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For how can the servant of my lord talk with this my lord? for as for me, there remains now no strength in me, nor is there breath left in me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And how could [I,] the servant of this, my lord [i.e., you,] speak with this, my lord, when [even] now [(when you have assumed human form)] no strength remains in me and my soul is not left in me!


###### Daniel 10:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:18)


וַיֹּ֧סֶף וַיִּגַּע־בִּ֛י כְּמַרְאֵ֥ה אָדָ֖ם וַֽיְחַזְּקֵֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then again came one in the likeness of a man, and touched me and strengthened me,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he touched me again, while [yet] in human form, and he strengthened me.


###### Daniel 10:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:19)


וַיֹּ֜אמֶר אַל־תִּירָ֧א אִישׁ־חֲמֻד֛וֹת שָׁל֥וֹם לָ֖ךְ חֲזַ֣ק וַחֲזָ֑ק וּֽכְדַבְּר֤וֹ עִמִּי֙ הִתְחַזַּ֔קְתִּי וָאֹ֥מְרָ֛ה יְדַבֵּ֥ר אֲדֹנִ֖י כִּ֥י חִזַּקְתָּֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and said, O man greatly beloved, fear not: peace be unto thee, be strong, and be resolute. And when he had spoken to me, I was strengthened, and said, Let my lord speak; for thou hast strengthened me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he said: Do not fear, man of purity — Peace unto you! Strengthen and strengthen yourself! And when he spoke with me, I strengthened myself, and I said: Let my lord speak, for you have strengthened me.


###### Daniel 10:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:20)


וַיֹּ֗אמֶר הֲיָדַ֙עְתָּ֙ לָמָּה־בָּ֣אתִי אֵלֶ֔יךָ וְעַתָּ֣ה אָשׁ֔וּב לְהִלָּחֵ֖ם עִם־שַׂ֣ר פָּרָ֑ס וַאֲנִ֣י יוֹצֵ֔א וְהִנֵּ֥ה שַׂר־יָוָ֖ן בָּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then he said to me, Dost thou know why I have come to thee? But now I will return to fight with the guardian angel of Paras: and when I depart from him, lo, the guardian angel of Yavan shall come.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he said: [Now] do you know why I have come to you? And now, I shall return to war against the prince of Paras [and I shall prevail], but I shall leave [(when the time for the reign of Antiochus arrives)], and, behold, the prince of Greece shall come [(and the subjugation of Israel by Greece will be far more severe than their subjugation by Paras had been)].


###### Daniel 10:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/10:21)


אֲבָל֙ אַגִּ֣יד לְךָ֔ אֶת־הָרָשׁ֥וּם בִּכְתָ֖ב אֱמֶ֑ת וְאֵ֨ין אֶחָ֜ד מִתְחַזֵּ֤ק עִמִּי֙ עַל־אֵ֔לֶּה כִּ֥י אִם־מִיכָאֵ֖ל שַׂרְכֶֽם׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But I will tell thee that which is inscribed in the true record: and none stands fast with me against these, except your guardian angel, Mikha᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But I shall tell you what is written in the [irrevocable] writing of truth. And no one [(among all the heavenly princes)] strengthened himself with me against these except Michael, your prince.



## Chapter 11

###### Daniel 11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:1)


וַאֲנִי֙ בִּשְׁנַ֣ת אַחַ֔ת לְדָרְיָ֖וֶשׁ הַמָּדִ֑י עׇמְדִ֛י לְמַחֲזִ֥יק וּלְמָע֖וֹז לֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As for me, in the first year of Daryavesh the Madian, I stood up to confirm and to strengthen him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I, [(Gabriel)] in the first year of Darius the Mede, stood up as a strengthener and a stronghold for him [(Michael)]. [(With the fall of the kingdom of Bavel, when the kingdom of Madai and Paras petitioned on high to strengthen their yoke over you, I, Gabriel came to the assistance of Michael, your heavenly intercessor)].


###### Daniel 11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:2)


וְעַתָּ֕ה אֱמֶ֖ת אַגִּ֣יד לָ֑ךְ הִנֵּה־עוֹד֩ שְׁלֹשָׁ֨ה מְלָכִ֜ים עֹמְדִ֣ים לְפָרַ֗ס וְהָֽרְבִיעִי֙ יַעֲשִׁ֤יר עֹֽשֶׁר־גָּדוֹל֙ מִכֹּ֔ל וּכְחֶזְקָת֣וֹ בְעׇשְׁר֔וֹ יָעִ֣יר הַכֹּ֔ל אֵ֖ת מַלְכ֥וּת יָוָֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And now will I tell thee the truth. Behold, there shall stand up yet three kings in Paras: and the fourth shall be far richer than all of them: and when he has grown strong through his riches, he shall stir up all against the kingdom of Yavan.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, I will tell you the truth. Behold, three more kings will arise for Paras [(Koresh, Achashverosh, and the Darius who built the Temple)], and the fourth [(the latter Darius, fourth in the succession of Madai)], will accumulate great wealth, more than all [the others], and, growing strong in his wealth, he will rouse all [his kingdom to war] with the kingdom of Yavan (Greece).


###### Daniel 11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:3)


וְעָמַ֖ד מֶ֣לֶךְ גִּבּ֑וֹר וּמָשַׁל֙ מִמְשָׁ֣ל רַ֔ב וְעָשָׂ֖ה כִּרְצוֹנֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And a mighty king shall stand up, who shall rule with great dominion, and do according to his will.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And there will arise a strong king [(Alexander)], whose dominion will be great, and he will do as he desires [(with Darius, king of Paras. He will kill him and take his kingdom, and Paras will become subservient to Yavan)].


###### Daniel 11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:4)


וּכְעׇמְדוֹ֙ תִּשָּׁבֵ֣ר מַלְכוּת֔וֹ וְתֵחָ֕ץ לְאַרְבַּ֖ע רוּח֣וֹת הַשָּׁמָ֑יִם וְלֹ֣א לְאַחֲרִית֗וֹ וְלֹ֤א כְמׇשְׁלוֹ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר מָשָׁ֔ל כִּ֤י תִנָּתֵשׁ֙ מַלְכוּת֔וֹ וְלַאֲחֵרִ֖ים מִלְּבַד־אֵֽלֶּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when he shall stand up, his kingdom shall be broken, and shall be divided towards the four winds of heaven; and not to his posterity, nor according to his dominion with which he ruled: for his kingdom shall be plucked up, and go to others besides those.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when he rises [i.e., at the height of his power], his kingdom will be broken, and it will be divided to the four ends of heaven, but not to his sons, and not in [the strength of] the reign wherein he reigned. For his kingdom will be uprooted [into four parts], and to others aside from these [four].


###### Daniel 11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:5)


וְיֶחֱזַ֥ק מֶֽלֶךְ־הַנֶּ֖גֶב וּמִן־שָׂרָ֑יו וְיֶחֱזַ֤ק עָלָיו֙ וּמָשָׁ֔ל מִמְשָׁ֥ל רַ֖ב מֶמְשַׁלְתּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the king of the south shall be strong, but one of his princes shall be stronger than he, and have dominion; his dominion shall be a great dominion.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king of the south will grow stronger [than the king of the north] and than his chiefs, and he will overpower him and rule. And he will exercise great sway in his dominion.


###### Daniel 11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:6)


וּלְקֵ֤ץ שָׁנִים֙ יִתְחַבָּ֔רוּ וּבַ֣ת מֶֽלֶךְ־הַנֶּ֗גֶב תָּבוֹא֙ אֶל־מֶ֣לֶךְ הַצָּפ֔וֹן לַעֲשׂ֖וֹת מֵישָׁרִ֑ים וְלֹֽא־תַעְצֹ֞ר כּ֣וֹחַ הַזְּר֗וֹעַ וְלֹ֤א יַעֲמֹד֙ וּזְרֹע֔וֹ וְתִנָּתֵ֨ן הִ֤יא וּמְבִיאֶ֙יהָ֙ וְהַיֹּ֣לְדָ֔הּ וּמַחֲזִקָ֖הּ בָּעִתִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And at the end of years they shall join themselves together; for the king’s daughter of the south shall come to the king of the north to make an agreement: but she shall not retain the power of her arm; nor shall he stand, nor his arm: but she shall be given up, and they who brought her, and he who begot her, and he who strengthened her in those times.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And after many years, they will unite, and the daughter of the king of the south will come to the king of the north to make peace [(between him and her father.)] And they [(her attendants)] will not be able to summon up strength [(to oppose him)]. Nor will he [(her father)] nor his warriors [(be able to oppose him)]. And she will be delivered — she, her attendants and her begetter [i.e., her father] and her supporter [i.e., her counselor] for the times [i.e., for contingencies].


###### Daniel 11:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:7)


וְעָמַ֛ד מִנֵּ֥צֶר שׇׁרָשֶׁ֖יהָ כַּנּ֑וֹ וְיָבֹ֣א אֶל־הַחַ֗יִל וְיָבֹא֙ בְּמָעוֹז֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ הַצָּפ֔וֹן וְעָשָׂ֥ה בָהֶ֖ם וְהֶחֱזִֽיק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But from a branch of her roots shall one stand up in his place: he shall come with an army, and shall enter into the fortress of the king of the north, and shall deal against them, and shall prevail:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And there shall arise from the branch of her roots [a son] sitting on his [(her father's)] throne, and he shall come against the army [(of the king of the north)], and he will enter the stronghold of the king of the north, and he will succeed against them, and he will vanquish (them).


###### Daniel 11:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:8)


וְגַ֣ם אֱֽלֹהֵיהֶ֡ם עִם־נְסִֽכֵיהֶם֩ עִם־כְּלֵ֨י חֶמְדָּתָ֜ם כֶּ֧סֶף וְזָהָ֛ב בַּשְּׁבִ֖י יָבִ֣א מִצְרָ֑יִם וְהוּא֙ שָׁנִ֣ים יַעֲמֹ֔ד מִמֶּ֖לֶךְ הַצָּפֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he shall also carry captives into Miżrayim their gods, with their princes, and with their precious vessels of silver and of gold; and he shall abstain for some years from attacking the king of the north.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And also their gods and their princes and their precious vessels of silver and gold will he bring in captivity to Egypt, and he will reign more years [in that kingdom] than did the king of the north.


###### Daniel 11:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:9)


וּבָ֗א בְּמַלְכוּת֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ הַנֶּ֔גֶב וְשָׁ֖ב אֶל־אַדְמָתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But he shall come into the kingdom of the king of the south, and shall return to his own land.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he will come through the kingdom of the king of the south [(her father's father)], and he will return to his land [(Egypt)].


###### Daniel 11:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:10)


וּבָנָ֣ו יִתְגָּר֗וּ וְאָסְפוּ֙ הֲמוֹן֙ חֲיָלִ֣ים רַבִּ֔ים וּבָ֥א ב֖וֹא וְשָׁטַ֣ף וְעָבָ֑ר וְיָשֹׁ֥ב (ויתגרו) [וְיִתְגָּרֶ֖ה] עַד־מָעֻזֹּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But his sons shall be stirred up, and shall assemble a multitude of great forces: and one shall certainly come, and overflow, and pass through: then shall he return, and be stirred up, even to his fortress.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And his [(the northern king's)] son will rise up and gather a multitude of great armies and he will come sweeping through [to the land of the south], and he will penetrate until his [(the southern king's)] stronghold.


###### Daniel 11:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:11)


וְיִתְמַרְמַר֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ הַנֶּ֔גֶב וְיָצָ֕א וְנִלְחַ֥ם עִמּ֖וֹ עִם־מֶ֣לֶךְ הַצָּפ֑וֹן וְהֶעֱמִיד֙ הָמ֣וֹן רָ֔ב וְנִתַּ֥ן הֶהָמ֖וֹן בְּיָדֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the king of the south shall be moved with anger, and shall come out and fight with him, with the king of the north: and he shall set forth a great multitude; but the multitude shall be given into his hand.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king of the south will be embittered, and he will go out and war with him, with the king of the north, and he will mass a great multitude against him, and the multitude will be delivered into his [(the southern king's)] hand.


###### Daniel 11:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:12)


וְנִשָּׂ֥א הֶהָמ֖וֹן (ירום) [וְרָ֣ם] לְבָב֑וֹ וְהִפִּ֥יל רִבֹּא֖וֹת וְלֹ֥א יָעֽוֹז׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when he has taken away the multitude, his heart shall be lifted up; and he shall cast down tens of thousands: but he shall not prevail.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the northern multitude will rouse themselves in pride of heart and overthrow tens of thousands [of the southern king's army], but [still] they will not prevail.


###### Daniel 11:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:13)


וְשָׁב֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ הַצָּפ֔וֹן וְהֶעֱמִ֣יד הָמ֔וֹן רַ֖ב מִן־הָרִאשׁ֑וֹן וּלְקֵ֨ץ הָֽעִתִּ֤ים שָׁנִים֙ יָ֣בוֹא ב֔וֹא בְּחַ֥יִל גָּד֖וֹל וּבִרְכ֥וּשׁ רָֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the king of the north shall again set forth a multitude greater than the former, and shall certainly come after certain years with a great army and with abundant supplies.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king of the north shall return and mass a multitude greater than the first, and after many years, he will come with a vast army, heavily supplied.


###### Daniel 11:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:14)


וּבָעִתִּ֣ים הָהֵ֔ם רַבִּ֥ים יַֽעַמְד֖וּ עַל־מֶ֣לֶךְ הַנֶּ֑גֶב וּבְנֵ֣י**׀** פָּרִיצֵ֣י עַמְּךָ֗ יִֽנַּשְּׂא֛וּ לְהַעֲמִ֥יד חָז֖וֹן וְנִכְשָֽׁלוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And in those times there shall many stand up against the king of the south: also the renegades of thy people shall exalt themselves to fulfil the vision; but they shall stumble.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And in those times, many will rise up against the king of the south, and the sons of the rebels of your people will aspire to establish the vision [(according to their interpretation)], and they will fail.


###### Daniel 11:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:15)


וְיָבֹא֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ הַצָּפ֔וֹן וְיִשְׁפֹּךְ֙ סֽוֹלְלָ֔ה וְלָכַ֖ד עִ֣יר מִבְצָר֑וֹת וּזְרֹע֤וֹת הַנֶּ֙גֶב֙ לֹ֣א יַעֲמֹ֔דוּ וְעַם֙ מִבְחָרָ֔יו וְאֵ֥ין כֹּ֖חַ לַעֲמֹֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So the king of the north shall come, and cast up a mound, and seize a well fortified city and the arms of the south shall not withstand, nor his chosen people, for they shall have no strength to withstand.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king of the north will come and spill out a siege-mound and take a fortified city. And the southern strength will fail. And [though] his chosen men [be with the kings of the south], he will be unable to withstand [the forces of the north.]


###### Daniel 11:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:16)


וְיַ֨עַשׂ הַבָּ֤א אֵלָיו֙ כִּרְצוֹנ֔וֹ וְאֵ֥ין עוֹמֵ֖ד לְפָנָ֑יו וְיַעֲמֹ֥ד בְּאֶֽרֶץ־הַצְּבִ֖י וְכָלָ֥ה בְיָדֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But he who comes against him shall do according to his own will, and none shall stand before him: and he shall stand in the land of beauty, and in his hand shall be destruction.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he [(the king of the north)] who comes against him will do [with him] as he wills, and none will stand against him, and he will stand in Eretz Hatzvi [(Eretz Yisrael)], and he will destroy (it) with his forces.


###### Daniel 11:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:17)


וְיָשֵׂ֣ם׀פָּ֠נָ֠יו לָב֞וֹא בְּתֹ֧קֶף כׇּל־מַלְכוּת֛וֹ וִישָׁרִ֥ים עִמּ֖וֹ וְעָשָׂ֑ה וּבַ֤ת הַנָּשִׁים֙ יִתֶּן־ל֣וֹ לְהַשְׁחִיתָ֔הּ וְלֹ֥א תַעֲמֹ֖ד וְלֹא־ל֥וֹ תִהְיֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He shall also set his face to enter with the strength of his whole kingdom, but he shall make peace with him: and he shall give him the daughter of women, to destroy it: but she shall not stand or remain his.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he will set his face to come against the strength of his entire kingdom, and (against) the just [(those of Israel)] who are with him, and he will prevail. And he [(the king of the north, Antiochus)] will give to him [(his commander in chief, Polypus)] the daughter of women [i.e., "the choicest of women," the congregation of Israel] to destroy her; but it [(his counsel)] will not prevail, and she will not be his [(for Matithyahu the son of Yochanan will rescue her.)]


###### Daniel 11:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:18)


(וישב) [וְיָשֵׂ֧ם]׀פָּנָ֛יו לְאִיִּ֖ים וְלָכַ֣ד רַבִּ֑ים וְהִשְׁבִּ֨ית קָצִ֤ין חֶרְפָּתוֹ֙ ל֔וֹ בִּלְתִּ֥י חֶרְפָּת֖וֹ יָשִׁ֥יב לֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
After this he shall turn his face to the isles, and shall take many: but a commander shall put an end to his insult; indeed, he shall cause his insult to return upon him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he will turn his face to the isles, and he will take many. And the Chief will end his blasphemies against Him [and Israel]. He will requite unto him more than his blasphemies.


###### Daniel 11:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:19)


וְיָשֵׁ֣ב פָּנָ֔יו לְמָעוּזֵּ֖י אַרְצ֑וֹ וְנִכְשַׁ֥ל וְנָפַ֖ל וְלֹ֥א יִמָּצֵֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then he shall turn his face towards the strongholds of his own land: but he shall stumble and fall, and not be found.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he will turn his face to [return to] the strongholds of his land, and he will stumble and fall and no longer be found [on earth].


###### Daniel 11:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:20)


וְעָמַ֧ד עַל־כַּנּ֛וֹ מַעֲבִ֥יר נוֹגֵ֖שׂ הֶ֣דֶר מַלְכ֑וּת וּבְיָמִ֤ים אֲחָדִים֙ יִשָּׁבֵ֔ר וְלֹ֥א בְאַפַּ֖יִם וְלֹ֥א בְמִלְחָמָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then there shall stand up in his place one who shall send an exactor of taxes throughout glory of the kingdom, but within a few days he shall be destroyed, but not in anger, or in battle.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And there will rise on his base the caster-out of the oppressor, the glory of kingdom [(the Hasmonean dynasty)]. But in a short time it will be broken — and not by the wrath [of another nation], and not by war, [(but by the contention of the brothers Aristobulus and Hyrcanus for the succession)].


###### Daniel 11:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:21)


וְעָמַ֤ד עַל־כַּנּוֹ֙ נִבְזֶ֔ה וְלֹא־נָתְנ֥וּ עָלָ֖יו ה֣וֹד מַלְכ֑וּת וּבָ֣א בְשַׁלְוָ֔ה וְהֶחֱזִ֥יק מַלְכ֖וּת בַּחֲלַקְלַקּֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And in his place shall stand up a vile person, to whom the honour of the kingdom has not been given: but he shall come in without difficulty, and obtain the kingdom by flatteries.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And there will rise on his place the despised one [(Esav [Rome, viz. Ovadiah 1:2])], upon whom the majesty of kingdom was not conferred. But he will come quietly and hold the kingdom with subtlety.


###### Daniel 11:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:22)


וּזְרֹע֥וֹת הַשֶּׁ֛טֶף יִשָּׁטְפ֥וּ מִלְּפָנָ֖יו וְיִשָּׁבֵ֑רוּ וְגַ֖ם נְגִ֥יד בְּרִֽית׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the force of the flood shall be swept away before him, and shall be broken; even the prince of the covenant.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And those who swept [all before them] by force of arms [i.e., the Greeks] will be swept away before him, as will the maker of the covenant [i.e., the king of Israel who made a covenant with the Romans, which they broke].


###### Daniel 11:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:23)


וּמִן־הִֽתְחַבְּר֥וּת אֵלָ֖יו יַעֲשֶׂ֣ה מִרְמָ֑ה וְעָלָ֥ה וְעָצַ֖ם בִּמְעַט־גּֽוֹי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And after the league made with him he shall work deceitfully: for he shall come up, and shall become strong with a small people.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And from this alliance with him [(the king of Israel)] he will weave deceit [(concealing evil designs)], and he will come up [from his place] and with scant forces overcome [(his enemies around the land of Judah — Edom, Ammon, and Moav — the king of Judah assisting him)].


###### Daniel 11:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:24)


בְּשַׁלְוָ֞ה וּבְמִשְׁמַנֵּ֣י מְדִינָה֮ יָבוֹא֒ וְעָשָׂ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר לֹא־עָשׂ֤וּ אֲבֹתָיו֙ וַאֲב֣וֹת אֲבֹתָ֔יו בִּזָּ֧ה וְשָׁלָ֛ל וּרְכ֖וּשׁ לָהֶ֣ם יִבְז֑וֹר וְעַ֧ל מִבְצָרִ֛ים יְחַשֵּׁ֥ב מַחְשְׁבֹתָ֖יו וְעַד־עֵֽת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He shall enter without difficulty even upon the fattest places of the province; and he shall do that which his fathers have not done, or his fathers’ fathers; he shall scatter among them prey, and spoil, and riches: and he shall devise schemes against the strongholds, but only for a time.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Quietly [(and deceitfully)] and into the fats of the land shall he come, and he will do what his fathers and his fathers' fathers had not done. And spoils and wealth shall he spread among them [(among all who would take his yoke upon them)]. And over fortresses will he plot his designs [(placing his commanders over them)] until the time [(when all is under his domination)].


###### Daniel 11:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:25)


וְיָעֵר֩ כֹּח֨וֹ וּלְבָב֜וֹ עַל־מֶ֣לֶךְ הַנֶּ֘גֶב֮ בְּחַ֣יִל גָּדוֹל֒ וּמֶ֣לֶךְ הַנֶּ֗גֶב יִתְגָּרֶה֙ לַמִּלְחָמָ֔ה בְּחַֽיִל־גָּד֥וֹל וְעָצ֖וּם עַד־מְאֹ֑ד וְלֹ֣א יַעֲמֹ֔ד כִּֽי־יַחְשְׁב֥וּ עָלָ֖יו מַחֲשָׁבֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall stir up his power and his courage against the king of the south with a great army; and the king of the south shall be stirred up to battle with a very great and mighty army; but he shall not stand: for they shall devise schemes against him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he will rouse up his strength and his heart against the king of the south with a great army. And the king of the south will wage war with a great and extremely powerful army, but he will not prevail. For they will have plotted designs against him [(by bribing his retainers)].


###### Daniel 11:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:26)


וְאֹכְלֵ֧י פַת־בָּג֛וֹ יִשְׁבְּר֖וּהוּ וְחֵיל֣וֹ יִשְׁט֑וֹף וְנָפְל֖וּ חֲלָלִ֥ים רַבִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For those who eat his food shall destroy him, and his army shall overflow: and many shall fall down slain.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the eaters of his pathbag [viz. 1:5] will break him, and he [(the king of the north)] will sweep away his army, and many slain will fall.


###### Daniel 11:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:27)


וּשְׁנֵיהֶ֤ם הַמְּלָכִים֙ לְבָבָ֣ם לְמֵרָ֔ע וְעַל־שֻׁלְחָ֥ן אֶחָ֖ד כָּזָ֣ב יְדַבֵּ֑רוּ וְלֹ֣א תִצְלָ֔ח כִּי־ע֥וֹד קֵ֖ץ לַמּוֹעֵֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And both these kings’ hearts shall be to do mischief, and they shall speak lies at one table; but it shall not prosper: for the end is yet to come at the time appointed.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the two kings [(the Roman king and the "maker of the covenant," Hyrcanus, who joined him in order to receive his aid against his brother Aristobulus, reigning in Jerusalem)] — their hearts will be [joined] to do evil [against your people, Judah]. And against one "table" [(the kingdom of Aristobulus)] will they speak deceit. But it [(their counsel)] will not prevail, for there is yet [time remaining] for the end [i.e., the destruction] appointed [for them (viz. 9:25)].


###### Daniel 11:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:28)


וְיָשֹׁ֤ב אַרְצוֹ֙ בִּרְכ֣וּשׁ גָּד֔וֹל וּלְבָב֖וֹ עַל־בְּרִ֣ית קֹ֑דֶשׁ וְעָשָׂ֖ה וְשָׁ֥ב לְאַרְצֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then shall he return into his land with great riches; and his heart shall be set against the holy covenant; and he shall work his will, and return to his own land.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he [(the king of Rome)] will return to his land [(from his campaign against the king of the south)] with great wealth. And his heart will [plot to break] the sacred covenant [with Israel]. And he will prevail and return to his land.


###### Daniel 11:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:29)


לַמּוֹעֵ֥ד יָשׁ֖וּב וּבָ֣א בַנֶּ֑גֶב וְלֹֽא־תִהְיֶ֥ה כָרִאשֹׁנָ֖ה וְכָאַחֲרוֹנָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
At the time appointed he shall return, and come towards the south; but it shall not be at the latter time as it was at the former.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
After some time, he will return and come to the south, but [his coming] will not be [as successful] as the first [viz. 11:7] or the last [28].


###### Daniel 11:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:30)


וּבָ֨אוּ ב֜וֹ צִיִּ֤ים כִּתִּים֙ וְנִכְאָ֔ה וְשָׁ֛ב וְזָעַ֥ם עַל־בְּרִֽית־ק֖וֹדֶשׁ וְעָשָׂ֑ה וְשָׁ֣ב וְיָבֵ֔ן עַל־עֹזְבֵ֖י בְּרִ֥ית קֹֽדֶשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For ships of Kittim shall come against him: therefore he shall lose heart and return, and have indignation against the holy covenant, and do his will; he shall even return, and have an understanding with those who forsake the holy covenant.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And there will come against him factions and troops [(of rebellious Romans)], and he will be weakened, and he will return [to his land] in wrath over the sacred covenant [(that he made with Israel, resolved to break it)]. And he will prevail and return and reflect upon the forsakers of the sacred covenant [(between themselves and the L-rd, and take heart therefrom that he will succeed in breaking his covenant with them.)]


###### Daniel 11:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:31)


וּזְרֹעִ֖ים מִמֶּ֣נּוּ יַעֲמֹ֑דוּ וְחִלְּל֞וּ הַמִּקְדָּ֤שׁ הַמָּעוֹז֙ וְהֵסִ֣ירוּ הַתָּמִ֔יד וְנָתְנ֖וּ הַשִּׁקּ֥וּץ מְשֹׁמֵֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And arms shall stand up on his part, and they shall profane sanctuary and fortress, and shall take away the daily sacrifice, and they shall set up the abomination that makes desolate.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And forces will arise from him [(He will send his chiefs and warriors to Jerusalem)], and they will desecrate the abode of the sanctuary. And they will abolish the continual [burnt-offering], and they will install "the mute abomination."


###### Daniel 11:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:32)


וּמַרְשִׁיעֵ֣י בְרִ֔ית יַחֲנִ֖יף בַּחֲלַקּ֑וֹת וְעַ֛ם יֹדְעֵ֥י אֱלֹהָ֖יו יַחֲזִ֥קוּ וְעָשֽׂוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And such as do wickedly against the covenant shall he seduce by flatteries: but the people who know their God shall be strong, and prevail.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the forsakers of the covenant [(with their fellow-Jews)] he will flatter with smoothness; but the people who know their G-d will persist [(in their belief)] and carry on.


###### Daniel 11:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:33)


וּמַשְׂכִּ֣ילֵי עָ֔ם יָבִ֖ינוּ לָֽרַבִּ֑ים וְנִכְשְׁל֞וּ בְּחֶ֧רֶב וּבְלֶהָבָ֛ה בִּשְׁבִ֥י וּבְבִזָּ֖ה יָמִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they who understand among the people shall instruct many: yet they shall fall by the sword, and by flame, by captivity, and by spoil, some days.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the enlighteners of the people [i.e., the Torah sages] will propound (it) to the many, and they will "stumble" [(in their exile)] by sword, flame, captivity, and plunder for [many] days.


###### Daniel 11:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:34)


וּבְהִכָּ֣שְׁלָ֔ם יֵעָזְר֖וּ עֵ֣זֶר מְעָ֑ט וְנִלְו֧וּ עֲלֵיהֶ֛ם רַבִּ֖ים בַּחֲלַקְלַקּֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now when they shall fall, they shall receive a little help: but many shall join themselves to them with flatteries.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And in their stumbling, they will be assisted somewhat [(by bribing the foe)], but many will join them because of their "smoothnesses."


###### Daniel 11:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:35)


וּמִן־הַמַּשְׂכִּילִ֣ים יִכָּֽשְׁל֗וּ לִצְר֥וֹף בָּהֶ֛ם וּלְבָרֵ֥ר וְלַלְבֵּ֖ן עַד־עֵ֣ת קֵ֑ץ כִּי־ע֖וֹד לַמּוֹעֵֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And some of them of understanding shall fall, to try them, and to refine, and to make them white, even to the time of the end: because it is yet for the time appointed.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And [some] of the enlightened ones will stumble [in attempting] to decipher them [(the times given for the redemption)] and to clarify and elucidate [them], until the time of the end [of the exile]; for [much time] yet remains until the appointed time.


###### Daniel 11:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:36)


וְעָשָׂ֨ה כִרְצֹנ֜וֹ הַמֶּ֗לֶךְ וְיִתְרוֹמֵ֤ם וְיִתְגַּדֵּל֙ עַל־כׇּל־אֵ֔ל וְעַל֙ אֵ֣ל אֵלִ֔ים יְדַבֵּ֖ר נִפְלָא֑וֹת וְהִצְלִ֙יחַ֙ עַד־כָּ֣לָה זַ֔עַם כִּ֥י נֶחֱרָצָ֖ה נֶעֱשָֽׂתָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the king shall do according to his will; and he shall exalt himself, and magnify himself above every god, and shall speak astonishing things against the God of gods, and shall prosper till the indignation be accomplished: for that which is determined shall be done.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king [of Rome] will do as he wills, and he will be exalted and aggrandized over all the mighty ones. And against the Mighty of the mighty he will "speak wonders" [i.e., he will spout blasphemies.] And he will succeed until the wrath [of the L-rd against Israel] ends, when His decree has been executed.


###### Daniel 11:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:37)


וְעַל־אֱלֹהֵ֤י אֲבֹתָיו֙ לֹ֣א יָבִ֔ין וְעַל־חֶמְדַּ֥ת נָשִׁ֛ים וְעַֽל־כׇּל־אֱל֖וֹהַּ לֹ֣א יָבִ֑ין כִּ֥י עַל־כֹּ֖ל יִתְגַּדָּֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Nor shall he regard the gods of his fathers, nor the desire of women, nor regard any god: for he shall magnify himself above all.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And to the G-d of his [(Israel's)] fathers [(Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob)] he [(the king of Rome)] will not pay heed. And to "the beauteous among the women" [(the congregation of Israel)] and to any god he will not pay heed, for he will exalt himself above all.


###### Daniel 11:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:38)


וְלֶאֱלֹ֙הַּ֙ מָֽעֻזִּ֔ים עַל־כַּנּ֖וֹ יְכַבֵּ֑ד וְלֶאֱל֜וֹהַּ אֲשֶׁ֧ר לֹא־יְדָעֻ֣הוּ אֲבֹתָ֗יו יְכַבֵּ֛ד בְּזָהָ֥ב וּבְכֶ֛סֶף וּבְאֶ֥בֶן יְקָרָ֖ה וּבַחֲמֻדֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But in his place he shall honour the god of strongholds, and a god whom his fathers did not know shall he honour with gold, and silver, and with precious stones, and costly things.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the god of the stronghold [(cities which make a covenant with him)] he will honor [while it stands] on its base, [(flattering those who subject themselves to him)]. And a god that his fathers did not know he will honor with gold and silver and precious stones and beautiful objects.


###### Daniel 11:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:39)


וְעָשָׂ֞ה לְמִבְצְרֵ֤י מָֽעֻזִּים֙ עִם־אֱל֣וֹהַּ נֵכָ֔ר אֲשֶׁ֥ר (הכיר) [יַכִּ֖יר] יַרְבֶּ֣ה כָב֑וֹד וְהִמְשִׁילָם֙ בָּֽרַבִּ֔ים וַאֲדָמָ֖ה יְחַלֵּ֥ק בִּמְחִֽיר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall deal with the strongest fortresses with the help of a foreign god; those whom he acknowledges, he will magnify with honour: and he shall make them rulers over many, and shall divide the land for a price.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he will make [buildings] for stronghold cities with [i.e., in honor of] foreign gods. [And to those] chiefs whom he recognizes [and would flatter], he will accord much honor, and he will give them dominion over the many and will apportion land [to them] at a [token] price.


###### Daniel 11:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:40)


וּבְעֵ֣ת קֵ֗ץ יִתְנַגַּ֤ח עִמּוֹ֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ הַנֶּ֔גֶב וְיִשְׂתָּעֵ֨ר עָלָ֜יו מֶ֣לֶךְ הַצָּפ֗וֹן בְּרֶ֙כֶב֙ וּבְפָ֣רָשִׁ֔ים וּבׇאֳנִיּ֖וֹת רַבּ֑וֹת וּבָ֥א בַאֲרָצ֖וֹת וְשָׁטַ֥ף וְעָבָֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And at the time of the end shall the king of the south push at him: and the king of the north shall come against him like a storm, with chariots, and with horsemen, and with many ships; and he shall enter into the countries, and shall overflow and pass over.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And towards the end [(when our redemption draws near)], the king of the south will battle with him, and the king of the north will assault him with chariots and cavalry and many ships; and he will come into [his] lands and sweep [all before him] and pass on.


###### Daniel 11:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:41)


וּבָא֙ בְּאֶ֣רֶץ הַצְּבִ֔י וְרַבּ֖וֹת יִכָּשֵׁ֑לוּ וְאֵ֙לֶּה֙ יִמָּלְט֣וּ מִיָּד֔וֹ אֱד֣וֹם וּמוֹאָ֔ב וְרֵאשִׁ֖ית בְּנֵ֥י עַמּֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He shall enter also into the land of beauty, and many countries shall be overthrown: but these shall escape out of his hand – Edom, and Mo᾽av, and the chief of the children of ῾Ammon.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he will come into Eretz Hatzvi [(Eretz Yisrael)], and many will stumble [(and be lost)]; and these will escape him: Edom and Moav and the first [cities] of the children of Ammon.


###### Daniel 11:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:42)


וְיִשְׁלַ֥ח יָד֖וֹ בַּאֲרָצ֑וֹת וְאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרַ֔יִם לֹ֥א תִהְיֶ֖ה לִפְלֵיטָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He shall stretch forth his hand also upon the countries: and the land of Miżrayim shall not escape.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he will send his hand against [many] lands, and the land of Egypt will not escape.


###### Daniel 11:43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:43)


וּמָשַׁ֗ל בְּמִכְמַנֵּי֙ הַזָּהָ֣ב וְהַכֶּ֔סֶף וּבְכֹ֖ל חֲמֻד֣וֹת מִצְרָ֑יִם וְלֻבִ֥ים וְכֻשִׁ֖ים בְּמִצְעָדָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But he shall have power over the treasures of gold and of silver, and over all the precious things of Miżrayim: and the Lubbians and the Kushites shall follow in his train.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he will command the caches of gold and silver and all the precious things of Egypt, and he will tread Lubites and Cushites underfoot.


###### Daniel 11:44

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:44](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:44)


וּשְׁמֻע֣וֹת יְבַהֲלֻ֔הוּ מִמִּזְרָ֖ח וּמִצָּפ֑וֹן וְיָצָא֙ בְּחֵמָ֣א גְדֹלָ֔ה לְהַשְׁמִ֥יד וּֽלְהַחֲרִ֖ים רַבִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But tidings out of the east and out of the north shall affright him: therefore he shall go forth with great fury to destroy, and utterly to do away with many.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And reports [of uprisings] will confound him from the east and from the north, and he will leave in great wrath to destroy and to lay waste many.


###### Daniel 11:45

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:45](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/11:45)


וְיִטַּע֙ אׇהֳלֵ֣י אַפַּדְנ֔וֹ בֵּ֥ין יַמִּ֖ים לְהַר־צְבִי־קֹ֑דֶשׁ וּבָא֙ עַד־קִצּ֔וֹ וְאֵ֥ין עוֹזֵ֖ר לֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall plant the tents of his palace between the seas and the holy mountain of beauty; yet he shall come to his end, and none shall help him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he will pitch the tents of his pavilions between the seas and the mountain of the holy land, and he will come to his end with none to help him.



## Chapter 12

###### Daniel 12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/12:1)


וּבָעֵ֣ת הַהִיא֩ יַעֲמֹ֨ד מִֽיכָאֵ֜ל הַשַּׂ֣ר הַגָּד֗וֹל הָעֹמֵד֮ עַל־בְּנֵ֣י עַמֶּ֒ךָ֒ וְהָיְתָה֙ עֵ֣ת צָרָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֤ר לֹֽא־נִהְיְתָה֙ מִֽהְי֣וֹת גּ֔וֹי עַ֖ד הָעֵ֣ת הַהִ֑יא וּבָעֵ֤ת הַהִיא֙ יִמָּלֵ֣ט עַמְּךָ֔ כׇּל־הַנִּמְצָ֖א כָּת֥וּב בַּסֵּֽפֶר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And at that time shall Mikha᾽el stand up, the great chief angel, who stands for the children of thy people: and there shall be a time of trouble, such as never was since there was a nation till that same time: and at that time thy people shall be delivered, every one who shall be found written in the book.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And at that time Michael, the great prince who stands [up] for the children of Your people, will stand [mute, seeing the Holy One Blessed be He "contending" with Himself, saying: "How shall I destroy such a great nation for the sake of Israel?"] And there will be a time of turmoil [(in the heavenly synod and denunciation of Torah scholars)], the likes of which had never been, from [the time Israel] had become a nation until that time. And at that time your people will be delivered, according to all that is written in this book [viz. 7:11, 18].


###### Daniel 12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/12:2)


וְרַבִּ֕ים מִיְּשֵׁנֵ֥י אַדְמַת־עָפָ֖ר יָקִ֑יצוּ אֵ֚לֶּה לְחַיֵּ֣י עוֹלָ֔ם וְאֵ֥לֶּה לַחֲרָפ֖וֹת לְדִרְא֥וֹן עוֹלָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And many of those who sleep in the dust of the earth shall awake, some to everlasting life, and some to shame and everlasting contempt.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And many of those who sleep in the earth will wake [(at the resurrection)] — these for eternal life and these for shamings and eternal mortification.


###### Daniel 12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/12:3)


וְהַ֨מַּשְׂכִּלִ֔ים יַזְהִ֖רוּ כְּזֹ֣הַר הָרָקִ֑יעַ וּמַצְדִּיקֵי֙ הָֽרַבִּ֔ים כַּכּוֹכָבִ֖ים לְעוֹלָ֥ם וָעֶֽד׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they who are wise shall shine like the brightness of the firmament; and they who turn many to righteousness like the stars for ever and ever.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the enlightened [(who occupied themselves with Torah and mitzvoth)] will shine like the splendor of the firmament, and the turners of the many to righteousness [will shine] like the stars for all eternity.


###### Daniel 12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/12:4)


וְאַתָּ֣ה דָֽנִיֵּ֗אל סְתֹ֧ם הַדְּבָרִ֛ים וַחֲתֹ֥ם הַסֵּ֖פֶר עַד־עֵ֣ת קֵ֑ץ יְשֹׁטְט֥וּ רַבִּ֖ים וְתִרְבֶּ֥ה הַדָּֽעַת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But thou, O Daniyyel, shut up the words, and seal the book, until the time of the end: many shall run to and fro, and knowledge shall be increased.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you, Daniel, conceal [i.e., shroud] the words and seal the book until the time of the end [i.e., towards the advent of the redemption], when many will aspire [to unravel the secrets of the time of the redemption] and knowledge will increase.


###### Daniel 12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/12:5)


וְרָאִ֙יתִי֙ אֲנִ֣י דָנִיֵּ֔אל וְהִנֵּ֛ה שְׁנַ֥יִם אֲחֵרִ֖ים עֹמְדִ֑ים אֶחָ֥ד הֵ֙נָּה֙ לִשְׂפַ֣ת הַיְאֹ֔ר וְאֶחָ֥ד הֵ֖נָּה לִשְׂפַ֥ת הַיְאֹֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then I Daniyyel looked, and, behold, there stood two others, the one on this side of the bank of the River, and the other on that side of the bank of the River.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I saw — I, Daniel — and, behold, two other [angels] standing; one on one bank of the river [(Chidekel)], one on the other.


###### Daniel 12:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/12:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/12:6)


וַיֹּ֗אמֶר לָאִישׁ֙ לְב֣וּשׁ הַבַּדִּ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר מִמַּ֖עַל לְמֵימֵ֣י הַיְאֹ֑ר עַד־מָתַ֖י קֵ֥ץ הַפְּלָאֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And one said to the man clothed in linen, who was above the waters of the River, How long shall it be to the end of the wonders?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he [(one of them)] said to the man clothed in linen [(the angel who spoke with me)], who [stood] above the waters of the river: Until when will the hidden end delay [(in coming)]?


###### Daniel 12:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/12:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/12:7)


וָאֶשְׁמַ֞ע אֶת־הָאִ֣ישׁ**׀** לְב֣וּשׁ הַבַּדִּ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר מִמַּ֘עַל֮ לְמֵימֵ֣י הַיְאֹר֒ וַיָּ֨רֶם יְמִינ֤וֹ וּשְׂמֹאלוֹ֙ אֶל־הַשָּׁמַ֔יִם וַיִּשָּׁבַ֖ע בְּחֵ֣י הָעוֹלָ֑ם כִּי֩ לְמוֹעֵ֨ד מֽוֹעֲדִ֜ים וָחֵ֗צִי וּכְכַלּ֛וֹת נַפֵּ֥ץ יַד־עַם־קֹ֖דֶשׁ תִּכְלֶ֥ינָה כׇל־אֵֽלֶּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I heard the man clothed in linen, who was above the waters of the River, when he lifted up his right hand and his left hand to heaven, and swore by the One who lives for ever that it shall be for a time, times, and a half; and when the crushing of the power of the holy people shall have been completed, all these things shall be finished.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I heard the man clothed in linen above the waters of the river. And he lifted his right hand and his left hand heavenwards, and he swore by the Life of the World that at a time of two times and half a time, and at the end of the quashing of the strength of the holy people [viz. Deuteronomy 32:36], all of these shall come to an end.


###### Daniel 12:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/12:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/12:8)


וַאֲנִ֥י שָׁמַ֖עְתִּי וְלֹ֣א אָבִ֑ין וָאֹ֣מְרָ֔ה אֲדֹנִ֕י מָ֥ה אַחֲרִ֖ית אֵֽלֶּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I heard, but I did not understand: then I said, O my Lord, what shall be the end of these things?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I heard, but I did not understand. And I said: My master, what shall be the end of these [computations]?


###### Daniel 12:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/12:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/12:9)


וַיֹּ֖אמֶר לֵ֣ךְ דָּנִיֵּ֑אל כִּֽי־סְתֻמִ֧ים וַחֲתֻמִ֛ים הַדְּבָרִ֖ים עַד־עֵ֥ת קֵֽץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he said, Go thy way, Daniyyel: for the words are closed up and sealed till the time of the end.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he said: Go, Daniel, for the things are concealed and sealed until the time of the end [i.e., towards the advent of the redemption].


###### Daniel 12:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/12:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/12:10)


יִ֠תְבָּֽרְר֠וּ וְיִֽתְלַבְּנ֤וּ וְיִצָּֽרְפוּ֙ רַבִּ֔ים וְהִרְשִׁ֣יעוּ רְשָׁעִ֔ים וְלֹ֥א יָבִ֖ינוּ כׇּל־רְשָׁעִ֑ים וְהַמַּשְׂכִּלִ֖ים יָבִֽינוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Many shall purify themselves, and make themselves white, and be tried; but the wicked shall do wickedly: and none of the wicked shall understand; but the wise shall understand.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[These computations] will be clarified and elucidated and deciphered by many, but the wicked will distort [(them, and when their "times" have come and gone, they will deny the redemption)]. And all the wicked will not understand (them), but the enlightened [(the Torah sages)] will understand [(when the time arrives)].


###### Daniel 12:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/12:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/12:11)


וּמֵעֵת֙ הוּסַ֣ר הַתָּמִ֔יד וְלָתֵ֖ת שִׁקּ֣וּץ שֹׁמֵ֑ם יָמִ֕ים אֶ֖לֶף מָאתַ֥יִם וְתִשְׁעִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And from the time that the daily sacrifice shall be taken away, and the abomination that makes desolate be set up, there shall be a thousand two hundred and ninety days.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And from the time of the abolition of the continual [burnt-offering] and its displacement by "the mute abomination" [until its return in the days of the Messiah], one thousand and two hundred and ninety years [shall elapse].


###### Daniel 12:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/12:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/12:12)


אַשְׁרֵ֥י הַֽמְחַכֶּ֖ה וְיַגִּ֑יעַ לְיָמִ֕ים אֶ֕לֶף שְׁלֹ֥שׁ מֵא֖וֹת שְׁלֹשִׁ֥ים וַחֲמִשָּֽׁה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Happy is he who waits, and reaches a thousand three hundred and thirty five days.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Happy is he who waits [(forty-five years beyond this sum)] and attains to one thousand and three hundred and thirty-five years [(the Messiah being destined to be concealed for forty-five years after he is revealed.)]


###### Daniel 12:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/12:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Daniel/r/12:13)


וְאַתָּ֖ה לֵ֣ךְ לַקֵּ֑ץ וְתָנ֛וּחַ וְתַעֲמֹ֥ד לְגֹרָלְךָ֖ לְקֵ֥ץ הַיָּמִֽין׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But go thou thy way till the end be: for thou shalt rest, and stand up for thy allotted portion at the end of the days.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you, go to the end [i.e., to your grave]; and you shall rest and arise to your lot [with the righteous] at the end of days.
